### [1] [MV2: Multi-View Multi-Vehicle Driving Dataset for Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12442)
*Sanjay Bhargav Dharavath,Hanvitha Saraswathi Mukkamala,Faizan Farooq Khan,Ioannis Kakogeorgiou,Aditya Arun,C V Jawahar,Zakaria Laskar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MV2（多视角多车辆）数据集和基准，用于评估在动态城市场景中大幅视角变化下的新视角合成（NVS）方法。该数据集包含来自汽车、摩托车和无人机三台同步设备在不同但同步轨迹上的采集，训练在一个车辆的相机流上、在另一个车辆上测试，从而比现有单轨迹数据集提供更大的视角变化。共50个高质量场景、12000张图像，并通过SfM注册和人工像素级对应验证位姿。基准测试表明NVS性能随视角差异增大而下降，前馈位姿估计方法明显落后于优化方法，凸显了MV2作为驾驶场景NVS严格测试平台的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然可微渲染推动了新视角合成的发展，但将其应用于真实驾驶场景仍面临挑战，主要源于稀疏的采集视角、动态物体以及有限的多轨迹数据。现有数据集大多基于单条轨迹，视角变化不足，难以真实评估NVS方法在大视角差异下的鲁棒性，因此需要构建一个多轨迹、多视角的驾驶场景基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建MV2数据集：从汽车、摩托车和无人机三个载体上同步采集城市场景视频，三者在不同但同步的轨迹上行驶；通过结构从运动（SfM）对序列进行配准，并使用人工像素级对应标注验证相机位姿，最终生成50个高质量场景和12000张图像。评测方式为在一个车辆的相机流上训练NVS方法，在另一个车辆上进行测试，以衡量大视角变化下的合成性能。

Result: 对最新NVS方法和相机位姿估计方法进行基准测试后发现：NVS性能随视角差异增大而显著下降；前馈式位姿估计器在精度上明显落后于基于优化的方法。这些结果验证了MV2作为驾驶场景下NVS严格测试基准的有效性。

Conclusion: MV2填补了驾驶场景NVS评估中缺乏大视角变化基准的空白，为研究视角差异对合成质量的影响提供了严谨的测试平台。数据集、基准协议和相关资源已公开，可推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Differentiable rendering has advanced novel view synthesis (NVS), yet applying it to real-world driving remains difficult due to sparse capture viewpoints, dynamic objects, and limited multi-trajectory data. We introduce the Multi-View Multi-Vehicle (MV2) dataset and benchmark for evaluating NVS models under large viewpoint changes in dynamic urban scenes. MV2 features synchronized captures from a car, scooter, and drone, each following distinct yet synchronized trajectories. Training NVS methods on one vehicle's camera stream and testing on another enables evaluation under substantially larger viewpoint variations than existing single-trajectory datasets. All sequences are registered via Structure-from-Motion and camera poses verified using manual pixel-level correspondence annotations, yielding 50 high-quality scenes with 12000 images. Benchmarking recent NVS and camera pose estimation methods shows that NVS performance degrades with increasing viewpoint disparity, and that feed-forward pose estimators notably lag behind optimization-based approaches, highlighting MV2 as a rigorous testbed for NVS in driving. The dataset, benchmark protocol, and project resources are available at https://mv2-dataset.github.io/.

</details>


### [2] [HIMEC: Directional Change Representation and Fixed-Interface Decoding for Remote Sensing Image Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12502)
*Aysha Ashraf,Shaina Ashraf,Wafaa I. M. Hussin,Ali Haider,Zhi Lu,Zhenming Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HIMEC方法用于遥感图像变化描述（RSICC），核心创新是将方向性变化表示（DCR）与固定接口解码器结合，并系统研究了训练/推理状态下条件机制的一致性对解码性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数RSICC方法直接用融合视觉特征条件化解码器，忽视了中间变化结构的建模以及解码器接口一致性问题，导致特征表示与解码器交互方式欠优化。

Method: HIMEC包含三个主要组件：1）方向性变化表示（DCR），将符号化的差分特征分离为出现导向、消失导向和共享上下文三个流后进行融合；2）学习型查询编码器，将融合表示转化为仅有的、视觉条件化的变化查询令牌作为场景解码器的样本依赖记忆；3）训练专用的辅助短语解码器，提供基于描述文本的监督。场景解码器在训练和推理时均使用固定零输入以保持同一接口。此外，作者还评估了一种局部到场景级联方法，其在训练时基于教师强制的局部状态、推理时基于自回归状态进行条件化。

Result: 在变更的LEVIR-CC验证对上，训练/推理两种状态的均余弦距离为0.69。与状态匹配的条件方式可恢复大多数相关性能损失，而置换状态对应关系没有可检测的惩罚。在三种子均值的对比中，HIMEC在LEVIR-CC上达到CIDEr 142.81±0.60，而直接融合特征记忆为139.51±3.40。在SECOND-CC上，固定零输入和状态匹配的诊断条件分别达到75.67和76.99 CIDEr，而不匹配的级联为60.77。

Conclusion: HIMEC通过解耦变化表示和保持解码器接口一致性，在遥感图像变化描述任务上优于直接融合特征的基线方法。实验结果揭示了级联条件化中训练/推理状态不匹配的核心问题，但这一发现仅限于所评估的级联架构。源代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Remote sensing image change captioning (RSICC) converts bitemporal imagery into a sentence describing semantic changes. Most RSICC methods condition caption decoders directly on fused visual features, leaving intermediate change structure and decoder-interface consistency less studied. We present HIMEC, combining Directional Change Representation (DCR) with fixed-interface decoding. DCR separates signed differences into appearance-oriented, disappearance-oriented, and shared-context streams before fusion. A learned-query encoder converts the fused representation into visually conditioned change-query tokens that form the scene decoder's only sample-dependent memory. A training-only auxiliary phrase decoder supplies caption-derived supervision. With a fixed zero input, the scene decoder maintains the same interface during training and inference. Separately, we evaluate a local-to-scene cascade conditioned on teacher-forced local states during training and autoregressive states at inference. On changed LEVIR-CC validation pairs, these states have a mean cosine distance of 0.69. Regime-matched conditioning recovers most of the associated deficit, whereas permuting state correspondence causes no detectable penalty. These findings are limited to the evaluated cascade. In a matched three-seed comparison, HIMEC reaches a Consensus-based Image Description Evaluation (CIDEr) score of $142.81\pm0.60$ on LEVIR-CC, versus $139.51\pm3.40$ for direct fused-feature memory. On SECOND-CC, fixed-zero and regime-matched diagnostic conditioning reach 75.67 and 76.99 CIDEr, respectively, versus 60.77 for the mismatched cascade. The source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/ayshaashra/HIMEC upon publication.

</details>


### [3] [Can Vision-Language Models Assess Proxemic Risk from Egocentric Robot Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12515)
*Vladyslava Rudas,Dmytro Kuzmenko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文评估了三种开源VLM（InternVL、Qwen-VL、SmolVLM）在机器人自我中心视角图像上进行危险等级分类（四个级别）的能力，比较了三种提示策略和两轮QLoRA微调。结果表明未经微调时所有模型接近随机基线，微调仅带来适度提升；但Qwen-VL配合高级提示在高危案例召回率上显著优于其他模型。进一步分析发现正确的危险分类并不对应更好的空间定位能力，说明模型可能在不关注相关场景区域的情况下产生有用的安全标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从机器人第一人称视角评估近距离威胁（proxemic danger）对安全具身导航至关重要，这需要视觉和上下文推理的结合。现有VLM在此类细粒度安全推理任务上的能力尚未得到充分评估，研究者希望系统性地衡量当前开源VLM在此任务上的表现与局限。

Method: 选取三种开源视觉语言模型（InternVL、Qwen-VL、SmolVLM），在自我中心机器人图像的四级危险分类任务上进行评估。使用三种提示策略和两轮QLoRA微调，并与分层随机基线对比。同时进行了人物定位分析，用于检验危险分类与空间定位能力之间的关系。

Result: 未经微调的模型均接近基线水平；微调仅带来适度的整体提升。Qwen-VL结合高级提示在高危案例召回率上显著优于其他模型。人物定位分析显示正确的危险分类并不对应更好的空间定位，揭示了模型在安全标签生成与空间理解之间存在脱节。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在细粒度近距离推理和空间定位方面仍然受限，但针对性的提示和微调可以在选定模型中提升高危检测能力。这为后续改进VLM在机器人安全导航场景中的表现提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: Assessing proxemic danger from a robot's egocentric perspective is critical for safe embodied navigation in human environments and requires both visual and contextual reasoning. We evaluate three opensource vision-language models (VLMs) (\textit{InternVL}, \textit{Qwen-VL}, and \textit{SmolVLM}) on the classification of egocentric robot images into four danger levels, comparing three prompting strategies and two rounds of QLoRA fine-tuning against a stratified random baseline. Without fine-tuning, all models perform near the baseline, while fine-tuning yields only modest overall improvements. However, \textit{Qwen-VL} with an advanced prompt achieves substantially higher recall for high-danger cases than the other models. An analysis of person localization further shows that correct danger classification does not correspond to better spatial grounding, indicating that a model may produce a useful safety label without attending to the relevant region of the scene. These results show that current VLMs remain limited in fine-grained proxemic reasoning and spatial grounding, although targeted prompting and fine-tuning can improve high-danger detection in selected models.

</details>


### [4] [Surface-to-Skeleton 3D Cephalometry: Estimating Hidden Skeletal Landmarks from CT-Derived External Soft-Tissue Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12537)
*Tomoki Abe,Taiki Kanaya,Kazuki Saita,Mao Noda,Chie Tachiki,Yasushi Nishii,Hideo Saito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统地研究了从外部软组织几何推断CT定义的内部骨骼标志点的可行性，通过控制坐标一致性、配准、扫描域和采集状态等因素，在240个临床CT扫描上使用层次化点云模型实现了2.97mm的骨骼标志点平均径向误差，并在40个保留患者上验证了患者特异性信号的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D面部标志点方法仅关注可见皮肤上的点，而能否从外部软组织几何推断CT内部骨骼标志点尚不明确。该问题对骨骼标志点推断（如虚拟手术规划、解剖结构对齐）具有重要临床意义，但缺乏系统化的可行性验证。

Method: 使用同一采集的CT数据构建坐标一致的表面对骨骼任务，从240个双医院临床CT扫描中构建外部软组织点云与21个骨骼标志点及3个可见软组织标志点的配对数据集；采用集成层次化点云模型进行推理；通过患者错配对照和覆盖消融实验分析患者特异性信号和几何依赖性；并进行光学传送诊断评估可部署性。

Result: 在40个保留患者上，骨骼标志点平均径向误差为2.97mm，深部或表面不可见标志点为3.03mm。患者错配对照证明存在超越固定群体构型或全局相似性的患者特异性信号；覆盖消融表明模型依赖非前侧几何；光学传送诊断显示存在显著的覆盖相关和全局构型成分，但可部署的光学推理仍未解决。

Conclusion: 受控条件下的可行性问题得到肯定回答，该研究为隐藏骨骼标志点推断提供了基础，但光学传送场景下的实际部署能力仍有待进一步探索。

Abstract: Existing 3D facial-landmark methods localize points on visible skin, but whether CT-defined internal skeletal landmarks can be inferred from external soft-tissue geometry remains unclear. We formulate a coordinate-consistent surface-to-skeleton task using same-acquisition CT-derived surfaces, separating estimation from optical-to-CT registration, scanner-domain, and acquisition-state effects, with coverage analyzed separately. From 240 clinical CT scans from two hospitals, we construct a locked retrospective protocol pairing CT-derived external soft-tissue point clouds with 21 skeletal landmarks and three visible soft-tissue landmarks. An integrated hierarchical point-cloud model achieves 2.97 mm mean radial error on skeletal landmarks and 3.03 mm on deep or surface-invisible landmarks in 40 held-out patients. Patient-mismatch controls support patient-specific signal beyond a fixed population configuration or global similarity alone, while coverage ablations indicate dependence on non-anterior geometry. Optical-transfer diagnostics reveal substantial coverage-related and global-configuration components, although deployable optical inference remains unresolved. These results answer the controlled feasibility question affirmatively and provide a basis for hidden skeletal landmark inference.

</details>


### [5] [StrAD: A Streaming Method and Benchmark for Audio Description Generation for Long-form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12549)
*Julian Spravil,Sebastian Houben,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StrAD基准，用于长视频无障碍音频描述（AD）生成任务，将其重新定义为流式密集视频字幕生成，无需真实时间戳。提出的StrAD-FT模型在分段级任务上达到SOTA（CMD-AD上CIDEr 36.3），在完整视频流式任务上SODA得分2.4，是首个流式AD生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动制作音频描述成本高昂，导致覆盖范围有限；现有自动AD生成方法依赖真实时间戳和额外的角色数据库等上下文线索，且当前基准仅包含短片段与自动或任务不匹配的标注，无法支持全视频长篇幅AD生成。

Method: 将AD生成重新定义为流式密集视频字幕生成，采用滑动窗口处理全长视频，将AD插入现有转录文本中而不需要真实时间戳；支持微调模型（StrAD-FT）和视觉语言模型的零样本提示（StrAD-Zero）两种方式。同时构建覆盖多种类型（电影、纪录片、短片、表演、视频游戏）的长视频AD基准StrAD。

Result: 在分段级任务上，StrAD-FT在CMD-AD上达到36.3 CIDEr（比Shot-by-shot高+10.0），在StrAD上建立51.0 CIDEr的参考点，在MAD-Eval上为24.9 CIDEr；在全视频流式任务上，StrAD-FT的SODA为2.4，零样本基线StrAD-Zero为1.1，但两者在时间定位和叙事连贯性上仍有局限。

Conclusion: StrAD使全视频AD生成的可测量评估成为可能，是规模化为无障碍服务的前提条件；首个流式AD生成方法无需真实时间戳即可在线生成AD，尽管在时间定位和叙事连贯性方面仍存在挑战，但为后续工作奠定了可衡量的基准。

Abstract: Visual content is the dominant medium of communication, yet without audio descriptions (ADs), it remains inaccessible to blind and low-vision people. ADs narrate context-relevant visual events during natural audio pauses. Manually creating ADs is expensive, limiting coverage to a small fraction of available content. Most existing automatic AD generation methods frame the task as video clip captioning, requiring ground-truth timestamps and additional context cues such as character databases. Current benchmarks reinforce this framing, consisting of short video segments paired with automatic or task-mismatched annotations. We introduce StrAD, a benchmark for long-form AD generation on full-length videos spanning diverse genres such as movies, documentaries, short films, performances, and video games. We reformulate AD generation as streaming dense video captioning. Our approach processes full-length videos with a sliding window, inserting ADs into existing transcripts without ground-truth timestamps, and supports both fine-tuned models and zero-shot prompting of vision-language models. On the segment-level task with given timestamps, our fine-tuned StrAD-FT sets the state of the art on CMD-AD with 36.3 CIDEr (+10.0 over Shot-by-shot), establishes a reference point on StrAD (51.0 CIDEr), and remains competitive on MAD-Eval at 24.9 CIDEr. On the full-video streaming task, StrAD-FT reaches a SODA score of 2.4 against 1.1 for our zero-shot baseline StrAD-Zero, though both exhibit limitations in temporal localization and narrative coherence. While prior work has tackled full-video AD generation in an offline, multi-stage fashion, ours is the first streaming approach, generating ADs on the fly without ground-truth timestamps. StrAD makes progress on full-video AD generation measurable, a prerequisite for scaling accessibility.

</details>


### [6] [Attribute-Conditioned Multimodal Slot Factorization for Controllable Fashion Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12570)
*Najmeh Forouzandehmehr,Topojoy Biswas,Evren Korpeoglu,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-slotgate，一个多模态槽位编码器，将Fashion-CLIP的文本和图像嵌入分解为四个可命名的属性槽位（类别、颜色、图案、人口统计），每个槽位学习独立的图文门控机制。在H&M数据集上，该方法通过组合槽位相似度和槽位logit检索分数，macro ConstraintSatisfied@10达到0.7566，显著优于等权多模态融合（0.7142）和文本-only检索（0.4755），尤其在颜色属性上提升最大（从0.321到0.889，+0.568）。学习的门控可解释且无需模态监督，支持可控的槽位级干预（颜色提升15.3倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时尚检索需要同时满足类别、颜色、图案、人口统计等多个属性，但单一大规模嵌入（monolithic embedding）将这些信号混在一个向量中，难以在检索时进行属性级控制。已有的语义ID方法虽然提供离散物品编码，但通常作为物品级或残差地址优化，未暴露命名的、可独立控制的属性槽位，无法实现细粒度的属性条件化检索。

Method: MM-slotgate将Fashion-CLIP的文本和图像嵌入通过多模态槽位编码器分解为四个命名属性槽位。每个槽位学习自己的文本-图像门控（gate），使视觉可落地的属性（如颜色、图案）更多依赖图像证据，而基于分类法的属性（如类别、人口统计）保持文本驱动。检索时采用组合的槽位相似度和槽位logit分数进行加权融合。

Result: 在H&M数据集上，MM-slotgate达到0.7566的macro ConstraintSatisfied@10，优于等权多模态融合（0.7142）和fCLIP文本-only检索（0.4755）。颜色属性改进最大，从0.321提升至0.889（+0.568），归因于学习的颜色门控赋予图像证据57.4%的权重。门控无需模态监督即可解释：颜色偏向图像、类别偏向文本、图案和人口统计居中。线性探针显示无超出标签相关基线的泄漏，量化槽位码支持目标干预，颜色提升15.3倍。

Conclusion: 可控时尚检索受益于类型化的、属性条件化的多模态槽位，而非单一全局嵌入或不透明的物品级语义ID。按属性类型分配模态权重能够显著提升多属性约束下的检索性能，同时保持槽位的可解释性和可控性。

Abstract: Fashion retrieval often requires satisfying multiple attributes at once, such as category, color, pattern, and demographic. Monolithic embeddings mix these signals into a single vector, making attribute-specific control difficult at retrieval time. Many existing semantic-ID methods provide discrete item codes, but these codes are typically optimized as item-level or residual addresses and do not expose named, independently controllable attribute slots.
  We introduce MM-slotgate, a multimodal slot encoder that factorizes Fashion-CLIP text and image embeddings into four named attribute slots. Each slot learns its own text-image gate, so visually grounded attributes such as color and pattern can rely more on image evidence, while taxonomy-oriented attributes such as category and demographic can remain more text-driven.
  On H&M, using a combined slot-similarity and slot-logit retrieval score, MM-slotgate achieves 0.7566 macro ConstraintSatisfied@10, outperforming equal-weight multimodal fusion (0.7142) and fCLIP text-only retrieval (0.4755). The largest gain is on color, which improves from 0.321 to 0.889 (+0.568 absolute), as the learned color gate assigns 57.4% weight to image evidence. The learned gates are interpretable without modality supervision: color is image-leaning, category is text-leaning, and pattern and demographic lie near the middle.
  The resulting slots also remain controllable: linear probes show no measured excess leakage beyond the label-correlation baseline, and quantized slot codes support targeted intervention, including a 15.3x lift for color. These results suggest that controllable fashion retrieval benefits from typed, attribute-conditioned multimodal slots rather than either a single global embedding or opaque item-level semantic IDs.

</details>


### [7] [PseudoMapLabeler: Confidence-Aware Pseudo-Label Generation for Semi-Supervised Online Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12600)
*Chikao Tsuchiya,Dhaval Bhanderi,David Ilstrup,Hsinmin Cheng,Christopher Ostafew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于教师-学生半监督学习框架的在线高精地图构建方法，通过Beta分布置信图评估预测可靠性，并引入空间裁剪技术保留高置信区域生成高质量伪标签，在nuScenes数据集低标注条件下相比仅用标注数据训练提升了+6.1 mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线高精地图构建在实际部署中面临标注数据稀缺的挑战，有限的训练数据限制了模型在不同环境中的泛化能力。

Method: 采用教师-学生半监督学习框架：1) 在有限标注数据上训练教师模型；2) 利用Beta分布置信图评估时序观测中预测地图元素的可信度；3) 引入空间裁剪技术代替传统整体丢弃的方法，选择性保留高置信区域并移除不可靠片段；4) 精炼后的地图元素作为地图先验提升教师模型在无标注数据上的二次预测精度；5) 增强的预测作为伪标签从头训练学生模型，并在原始标注数据上微调。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，所提出的教师-学生框架在低标注条件下相比仅用标注数据训练提升了+6.1 mAP。

Conclusion: 该框架为在线高精地图构建中的标注数据稀缺问题提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: A critical challenge in deploying online HD map construction systems to real-world scenarios is the scarcity of labeled training data, which limits model generalization in diverse environments. To address this limitation, we propose a teacher-student semi-supervised learning (SSL) framework that generates high-quality pseudo-labels from unlabeled data through confidence-aware map refinement. Our approach first trains a teacher model on limited labeled data, then leverages Beta-distribution-based confidence maps to assess the reliability of predicted map elements across temporal observations. Unlike conventional filtering methods that discard entire elements, we introduce a spatial clipping technique that selectively preserves high-confidence regions while removing unreliable segments. The refined map elements serve as map priors that improve the teacher model's prediction accuracy on unlabeled data in a second pass. These enhanced predictions become pseudo-labels for training a student model from scratch, followed by fine-tuning on the original labeled data. Experimental results on the nuScenes dataset demonstrate that our teacher-student framework with refined pseudo-labels improves performance by +6.1 mAP under a low-label regime compared to training on labeled data alone, offering a practical solution to the labeled data scarcity problem in online HD map construction.

</details>


### [8] [From Visual Widgets to UI Code: Efficient Tool-Grounded Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12611)
*Houston H. Zhang,Tao Zhang,Li Gu,Linfeng Ye,Yuanhao Yu,Xinxin Zuo,Yang Wang,Zhixiang Chi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WidgetGen框架，这是一个轻量级工具接地框架，通过提取可观察的文本和颜色证据、进行高层布局及可选图表推理，直接生成可执行的JSX代码，在保真度与效率之间实现了优于直接提示和结构化管线的新平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的截图转代码系统在灵活性和可控性之间存在权衡：直接多模态生成容易产生幻觉（遗漏可见细节），而结构化管线虽然减少了错误，但引入了额外的生成编排成本，并将输出限制在预设表示所覆盖的设计范围内。作者希望探索选择性工具接地是否能改善直接widget到代码生成的保真度-效率权衡。

Method: WidgetGen是一个轻量级工具接地框架，包含三个核心步骤：(1) 提取可观察的文本和颜色证据；(2) 进行高层布局和可选图表推理；(3) 直接生成可执行的JavaScript XML (JSX)。该设计减少了对方组件生成的依赖，同时避免了固定的UI模式框架。研究在6个多模态模型和1000个held-out widgets上进行评估，并通过重建得到的图像-代码对进一步对Qwen系列6个开源模型进行监督微调。

Result: 在大多数视觉重建指标上，WidgetGen优于直接提示方法和结构化的Widget2Code管线，在面积、可读性和风格方面均有持续提升。此外，重建衍生的图像-代码对在监督微调后使6个Qwen系列开放权重模型在所有报告指标上均获得改进。

Conclusion: WidgetGen作为一个强大的轻量级基线，证明选择性证据接地是广泛表示约束的有效替代方案，能够在保持灵活性的同时提升截图转代码的保真度和效率。

Abstract: Existing screenshot-to-code systems face a trade-off between flexibility and controllability. Direct multimodal generation can hallucinate visible details, whereas structured pipelines reduce such errors through component-wise decomposition, predefined templates, and customized intermediate representations. These structures, however, introduce additional generative orchestration and restrict outputs to designs covered by the representation. We investigate whether selective tool grounding can improve the fidelity--efficiency trade-off of direct widget-to-code generation. We introduce \textbf{WidgetGen}, a lightweight tool-grounded framework that extracts observable text and color evidence, performs high-level layout and optional chart reasoning, and directly generates executable JavaScript XML (\emph{JSX}). This design reduces reliance on component-wise generation while avoiding a fixed UI schema. Across six multimodal models and \(1{,}000\) held-out widgets, WidgetGen outperforms direct prompting and the structured Widget2Code pipeline on most visual reconstruction metrics, with consistent gains in area, legibility, and style. Finally, reconstruction-derived image-code pairs improve six Qwen-family open-weight models across every reported metric through supervised fine-tuning. These results establish WidgetGen as a strong lightweight baseline and show that selective evidence grounding offers an effective alternative to extensive representation constraints.

</details>


### [9] [EgoCITE: Context-Augmented Indexing and Time-Aware Retrieval for Long-Horizon Egocentric Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12627)
*Le Zhang,Ke Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoCITE是一个面向长时程第一人称视频问答的代理记忆框架，通过上下文增强索引（EgoScheme）、多视角记忆索引（EgoIndex）和时间感知检索（EgoRetrv）三个组件，解决了现有系统中的两大瓶颈：上下文贫乏的标题索引不可靠以及检索忽略问题的时间意图。在EgoLifeQA、EgoMem和EgoR1-Bench基准上，EgoCITE相比代理记忆基线准确率提升4.4%-14.2%，同时成本比长上下文LLM代理低36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程自我中心记忆系统存在两大瓶颈：一是基于缺乏上下文的标题构建的索引在代理搜索中不可靠；二是检索过程忽略了问题的时序意图，导致无法准确对齐目标事件。

Method: EgoCITE包含三个核心组件：EgoScheme利用局部多模态上下文将碎片化的视频标题和语音转录转化为自包含的原子记忆索引；EgoIndex将互补的动作、活动、话语和对话表征组织成多粒度、可搜索的多视角记忆索引；EgoRetrv结合语义搜索与基于问题条件的时序相关性评分，并对检索到的证据进行筛选整理。

Result: 在EgoLifeQA、EgoMem和EgoR1-Bench三个基准上，EgoCITE相比代理记忆基线在答案准确率和目标事件检索对齐方面均取得提升，准确率提升4.4%-14.2%，且推理成本比长上下文LLM代理低36倍。

Conclusion: EgoCITE证明了上下文增强索引与时间感知检索相结合的有效性，在显著降低计算成本的同时提升长时程自我中心QA的性能，为代理记忆系统提供了可行的设计方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon egocentric memory transforms continuous first-person video and audio into a searchable record of past experiences. We demonstrate two bottlenecks in existing systems: indices built from context-poor captions are unreliable for agentic search, while retrieval ignores a question's temporal intent. To address both bottlenecks, we introduce EgoCITE (Egocentric Context-augmented Indexing and Time-aware Evidence retrieval), a long-horizon agentic memory framework for egocentric QA. EgoCITE comprises three components. EgoScheme uses local multimodal context to turn fragmentary video captions and speech transcripts into self-contained atomic memory indices. EgoIndex organizes complementary action, activity, utterance, and conversation representations into searchable multi-view memory indices at multiple granularities. EgoRetrv combines semantic search with question-conditioned temporal relevance scoring and curation of retrieved evidence. We evaluate EgoCITE on EgoLifeQA, EgoMem, and EgoR1-Bench in terms of answer accuracy and target-event retrieval alignment. EgoCITE improves accuracy over agentic memory baselines by at least 4.4--14.2\% while achieving 36$\times$ lower cost than long-context LLM agents.

</details>


### [10] [Inference-Time Orthogonal Seeding Enables Geometry-Aligned 3D Organ Segmentation for Slice-Propagation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12658)
*Md Rakibul Haque,Tushar Kataria,Shireen Y. Elhabian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究3D医学图像分割中单切片传播标注方法，发现训练范式影响有限，而推理阶段的种子几何配置（正交性）才是性能提升的关键驱动因素；采用三个正交种子（轴向、冠状、矢状）融合传播标注，在改进Dice、Normalized Surface Dice和Average Hausdorff Distance等指标上显著优于单轴基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体素级密集标注是3D医学图像分割的主要瓶颈。现有单切片传播方法（如Sli2Vol）仅使用轴向切片传播标注，忽略了冠状和矢状方向的证据，导致远离种子区域时误差累积，尤其在表面距离指标上表现不佳，未能充分利用CT/MRI体积中的三维信息。

Method: 系统研究影响切片传播模型的关键训练与推理选择，包括单轴vs多轴无标签配准、单种子vs多种子传播、正交种子配置等。核心方法是从单一轴向种子改为三个正交种子（轴向、冠状、矢状各一个），并用简单的无标签规则融合传播后的标注。

Result: 在多种器官CT队列上，使用正交种子配合轴向Sli2Vol骨干网络，Dice提高21.9%，Normalized Surface Dice提高25.5%，Average Hausdorff Distance降低53.5%，优于单轴基线。

Conclusion: 训练范式对切片传播模型影响有限：轴向训练的网络用于离轴种子时即可获得绝大部分改进，显式三轴训练增益甚微。性能主要由推理阶段的种子几何配置驱动，尤其是种子的正交性而非注释切片数量；预算匹配的三轴对照无增益甚至可能降低性能。

Abstract: Dense voxel-level annotation remains a major bottleneck in 3D medical image segmentation. Single-slice propagation methods such as Sli2Vol reduce this burden by propagating one annotated seed slice through a volume using label-free registration. However, axial-only propagation accumulates errors with distance from the seed, especially in surface-distance metrics, because it ignores coronal and sagittal evidence and therefore underuses the 3D information available in CT/MRI volumes. To better leverage volumetric geometry, we study how key training and inference choices affect slice-propagation models, including single-axis versus multi-axis label-free registration, single-seed versus multi-seed propagation, and orthogonal seed configurations. Instead of propagating from a single axial seed, we use three orthogonal seeds---one axial, one coronal, and one sagittal---and fuse their propagated labels with a simple label-free rule. Our results show that the training paradigm has limited impact: an axially trained network applied to off-axis seeds captures nearly all the improvement, while explicit three-axis training adds little. Instead, performance is driven by inference-time seed geometry, especially orthogonality rather than the number of annotated slices, as a budget-matched three-axial control provides no benefit and can even degrade performance. On a multi-organ CT cohort, orthogonal seeding with the axial Sli2Vol backbone improves Dice by 21.9%, Normalized Surface Dice by 25.5%, and reduces Average Hausdorff Distance by 53.5% over the single-axis baseline.

</details>


### [11] [Mr3D-VL: A generalist vision language foundation model for Multiparametric 3D Magnetic Resonance Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12689)
*Zhi Qiao,Xintong Wu,Yichu He,Feng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Mr3D-VL，一个40亿参数的面向多参数3D MRI的视觉-语言基础模型，通过无监督预训练的共享3D编码器和4D旋转位置嵌入实现双模态-空间集成，采用多分辨率特征植入策略的跨模态投影层，在报告生成（BERTScore 0.856）、问答（0.713）和多选题（0.912）任务上超越现有4B/7B/30B领域专用及通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前脑肿瘤mpMRI的AI模型缺乏自然语言交互和可解释性，无法实现空间信息整合和跨模态推理；现有VLM主要聚焦2D建模，缺乏3D体积空间的直接感知；而已有的3D VLM仅针对CT，无法满足mpMRI多模态协同推理的临床需求。

Method: Mr3D-VL采用40亿参数规模，核心包括：(1) 无监督预训练的共享3D编码器；(2) 4D旋转位置嵌入实现模态与空间的双重集成；(3) 跨模态投影层采用多分辨率特征植入策略，增强跨分辨率特征感知能力。

Result: 在文本生成任务上显著优于现有4B/7B/30B的领域专用和通用模型，报告生成BERTScore达0.856，问答准确率0.713，多选题准确率0.912。

Conclusion: Mr3D-VL验证了3D视觉-语言基础模型在多参数MRI中的应用潜力，通过多模态空间协同建模和跨分辨率特征融合有效解决了现有模型的局限性。

Abstract: Multi-parametric magnetic resonance imaging (mpMRI) is a cornerstone for brain tumor diagnosis and treatment, yet current AI models face critical limitations: their lack of natural language interaction and interpretability impedes spatial information integration and cross-modal reasoning required clinically. Key challenges arise from significant physical meaning differences across modalities, spatial misalignment due to scan intervals, and the need for complex multi-feature interpretation in tasks like glioma grading. While visual-language models (VLMs) show promise in cross-modal understanding, existing methods focus mainly on 2D image modeling, neglecting direct perception of 3D volumetric space. Although 3D VLMs have been proposed for report generation and feature alignment in 3D CT imaging, mpMRI applications demand collaborative inference across multiple imaging modalities-a requirement unmet by current solutions. To address this, we introduce Mr3D-VL, a dedicated visual-language foundation model for multi-parametric 3D MRI. With 4 billion parameters, it employs an unsupervised pre-trained shared 3D encoder and 4D rotational positional embedding for dual modality-spatial integration. Its cross-modal projection layer uses a multi-resolution feature implantation strategy to enhance feature perception across resolutions. Experimental results show significant improvements over existing 4B/7B/30B domain-specific and general-purpose models in text generation tasks, achieving a BERTScore of 0.856 for report generation, with question-answering accuracy at 0.713 and multiple-choice accuracy at 0.912.

</details>


### [12] [Class Geometry as Supervision for Sample-Efficient Open-World Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12698)
*Akash Rao,Zhou Chen,Revanth Reddy Palem,Udhav Ramachandran,Ruth Scimeca,Sathyanarayanan N. Aakur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出类几何监督（CGS）框架，通过在原型/类表示空间中约束保持类别间视觉或语义的相似性/差异性结构，提升开放世界目标检测在少样本、开放集和新增类别场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界目标检测需要识别已知类别、拒绝未知对象并逐步吸收新类，尤其在生物医学和科学影像等稀缺数据场景中挑战严峻。现有的基于原型的检测器通常将类别原型作为独立锚点学习，忽略了类别间的结构化关系，导致在少样本和开放集场景下表征能力不足。

Method: 提出类几何监督（CGS）框架，该框架通过学习到的类别表示上施加不相似性保持目标函数，使类别表示间的成对距离与从训练数据估计的目标类几何矩阵对齐，同时保留标准任务损失。该目标函数可统一应用于原型识别、少样本生物医学目标检测、开放集检测、新类插入及COCO上的开放世界检测自适应等任务。

Result: 实验表明CGS在识别和开放词汇检测中提升了样本效率，显著增强了新类插入能力，并在COCO上提升了未知类召回率，同时保持大部分已知类检测性能。消融实验显示有意义的视觉几何提供最可靠的增益，而随机几何虽有助于新类分离但在少样本检测中一致性较差。

Conclusion: 关系类几何是一种有效的监督信号，可用于在有限监督条件下构建校准且可扩展的开放世界检测器，为稀缺数据场景下的开放世界目标检测提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Open-world object detection requires models to recognize known categories, reject unfamiliar objects, and incorporate new classes over time. This is especially challenging in scarce-data settings such as biomedical and scientific imaging, where rare categories may have only a few annotated examples and fine-grained classes differ by subtle morphology. Prototype-based detectors are natural for this regime, but they typically learn class prototypes as independent anchors, ignoring relational structure among classes. We propose class-geometry supervision (CGS), a general framework that constrains learned prototype or class-representation spaces to preserve visual or semantic class dissimilarities estimated from training data. CGS introduces a dissimilarity-preserving objective that aligns pairwise distances among learned class representations with a target class-geometry matrix while retaining the standard task loss. We instantiate the same objective across prototype recognition, few-shot biomedical object detection, open-set detection, novel-class insertion, and OWOD adaptation on COCO. Experiments show that CGS improves sample efficiency in recognition and ova detection, substantially strengthens novel-class insertion, and improves unknown recall on COCO while retaining much of the known-class detection performance. Ablations show that meaningful visual geometry provides the most reliable gains, while random geometry can help novel separation but is less consistent for few-shot detection. These results suggest that relational class geometry is an effective supervisory signal for building calibrated and extensible open-world detectors under limited supervision.

</details>


### [13] [Towards Sparsely Annotated Open-World Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12714)
*HeeJu Han,AJeong Kim,Jinsun Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏标注开放世界目标检测（SA-OWOD）这一新任务，并设计了统一框架DPOD，通过已知目标恢复模块（KTRM）和双不一致目标生成器（DDTG）协同处理稀疏监督与未知类别共存的挑战，在检测未知目标方面优于现有开放世界检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界目标检测面临模糊监督问题：未标注区域可能对应已知目标的缺失标注，也可能对应真正未知的类别。现有方法分别处理稀疏标注目标检测（SAOD）和开放世界目标检测（OWOD），但两者的共存问题尚未被联合解决。

Method: 提出DPOD统一框架，包含两个互补机制：已知目标恢复模块（KTRM）用于恢复未标注已知实例的监督信号并显式正则化特征空间以区分已知与未知表征；双不一致目标生成器（DDTG）通过跨视图语义不一致性识别可靠的未知候选目标。两模块集成以解决模糊未标注区域导致的矛盾监督信号。

Result: 在稀疏标注开放世界基准上的实验表明，所提方法优于现有开放世界检测方法，尤其在未知目标检测任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过整合KTRM和DDTG，DPOD有效防止已知与未知目标的误分类，稳定了决策边界，为SA-OWOD这一新任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world object detection operates under ambiguous supervision, where unlabeled regions may correspond to missing annotations of known objects or genuinely unknown categories. These challenges have been addressed separately in Sparsely Annotated Object Detection (SAOD) and Open-World Object Detection (OWOD). In practice, their co-occurrence remains an open problem. To address this problem, we introduce Sparsely Annotated Open-World Object Detection (SA-OWOD), a new task that jointly considers sparse supervision and the presence of unseen categories. We propose Dual-Perspective Object Discovery (DPOD), a unified framework that jointly models unlabeled known and unknown instances via two complementary mechanisms. The Known Target Recovery Module (KTRM) recovers supervision for unlabeled known instances and explicitly regularizes the feature space to separate known and unknown representations. Complementarily, the Dual-Disagreement Target Generator (DDTG) identifies reliable unknown candidates through cross-view semantic inconsistency. By integrating these modules, DPOD resolves contradictory supervision signals caused by ambiguous unlabeled regions. As a result, it prevents misclassification between known and unknown objects and stabilizes the decision boundaries. Experimental results on sparsely annotated open-world benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing open-world detection methods, particularly in detecting unknown objects.

</details>


### [14] [VOS-Agent: The 1st Place Solution for the 8th LSVOS Challenge (MOSEv2 Track)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12721)
*Canyang Wu,Jinrong Zhang,Xusheng He,Ce Bian,Xianjing Han,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VOS-Agent协作框架，保留SAM3作为共享分割模块，根据目标特征条件性激活专用代理（视觉追踪代理处理小目标，MLLM语义代理处理语义主导目标），在MOSEv2测试集上达到69.82% J&F，荣获ECCV 2026第8届LSVOS挑战赛MOSEv2赛道冠军。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂视频对象分割中，SAM3虽具有强大的可提示掩码传播能力，但统一的推理路径对两类目标不可靠：一是视觉证据不足的极小目标，二是身份依赖于显式属性的语义主导目标。现有方法缺乏根据目标特性自适应调整推理策略的机制。

Method: VOS-Agent采用协作式框架：保留SAM3作为共享的密集分割模块，同时引入目标感知与路由代理（Target Perception and Routing Agent）将每个序列路由到常规、小目标或语义主导三条路径之一。小目标路径由视觉追踪代理（Visual Tracking Agent）通过置信度感知的框提示提供支持；语义主导路径由基于MLLM的语义代理（Semantic Agent）通过描述引导定位和候选验证来处理。

Result: 在MOSEv2测试集上，VOS-Agent在官方J&F指标上达到69.82%，位列ECCV 2026第8届LSVOS挑战赛MOSEv2赛道第一名。

Conclusion: 通过条件性路由和专用代理的协同，VOS-Agent有效解决了小目标和语义主导目标在复杂视频分割中的鲁棒性问题，验证了面向目标特性的自适应推理框架在该任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Complex video object segmentation requires robust target propagation under severe occlusion, disappearance and reappearance. Although SAM3 provides strong promptable mask propagation, a uniform inference path remains unreliable for tiny targets with insufficient visual evidence and semantic-dominated targets whose identities depend on explicit attributes. To this end, we present VOS-Agent, a collaborative framework that retains SAM3 as the shared dense segmentation module and conditionally activates specialized agents according to target characteristics. A Target Perception and Routing Agent assigns each sequence to a regular, tiny, or semantic-dominated route. Tiny targets are supported by a Visual Tracking Agent through confidence-aware box prompts, while semantic-dominated targets are handled by an MLLM-based Semantic Agent through description-guided localization and candidate verification. On the MOSEv2 test set, VOS-Agent achieves 69.82% on the official $\mathcal{J}\&\dot{\mathcal{F}}$ metric and ranks first in the MOSEv2 Track of the 8th LSVOS Challenge at ECCV 2026.

</details>
