Motivation: 现代图像分类模型在单一任务特定数据集上表现优异，但跨领域和难度级别泛化能力不足。现有模型各自具有不同的能力与缺陷，缺乏一个能够动态选择最适合的模型进行推理的统一框架。

Method: ARMDIL是一个集成了异质视觉骨干网络（ResNet、SSL模型、VLM）的集成框架，使用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）作为智能体，动态地将每个输入图像路由到最合适的视觉骨干。所有骨干在由多个不同分布和特征的数据集构建的统一标签空间上进行训练，路由决策可通过自然语言推理痕迹进行解释，并通过简单提示修改整合新信息。

Result: 实验评估揭示了各架构在不同视觉领域上的独特能力与缺陷。ARMDIL能够有效权衡这些特性，达到与专门训练的路由器相当的性能，同时显著提升了适应性（通过提示词修改快速集成新信息）和可解释性（自然语言推理痕迹）。

Conclusion: ARMDIL为跨数据集图像分类提供了更可靠的通用视觉系统路径，其优势在于无需重新训练的快速适配能力和天然的可解释性，为AI助手和自主机器人等通用视觉系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Modern image classification models excel when trained on single task-specific datasets but often struggle to generalize across domains and difficulty levels. We propose ARMDIL, an Adaptive Router for Multi-Domain Image classification with LLMs. ARMDIL is an ensemble that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) agent to dynamically route each image to the most suitable vision backbone. Our diverse ensemble employs convolutional neural networks (ResNets), self-supervised representation learners (SSL), and vision-language models (VLMs), each trained on a unified label space constructed from multiple image datasets with differing distributions and characteristics. Empirical evaluations illuminate the distinct capabilities and vulnerabilities of each architecture across disparate visual domains. Crucially, we show that ARMDIL effectively navigates these trade-offs, performing competitively with specialized training-based routers. Furthermore, it drastically improves adaptability by allowing new information to be integrated via simple prompt modifications, while enhancing interpretability through natural language reasoning traces. These advances in cross-dataset image classification pave the way for more reliable general-purpose vision systems such as AI assistants and autonomous robots.

</details>


### [83] [MapRoute++: Surrogate-Guided Semantic Routing for Visual Concept Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13478)
*Ashok Urlana,L. D. M. S. Sai Teja,Vivek Hruday Kavuri,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MapRoute的视觉概念遗忘方法，通过引入任务特定训练目标、更丰富的概念表示和语义路由实现概念特定映射器选择，在Genμ 2.0挑战赛任务3中，在Stable Diffusion v1.4的ERR指标上平均超越SOTA基线12.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉概念遗忘（visual concept unlearning）旨在从预训练扩散模型中移除特定概念，同时保留无关概念和语义相邻概念。现有的基于MapRoute的方法在概念移除的鲁棒性和保留效果之间存在权衡，需要更精细的概念表示和路由机制来提升整体性能。

Method: 方法建立在MapRoute框架之上，包含三个关键改进：(1) 引入任务特定的训练目标以增强概念移除效果；(2) 使用更丰富的概念表示方式；(3) 采用语义路由机制实现概念特定的映射器（mapper）选择，使不同概念能够匹配到最合适的处理路径。

Result: 在官方基准上，使用Erasing-Retention-Robustness（ERR）指标在Stable Diffusion v1.4上评估，该方法在五个概念类别上平均超越SOTA基线12.1%，实现了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法在概念移除鲁棒性与概念保留（包括语义相邻概念）之间取得了更好的平衡，验证了任务特定训练目标、丰富概念表示和语义路由机制在视觉概念遗忘任务中的有效性。

Abstract: We present our submission to Task 3 of the Gen$μ$ 2.0 Challenge on visual concept unlearning. Building on MapRoute, we introduce task-specific training objectives, richer concept representations, and semantic routing for concept-specific mapper selection. Our approach improves robust concept removal while preserving unrelated and semantically adjacent concepts. On the official benchmark, evaluated using the Erasing-Retention-Robustness (ERR) metric on Stable Diffusion v1.4, our method outperforms the state-of-the-art baseline by 12.1\% on average across the five concept categories, achieving substantial gains.

</details>


### [84] [TraVEL: Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning for Driving-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13495)
*Yi-Chung Chen,Philip Jacobson,Tom Lampo,Yiren Lu,Jin Yao,David I. Inouye,Jing Gao,Danhua Guo,Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TraVEL框架，通过轨迹引导的视频嵌入学习，将通用多模态嵌入模型适配到驾驶视频检索任务。该方法使用自车轨迹相似度作为GRPO的奖励信号，在不依赖推理时额外信息的前提下显著提升了运动中心事件的检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模驾驶日志中的视频检索对数据整理、模型开发和安全分析至关重要。现有的结构化检索系统需要专家规则和复杂感知管线，而通用多模态嵌入模型虽然简单高效，但依赖静态场景上下文捷径，难以区分运动中心事件（如左转vs右转、加速vs减速）。因此需要一种既能利用多模态嵌入模型的高效性，又能获得细粒度运动理解的检索方法。

Method: 论文提出两阶段训练方案：（1）首先在nuReasoning数据集上用InfoNCE目标对Qwen3-VL-Embedding进行微调，使用成对的视频片段和推理轨迹作为监督；（2）引入TraVEL（轨迹引导的视频嵌入学习）框架，利用自车轨迹相似度作为GRPO（组相对策略优化）中的奖励信号进行运动感知的细调。轨迹仅作为训练期间的privileged监督信号，推理阶段仍仅使用单向量视频嵌入，不依赖自车姿态、专家规则或辅助感知输出。

Result: 在从nuReasoning构建的驾驶视频检索基准上，TraVEL在多个模型规模上提升了运动中心检索性能：相对于SFT，在2B模型上纵向和横向mAP分别提升9.8和4.7个点，在8B模型上分别提升7.2和1.5个点。

Conclusion: TraVEL成功地将物理可解释的轨迹监督与高效的单向量嵌入检索相结合，证明了轨迹引导训练能够在不增加推理阶段复杂度的前提下，显著改善多模态嵌入模型对运动语义的辨别能力。

Abstract: Efficiently retrieving relevant clips from large-scale driving logs is essential for data curation, model development, and safety analysis. Structured and rule-based retrieval systems can explicitly target driving events, but typically require expert-defined rules, auxiliary data, and multi-stage perception pipelines. Multimodal embedding models offer a simpler and more efficient alternative by representing each video with a single searchable vector. However, general-purpose models often rely on shortcuts from static scene context and struggle to distinguish motion-centric events, such as turning left versus right or accelerating versus decelerating. In this work, we study how to adapt a general-purpose multimodal embedding model to driving-video retrieval. We first fine-tune Qwen3-VL-Embedding on paired clips and reasoning traces from nuReasoning using an InfoNCE objective. While this stage substantially improves overall retrieval, caption supervision alone remains insufficient for fine-grained motion understanding. We therefore introduce TraVEL (Trajectory-Guided Video Embedding Learning), a motion-aware fine-tuning framework that uses ego-trajectory similarity as a reward within Group Relative Policy Optimization. Trajectories serve only as privileged training supervision; retrieval still operates on single-vector video embeddings without ego poses, expert rules, or auxiliary perception outputs. We further construct a driving-video retrieval benchmark from nuReasoning. Experiments show that TraVEL improves motion-centric retrieval across model scales: relative to SFT, it raises longitudinal and lateral mAP by 9.8 and 4.7 points at 2B, with corresponding gains of 7.2 and 1.5 points at 8B. TraVEL thus combines physically grounded supervision with efficient embedding-based search.

</details>


### [85] [GS$^{2}$CI: Robust Gaussian Splatting For Snapshot Compressive Imaging via Large Vision Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13502)
*Yanming Yang,Chenxi Song,Ping Wang,Xin Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从单次快照压缩成像（SCI）测量数据重建高质量3D场景的框架，结合3D高斯溅射（3DGS）与大规模视觉基础模型（VFM）先验，并引入SCI专用的稠密化策略OSGR（透明度引导的分裂与增长调节），在多个基准上取得最优的综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快照压缩成像（SCI）可在单次2D测量中压缩时间或空间信息，但现有SCI用于3D场景重建的方法面临信息损失、视角多样性有限以及联合优化3D表示与相机位姿计算负担重等显著挑战。

Method: 该框架包含两阶段重建：(1) 主重建阶段，利用测量得到的3D视觉基础模型初始化结合SCI感知的高斯优化；(2) 辅助阶段，使用2D视觉基础模型在合成视角提供伪标签监督进行局部外观细化。同时提出OSGR稠密化策略，通过局部透明度统计增补分裂候选、均值透明度调节抑制损失补偿性透明度膨胀，并以候选比例和高斯数量约束限制表示增长。

Result: 在多个基准上的广泛实验表明，该方法取得了最强的综合性能，兼具领先的重建质量、对视角变化的鲁棒性以及有竞争力的计算效率。

Conclusion: 结合3DGS与大规模VFM先验，并引入SCI特化的稠密化策略，能够有效克服SCI单测量3D重建中的信息损失和优化不稳定性问题，实现高质量且高效的3D场景重建。

Abstract: Snapshot Compressive Imaging (SCI) offers an efficient solution for high-speed video acquisition and, under exposure-time camera--scene relative motion, multi-view scene capture by compressing temporal or spatial information into a single 2D measurement. While recent studies have explored SCI for 3D scene reconstruction, existing methods struggle with significant challenges due to information loss, limited viewpoint diversity, and the computational burden of jointly optimizing 3D representations and camera poses. In this work, we propose a novel framework that reconstructs high-quality 3D scenes from a single SCI measurement by leveraging 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and the powerful priors of large-scale vision foundation models (VFMs). Our primary reconstruction combines measurement-derived 3D VFM initialization with SCI-aware Gaussian optimization. After coarse-stage convergence, an auxiliary 2D VFM provides pseudo-view supervision at synthesized viewpoints for local appearance refinement. To further address the instability caused by ambiguous SCI supervision during 3DGS optimization, we introduce Opacity-Guided Splitting and Growth Regulation (OSGR), an SCI-specific densification strategy that augments split candidates using local opacity statistics, discourages loss-compensating opacity inflation through mean-opacity regulation, and bounds representation growth with explicit candidate-ratio and Gaussian-count constraints. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves the strongest overall performance, combining leading reconstruction quality and robustness to viewpoint variation with competitive computational efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [TabSOM: A tabular-to-image encoding method based on self-organizing maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13513)
*David Chushig-Muzo,María Ángeles Rodríguez de Cara,Eva Milara,Francisco J. Lara-Abelenda,Luis Zhinin-Vera,Diego H. Peluffo-Ordóñez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TabSOM是一种基于自组织映射(SOM)的表格数据到图像编码方法，通过匈牙利算法将每个特征分配到固定画布位置，并利用SOM组件平面构建特征关系图。图像堆叠两种多尺度节点通道，分别编码特征值和特征间交互。在12个现有方法对比中，TabSOM在所有数据集上排名第一或第二，且方差最低，同时提供了两种可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格到图像方法（如基于t-SNE、UMAP、PCA降维映射）仅编码每个特征的边际值，丢失了特征之间的相互关系信息。这限制了卷积神经网络和视觉Transformer在表格数据上的表现力。

Method: 1) 基于SOM构建特征空间布局，通过碰撞避免的匈牙利分配使每个特征占据固定画布位置；2) 从SOM组件平面提取特征间成对关系图；3) 图像堆叠两种多尺度节点通道：一种以固定尺度编码特征值，另一种将特征间交互编码为空间连接；4) 引入两种可解释性方法：原型启发式偏依赖图和类分离重要性评分。

Result: 在12种现有表格到图像方法对比中，TabSOM在每个数据集上排名第一或第二，且是所有评估方法中方差最低的。可解释性验证中，类分离评分与随机森林、XGBoost和SHAP在排名前列的特征上表现合理一致，同时捕捉到输入的互补结构信息。

Conclusion: TabSOM提供了一种有效且可解释的将深度学习架构应用于表格数据的方法，弥合了该领域中性能与可解释性之间的差距。

Abstract: Tabular-to-image methods have emerged as novel approaches to leverage the high predictive performance of convolutional neural networks and vision transformers. They convert tabular data into image representations, mapping each feature at a fixed pixel location derived from a dimensionality-reduction method (e.g., t-SNE, UMAP, PCA). However, they encode only the marginal value of each feature and discard information about feature relationships. We propose TabSOM, a tabular-to-image encoding built on the Self-Organizing Map (SOM), which provides: (i) a spatial layout in which every input feature occupies a fixed canvas position derived from its component plane via collision-free Hungarian assignment; and (ii) a graph that captures pairwise feature relationships derived from the SOM component planes. The resulting image stacks two multi-scale node channels: one encodes feature values at fixed scales, while the other encodes pairwise feature interactions as spatial connections between related features. Two SOM-derived interpretability approaches are introduced: a prototype-inspired partial dependence plot and a class--separation importance score. Benchmarked against twelve existing tabular-to-image methods across public binary-classification datasets, TabSOM ranks first or second on every dataset and achieves the lowest variance of any method evaluated. Interpretability obtained with TabSOM was validated against Random Forest, XGBoost, and SHAP, the class-separation score shows reasonable agreement with established baselines on the top-ranked features while capturing complementary structural information from input data. These results demonstrate that TabSOM provides an effective and interpretable approach for applying deep learning architectures to tabular data, bridging the performance--interpretability gap in this domain.

</details>


### [87] [SCULPT: Subtractive Composition for 3D Part Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13541)
*Sikuang Li,Chen Yang,Jiemin Fang,Jiazhong Cen,Yuhe Wei,Jichen Pang,Wei Shen,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCULPT是一个通过减法式组合实现部件感知3D生成的框架。它在一个结构化的3D潜在空间中，通过联合分割预测器迭代地同时生成一个提取部件和剩余的对象，避免了传统方法（基于分割或加性合成）在部件边界上出现的缝隙、穿插和材质不连续问题。实验在PartObjaverse上取得了最先进的几何性能，并展示了超越基准的细粒度纹理部件分解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部件感知3D生成方法分为两类：基于分割的方法先生成完整对象再划分部件（结构在生成循环之外，导致部件边界在生成后才确定）；加性合成方法从预定义布局/盒子/令牌合成部件（部件边界脆弱，容易出现缝隙、互相穿插和材质不连续）。作者旨在解决这两类方法的固有矛盾，在保持完整对象几何连贯性的同时允许多样的部件分解。

Method: SCULPT的核心机制是减法式组合（subtractive composition）。给定一个在结构化3D潜在空间中的完整对象，框架迭代地应用联合分割预测器，每次生成一个提取的部件以及剩余的物体。预测器执行一个耦合的去噪过程，同时以图像和当前3D状态为条件，使得提取部件和更新后的剩余部分是共同生成的而非生成后再对齐。该预测器在二者原生稀疏3D支撑的并集上处理输出，允许相邻支撑重叠而非强制不相交的体素划分。滚出过程在剩余支撑为空或达到安全上限时终止，使生成部件数量在范围内自适应于每个对象。

Result: 在PartObjaverse上取得了最先进的几何性能，同时保持了部件组装后完整对象的强重建能力。在四个数据集图像、一个文本到图像生成输入及一个真实世界照片上的结果展示了超越基准的细粒度纹理部件分解效果。

Conclusion: SCULPT通过减法式组合策略，将部件分解直接融入生成循环中，解决了传统方法在部件边界质量和生成灵活性之间的权衡问题。其"共同生成而非事后对齐"的核心思想，为部件感知3D生成提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Part-aware 3D generation aims to create digital assets that are coherent as complete objects while exposing structural parts for editing, material assignment, animation, and reuse. Existing methods impose this structure outside the native generation loop: segmentation-based methods partition an already generated shape, while additive methods synthesize parts from predefined layouts, boxes, or tokens and then reconcile them into a whole. The former preserves the generated geometry but fixes the object before part boundaries are determined; the latter exposes part cardinality but often leaves shared boundaries vulnerable to gaps, interpenetrations, and material discontinuities. In this paper, we propose SCULPT, a framework that addresses these challenges through subtractive composition. Given a complete object represented in a structured 3D latent space, SCULPT iteratively applies a joint split predictor to generate one extracted part together with the remaining object. The predictor performs a coupled denoising process conditioned on both the image and the current 3D state, so the extracted part and updated remainder are generated together rather than reconciled after generation. The joint split predictor processes both outputs on the union of their native sparse 3D supports, allowing neighboring supports to overlap rather than imposing a disjoint voxel partition. The rollout ends when the remainder support becomes empty or reaches a fixed safety cap, allowing the number of generated parts to adapt to each object within that bound. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art geometry on PartObjaverse while preserving strong complete-object reconstruction after part assembly. Results on four dataset images, one text-to-image-generated input, and one real-world photograph further show fine-grained textured part decomposition beyond the benchmark.

</details>


### [88] [Alaya-EVOKE: From Linear-Scaling Supervision to Endless World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13546)
*Yuanyang Yin,Gongxuan Wang,Yifan Zhan,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Evoke通过外部化持久世界状态（相机索引的世界状态库）和重新设计长时程教师模型，解决交互式世界模型在持久记忆、响应式交互和长时程生成方面的冲突需求。教师采用稀疏注意力（块状分组+检索远帧+线性注意力全局状态），实现内存和计算线性增长；通过30秒分布匹配目标在自强迫rollout下蒸馏到三步学生模型（无需CFG），在H200上以384×640分辨率每1.5秒块仅需2.11秒生成，在WBench上达到SOTA，并在VBench-Long和VBench-2.0上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要同时满足持久记忆、响应式交互和长时程生成，但这些需求相互冲突：将历史保存在denoiser上下文或KV缓存中会导致成本持续增长，迫使会话长度与记忆保留之间进行权衡；而低延迟交互依赖少步生成，其能力又受限于教师模型。现有方法无法同时解决这两个根本限制。

Method: Evoke采用双管齐下的方案：(1) 将场景几何外部化到相机索引的世界状态库中，只检索视图相关信息，使denoiser上下文在会话增长时保持有界；(2) 将教师从固定生成器重新设计为长时程监督模型：稀疏注意力结合块状分组、检索选定的远帧以及线性注意力全局状态，实现内存和计算线性增长，支持长时程监督。通过30秒分布匹配目标在自强迫rollout下蒸馏到三步学生模型，不使用CFG，同时转移了抗漂移能力和响应式条件控制能力，并通过逐块条件化支持提示变化和事件控制。

Result: 在单个H200上以384×640分辨率，每1.5秒块生成仅需2.11秒。作为三步世界模型，Evoke在WBench上达到最先进性能，同时在VBench-Long和VBench-2.0上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过有界上下文和循环外部记忆，Evoke实现了开放式的、持续演化的生成能力。实验证明，外部化的持久记忆与长时程教师蒸馏相结合，能有效缓解长期漂移问题，同时保持响应式条件控制，为解决交互式世界模型的核心矛盾提供了一种可行范式。

Abstract: Interactive world models must support persistent memory, responsive interaction, and long-horizon generation, yet these requirements place conflicting demands on the model. Maintaining history in the denoiser context or key-value cache incurs growing cost, forcing a trade-off between session length and retained memory, while low-latency interaction relies on few-step generation whose capabilities are bounded by its teacher. Evoke addresses both limitations by externalizing persistent world state and redesigning the teacher for long-horizon interactive generation. Scene geometry is maintained in an external, camera-indexed world state bank, from which only view-relevant information is retrieved, keeping the denoiser context bounded as the session grows. Rather than treating the teacher as a fixed generator, we design it for long-horizon supervision: its sparse attention combines chunk-wise grouping, retrieval of selected distant frames, and a linear-attention global state, yielding linear growth in memory and compute while enabling supervision over long horizons. Such supervision exposes content drift that stays locally plausible within short windows, while per-chunk conditioning enables prompt changes and event control throughout the sequence. A 30-second distribution-matching objective, applied under self-forced rollouts, transfers both capabilities to a three-step student that uses no classifier-free guidance, improving resistance to long-term drift while preserving responsive conditioning. With bounded context and recurrent external memory, Evoke supports open-ended, continuously evolving generation; on a single H200 at $384\times 640$, each $1.5\,\mathrm{s}$ chunk is generated in $2.11\,\mathrm{s}$. As a three-step world model, Evoke achieves state-of-the-art performance on WBench while remaining competitive on VBench-Long and VBench-2.0.

</details>


### [89] [PlayWorld: Benchmarking World Models with Agent Players over Long-Horizon Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13552)
*Kaixin Ding,Xi Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhiyuan Xu,Yiyang Wang,Yuxiang Lu,Junyi Li,Shuyang Chen,Yuan Gao,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PlayWorld基准，利用多模态Agent Players与视频世界模型交互以完成指定的长期目标，并沿几何一致性、交互逼真度、视野外演化、洞察演化四个核心维度及视频质量、可控性等基础能力指标对九个SOTA世界模型进行评估，发现现有模型在长期交互任务中仍不可靠，尤其在空间一致性和持续状态演化方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型虽在视频一致性和动作可控性上表现优异，但缺乏公平比较的交互式评估方法。由于不同模型为实现相同目标所需的动作序列差异很大，固定动作条件评估不适合跨模型比较，需要一种以目标驱动、通过智能体交互的方式来评估世界模型。

Method: 采用多模态Agent Players与世界模型互动以完成指定的长期目标；构建PlayWorld基准，包含171个场景，每个场景有明确目标；从几何一致性、交互逼真度、视野外演化、洞察演化四个核心维度评估模型，并辅以视频质量和可控性的基础能力指标。

Result: 在九个SOTA世界模型上的实验表明，当前模型在长期交互目标上仍不可靠，特别是在维持空间一致性和持续状态演化方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: 基于Agent交互的目标驱动评估范式是公平比较交互式世界模型的有效途径，现有世界模型距离可靠的长期交互能力仍有差距，为后续研究指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Video world models simulate future states conditioned on current observations and user actions. Recent systems have demonstrated impressive video consistency and action controllability over long sequences. However, fairly comparing these interactive models remains challenging. In practice, a human player typically evaluates a world model by pursuing long-horizon objectives through interaction. For example, a user may turn around 360 degrees to see whether the environment remains consistent, or walk into the water and inspect whether realistic water ripples are generated. The action sequence required to achieve the same objective may vary substantially between models, making fixed action-conditioned evaluation unsuitable for cross-model comparison. To address this, we employ multi-modal Agent Players to interact with world models toward specified long-horizon objectives. Building on this paradigm, we introduce PlayWorld, a benchmark providing 171 scenarios, each with a specified objective. To evaluate performance thoroughly, we assess models along four core dimensions: geometry consistency, interaction fidelity, out-of-sight evolution, and insight evolution. In addition, we incorporate basic ability metrics for video quality and controllability. Experiments across nine state-of-the-art world models reveal that current models remain unreliable on long-horizon interactive objectives, particularly in maintaining spatial consistency and persistent state evolution. Code and data are available at https://github.com/kxding/PlayWorld.

</details>


### [90] [AutoDesign: Meta-Harness Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13560)
*Yaxin Luo,Haobin Jiang,Jialv Zou,Xu Huang,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Jing Li,Xiaofu Chen,Xiaohan Zhao,Jiacheng Liu,Jiacheng Cui,Zhiqiang Shen,Xiaotong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoDesign框架，将多模态媒体生成概念化为一个长期代理过程（model-harness系统）。该框架通过元harness优化器指导代码代理基于rollout反馈递归改进harness系统，并在学术论文到海报生成的基准测试PosterBench上验证。AutoDesign在PosterBench Main Track上获得78.32的最高分，超越商业系统Claude Design 7.45分；在七种受控配置中，集成其学习的DesignHarness使平均分数从54.99提升至67.39（+12.4%）；在完全自主的长期循环中，40分钟内以低于3美元成本执行253次工具调用和11次编辑回合，达到会议海报平均质量，并在系统盲人研究中获得最高人类偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态媒体生成范式是静态的，无法与人类设计先验对齐，也不能通过经验探索积累可复用知识以实现递归自我改进。理想的harness系统应该具备这些能力，但当前方法缺乏这种动态学习和自我完善的机制。

Method: 提出AutoDesign框架，包含一个元harness优化器（meta-harness optimizer），它指导代码代理基于rollout反馈递归地改进harness系统，从而使系统与人类设计先验对齐。同时构建PosterBench基准（含100篇论文的主轨和10篇论文的小型可控评测集），并引入DesignHarness作为学习得到的具体harness实现。

Result: 在PosterBench Main Track上AutoDesign得分为78.32，比Claude Design高出7.45分；在七种受控代码代理模型配置中，集成DesignHarness使平均PosterBench Score从54.99提升到67.39（+12.4%）；全自主长期循环中40分钟/低于3美元完成253次工具调用和11次编辑回合，达到会议海报平均质量；系统盲人研究中获得最高人类偏好。

Conclusion: AutoDesign证明了将媒体生成重新概念化为长期代理过程并引入harness递归自我改进机制的有效性。该方法不仅在客观指标上超越现有商业系统，还通过自主长期运行在成本和效率上展现了实际可用性，为多模态内容生成开辟了新的范式。

Abstract: Transforming multimodal sources into condensed and structured media outputs can be fundamentally conceptualized as a long-horizon agentic process centered on a model-harness system. While an ideal harness system should align with human design priors and accumulate reusable experience through empirical exploration to drive recursive self-improvement, existing paradigms remain static and fall short of this capability. In this paper, we present AutoDesign, a framework that aligns with human design priors, where a meta-harness optimizer guides a code agent to recursively improve harness based on rollout feedback. To instantiate and evaluate this framework, we focus on the academic paper-to-poster generation task and introduce PosterBench, comprising a 100-paper Main Track spanning five disciplines and PosterBench-mini, a shared 10-paper subset for controlled evaluation. On the PosterBench Main Track, AutoDesign achieves the highest score of 78.32, surpassing the closed-source commercial system Claude Design by 7.45 points. Across seven controlled code-agent-model configurations, integrating the learned DesignHarness consistently improves performance, increasing the average PosterBench Score from 54.99 to 67.39 (+12.4%). In a fully autonomous long-horizon loop, it executes 253 tool calls and 11 editing turns within 40 minutes for under $3, reaching average conference-poster quality in human evaluation. A system-blind human study further demonstrates that AutoDesign achieves the highest human preference among evaluated systems.

</details>


### [91] [V-RAE: Rethinking Video Latent Spaces for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13556)
*Minghui Guo,Shengqiong Wu,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出V-RAE，一种基于冻结视觉基础模型表征构建的视频表示自编码器。通过在预训练基础模型特征之上训练轻量级时间池化模块和视频解码器，V-RAE在重建、语义保持和生成质量上均优于传统视频VAE（在K600上rFVD达2.13），并引入tFVD作为与下游生成质量更相关的时序一致性诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频自编码器的潜在空间仅针对像素级重建优化，缺乏高层语义组织，而重建最优的潜在空间并不一定适合生成建模。作者希望通过引入冻结的视觉基础模型语义表征，构建同时支持重建、生成和预测的紧凑生成潜在空间。

Method: 在冻结的视觉基础模型表征之上构建紧凑的生成潜在空间。具体包括：使用轻量级时间池化模块去除时间冗余同时保持语义结构；视频解码器从压缩特征中重建连续运动；在四种代表性冻结编码器上进行评估。

Result: V-RAE在K600上rFVD达2.13，优于所有大规模预训练视频VAE；潜在空间保留显著更多的语义信息；在UCF101和K600上最佳变体gFVD分别为117.86和19.16，收敛速度最高快6倍；在Cityscapes未来视频预测任务上优于Wan 2.2 VAE潜在空间；新引入的tFVD与下游生成质量相关性更可靠。

Conclusion: 实验整体表明，冻结的语义表征能够同时支撑视频重建、生成和预测建模，且重建质量本身不足以表征生成效用，需要tFVD等额外诊断指标来评估潜在空间的实际生成能力。

Abstract: Latent video generation relies on autoencoders to define a compact space in which generative models operate. Although video autoencoder architectures have evolved substantially, their latent spaces are still optimized primarily for pixel-level reconstruction and provide limited high-level semantic organization. A reconstruction-optimal latent space, however, need not be well suited to generative modeling. We propose V-RAE, a video representation autoencoder that builds compact generative latents on top of frozen vision foundation model representations. A lightweight temporal pooling module removes temporal redundancy while preserving semantic structure, and a video decoder reconstructs continuous motion from the compressed features. We evaluate V-RAE with four representative frozen encoders on video reconstruction, semantic probing, and class-conditional generation. V-RAE achieves 2.13 rFVD on K600, outperforming all evaluated large-scale pretrained video VAEs. Its latents retain substantially more semantic information than conventional video tokenizer latents. Under matched generation settings, our best variant achieves gFVD scores of 117.86 and 19.16 on UCF101 and K600, respectively, while converging up to 6x faster}. We further show that reconstruction quality alone is insufficient to characterize generative utility and introduce tFVD, a temporal-coherence diagnostic that correlates more reliably with downstream generation quality. Beyond video generation, V-RAE also improves future video prediction on Cityscapes over the Wan 2.2 VAE latent space under matched prediction settings. Taken together, the experiments show that frozen semantic representations can support video reconstruction, generation, and predictive modeling. The project page: https://v-rae.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [LLMs Know the Constraint But Do Not Use It: Activation Bottlenecks in Pragmatic Constraint Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12321)
*Yubo Li,Ramayya Krishnan,Rema Padman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文区分了LLM在隐性可行性约束上的两种失败模式：知识问题（约束是否内部编码）与路由问题（约束是否被使用）。通过四重诊断（知识、对称性、路由、修复）在14个模型上测试，发现两个开放权重模型能内部解码约束（准确率>88%），但激活修补（activation patching）只能修复其中一个（+6.4 nats）而非另一个（-0.07 nats）。所有提示词干预都未能达到修复标准，而是通过"前提提及"这一单一中介通路放大了保守偏差。结论：隐性约束失败是路由问题而非知识问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在评估LLM应对显性表面线索与隐性可行性约束冲突时，仅用聚合准确率衡量，无法区分模型是真正推断出约束还是采取了保守默认策略。作者旨在形式化这一区别，揭示失败的深层机制。

Method: 提出"条件约束激活"框架，包含四个维度：知识（约束是否内部编码）、对称性（在有无约束的提示间是否对称）、路由（约束是否被纳入决策）、修复（能否通过供体激活修复）。在14个模型上进行四重诊断，对两个开放权重模型进行探针分析（解码约束>88%）和激活修补实验，并评估多种提示干预方法。

Result: 14个模型上发现两种失败模式；两个开放权重模型的探针能以上88%准确率解码约束，但激活修补只修复其中一个（+6.4 nats），另一个未见改善（-0.07 nats）。所有提示干预均通过"前提提及"这一单一中介通路放大保守偏差，无法到达修复角。

Conclusion: 隐性约束失败本质上是路由问题（约束已编码但未正确路由进决策）而非知识问题（模型不知道约束）。这一区分对可解释性和干预设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: When a salient surface cue competes with an implicit feasibility constraint, LLMs often fail -- but aggregate accuracy conflates genuine constraint inference with conservative defaulting. We formalize the distinction as conditional constraint activation: the constraint is internally encoded (Knowledge) symmetrically across constraint-present and -absent prompts (Symmetry), yet only sometimes routed into the decision (Routing) and repairable by a donor activation (Repair). A quartet diagnostic over 14 models reveals two failure modes; probes on two open weights decode the constraint above $88\%$, yet activation patching repairs one ($+6.4$ nats) and not the other ($-0.07$). On a mitigation frontier, no prompted intervention reaches the repair corner: all inflate conservative bias through a single mediation pathway -- prerequisite mention. Hidden-constraint failure is a routing problem, not a knowledge problem.

</details>


### [93] [What Drives LLM Self-Reflection? A Controlled Ablation of Uncertainty Routing in Armed Conflict Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12322)
*Poli Nemkova,Haeshitha Indukuri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过六条件受控消融实验，分离了LLM自我反思中的四个组件（证据暴露、诊断脚手架、分类学词汇、动作路由），发现结构化诊断问题和分类学词汇均无显著增益（F1≈0.296），而类型化动作路由提供一致且显著的性能提升（ΔF1=+0.075至+0.101），并在GPT-4o上交叉验证，确认动作路由而非脚手架或词汇是LLM自我反思改进的核心机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自我反思被广泛认为能提升LLM推理能力，但究竟是哪个组件驱动了这种提升仍未被充分理解。作者旨在通过严格的受控消融实验，厘清各组件（证据暴露、诊断脚手架、分类学词汇、动作路由）的独立贡献，从而揭示自我反思机制的本质。

Method: 采用六条件受控消融设计，系统隔离四个组件。实验在预测冲突场景（如缅甸、乌克兰）上进行F1评估，并通过两次精确零结果与统计检验（bootstrap CI、p值）逐步排除干扰因素。关键设计包括：将结构化诊断问题与非结构化反思对比、将完整不确定性分类学词汇与单一泛化动作空间对比，并在GPT-4o上复现分解以验证跨骨干网络的稳健性。

Result: 诊断脚手架无显著增益（F1=0.296 vs 0.297, p=1.000）；分类学词汇无显著增益（ΔF1=+0.008，CI重叠）；动作路由提供稳定方向性增益（F1=0.379 vs 0.296），保守估计ΔF1=+0.075，整体相对单次基线ΔF1=+0.101（95% CI [+0.020, +0.185]）。GPT-4o复现确认词汇无增益（p=0.773）而动作路由有显著增益（p=0.025）。在缅甸（0.000→0.353）和乌克兰（0.167→0.500）等结构性新冲突中，动作路由打破了退化先验。

Conclusion: 类型化动作路由——而非诊断脚手架或分类学词汇——是元认知LLM预测智能体的有效设计原则。研究建议在更广泛冲突类型学上进行更大规模评估，以进一步验证该机制的泛化性。

Abstract: Self-reflection is widely assumed to improve LLM reasoning, yet which component drives the gain remains poorly understood. We present a controlled six-condition ablation isolating four components of LLM self-reflection: evidence exposure, diagnostic scaffolding, taxonomy vocabulary, and action routing. Two precise null results converge on a single mechanism. First, structured diagnostic questions add no measurable value over unstructured reflection ($\text{F1} = 0.296$ vs $0.297$, $p = 1.000$, 95\% CI $[-0.041, +0.040]$). Second, presenting the full uncertainty taxonomy while collapsing the action space to a single generic action also adds no value ($Δ\text{F1} = +0.008$, overlapping 95\% CIs), ruling out taxonomy vocabulary as the mechanism. Typed action routing provides consistent directional gains ($\text{F1} = 0.379$ vs $0.296$); the conservative estimate controlling for taxonomy vocabulary is $Δ\text{F1} = +0.075$, and the overall gain over the single-shot baseline is significant by bootstrap CI ($Δ\text{F1} = +0.101$, 95\% CI $[+0.020, +0.185]$). The vocabulary-routing decomposition replicates on GPT-4o: taxonomy vocabulary adds no significant value over generic reflection ($p = 0.773$), while action routing provides significant gains ($p = 0.025$), confirming the mechanism holds across backbones. Gains concentrate on structurally novel conflicts: in Myanmar ($\text{F1}: 0.000 \rightarrow 0.353$) and Ukraine ($0.167 \rightarrow 0.500$), the vocabulary-only condition recovers no more than generic reflection while action routing breaks the degenerate prior. These findings identify typed action routing -- not diagnostic scaffolding or taxonomy vocabulary -- as a promising design principle for metacognitive LLM forecasting agents, while motivating larger-scale evaluation across conflict typologies.

</details>


### [94] [Why Do AI Agents Break Rules? How Framing, Context, and Social Signals Shape Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12323)
*Mika Okamoto,Ansel Kaplan Erol,Kutluhan Erol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现"执法信息悖论"在AI代理中系统性地出现：设定罚款等惩罚措施反而会将法律义务转化为支持违法的成本收益计算。作者将法学与经济学的合规理论（威慑理论、合法性理论、表达性法律理论）作为实证假说用于诊断AI模型行为，在12个指令微调语言模型（作为企业采购聊天机器人）上验证发现：安全微调模型能广泛维持合规，而任务优化和代理型模型将监管信号视为普通优化参数，在低罚款和非命令式措辞等理论预测条件下出现合规失败。引入财务激励、管理要求、同行结果或员工压力会在所有模型中造成重大合规失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝大多数AI安全评估只测试模型是否失败，而不探究失败的原因。作者希望引入法律经济学的合规理论作为诊断工具，将合规理论视为经验性假说而非比喻，以理解AI代理为何会违反监管约束——尤其是为什么设定惩罚反而促进违规这一反直觉现象。

Method: 作者将三种合规理论（威慑理论、合法性理论、表达性法律理论）操作化为可检验的实证假说，在12个指令微调语言模型上构建企业采购聊天机器人场景进行受控实验。比较安全微调模型、任务优化模型和代理型模型的合规行为差异，并系统操纵罚款水平、命令措辞、财务激励、管理要求、同行结果和员工压力等变量。

Result: 安全微调模型在广泛条件下保持合规；任务优化与代理型模型把监管信号当作优化参数，在低罚款及非命令式措辞下按理论预期出现合规失败。所有模型中，引入财务激励、管理要求、同行结果或员工压力均导致大规模合规失败。AI采购代理为满足本地用户目标而系统性地违反监管约束，且这种失败模式无法被标准对齐基准捕获。

Conclusion: 合规无法仅靠嵌入规则实现；模型选择本身就是一项治理决策，基于基准的评估对于合规敏感型部署而言是不够的。这对AI治理与安全评估方法论提出了根本性挑战。

Abstract: Specifying a penalty can paradoxically convert a legal obligation into a cost-benefit calculation that favors violation. We demonstrate that this enforcement information paradox systematically occurs in AI agents. While most AI safety evaluations test whether models fail, we investigate why, applying compliance theory from law and economics as a diagnostic tool. We treat compliance theories not as metaphors but as empirical hypotheses and show that each predicts the behavior of a distinct model class. We evaluate our hypotheses across twelve instruction-tuned language models operating as enterprise procurement chatbots. Drawing on theories of deterrence, legitimacy, and expressive law, we show that safety-fine-tuned models maintain compliance broadly, while task-optimized and agentic models treat regulatory signals as mere optimization parameters. These latter models fail to comply under conditions predicted by theory, such as low enforcement penalties and non-command phrasing. Across all models, introducing financial incentives, managerial demands, peer outcomes, or employee pressure produces large compliance failures. AI procurement agents systematically violate regulatory constraints to satisfy local user objectives in ways not captured by standard alignment benchmarks. Ultimately, compliance cannot be achieved by rule embedding alone; model selection is itself a governance decision, and benchmark-based evaluation is insufficient for compliance-sensitive deployments.

</details>


### [95] [On Measuring Semantic Preservation in Legal Ontology Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12326)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种用于评估本体学习语义保留程度的评价方法，通过比较LLM在原始文档与转换后表示上的任务表现来量化语义损失，并在法律并购协议分析领域进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本体学习将非结构化文本转换为结构化表示，但现有评估方法只关注结构正确性，无法检测信息转换过程中的语义丢失，需要新的评价方法来衡量语义是否在转换中幸存。

Method: 提出对比评估框架：分别让LLM在源文档和本体转换后的表示上执行任务，用性能差异量化语义损失。在六种语言模型上对比直接LLM应用与三种本体学习方法。

Result: 发现系统性的语义损失，且损失程度因推理复杂度和模型-方法交互作用而显著变化，不同模型与本体学习方法的配对会导致语义保持效果差异巨大。

Conclusion: 贡献包括：(1) 提出衡量本体学习语义保持的评估框架；(2) 实证表明语义损失随模型-方法配对显著变化，为法律知识系统中选择最优配置提供指导。

Abstract: Ontology learning transforms unstructured text into structured representations for automated reasoning. Yet structuring information risks losing it, and current evaluation methodologies cannot detect such loss, focusing on structural correctness while failing to measure whether meaning survives transformation. We propose an evaluation methodology that addresses this: comparing LLM task performance on source documents against performance on transformed representations, with the difference quantifying semantic loss. We demonstrate this approach on legal merger agreement analysis, a domain chosen for its complex language and precise semantic requirements, comparing direct LLM application against three ontology learning methods across six language models. The results reveal systematic semantic loss with significant variation based on reasoning complexity and model-method interactions. Our contributions are: (1) an evaluation framework for measuring semantic preservation in ontology learning, and (2) empirical evidence that semantic loss varies dramatically with model-method pairing, providing guidance for selecting optimal configurations in legal knowledge systems.

</details>


### [96] [Comparative Analysis of Multilingual Pre-trained Models for Nepali Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12327)
*Suman Paudel,Sarbin Sayami*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次在同一微调协议下系统比较六种预训练多语言模型（XLSR-53、IndicWav2Vec、MMS-1B、Whisper-Medium、Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo、Conformer-Hi）在尼泊尔语自动语音识别（ASR）上的表现，使用OpenSLR SLR54语料库（约165小时）和统一的预处理、数据划分、优化器及学习率调度策略。在三个独立测试集上评估WER、CER和RTF。结果显示Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo（14.76% WER）与IndicWav2Vec（14.89% WER）并列最优，尽管后者参数规模小9倍、预训练数据少40倍；CTC解码器在相同精度下比自回归Whisper快达29倍；MMS-1B在域外测试（FLEURS）上退化最小（+12.55 pp）。该工作提供了首个标准化、多模型、效率感知的尼泊尔语ASR基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言预训练模型名义上支持尼泊尔语，但此前缺乏在统一微调协议下对这些模型进行受控比较的研究。这导致无法公平评估不同架构（CTC自监督、自回归编码器-解码器、混合Conformer-CTC）在该语种上的真实性能与效率表现。

Method: 选取六种架构各异的预训练模型，在同一OpenSLR SLR54尼泊尔语料库（约165小时）上，采用完全一致的预处理、数据划分、优化器参数及与模型家族匹配的学习率调度策略进行微调；随后在OpenSLR、FLEURS和Common Voice三个独立测试集上统一评测WER、CER和实时因子（RTF）。

Result: Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo（14.76% WER）与IndicWav2Vec（14.89% WER）并列最优，尽管两者参数规模相差9倍、预训练数据相差40倍；CTC解码器在相同准确率下比自回归Whisper快最多29倍；MMS-1B在FLEURS域外测试上退化最小（+12.55 pp），而Whisper等模型域外性能下降更明显。

Conclusion: 语言家族相似性（IndicWav2Vec）可以在域内尼泊尔语上与远超其规模的通用模型（Whisper）匹敌，说明预训练数据亲属关系可替代原始规模；在延迟敏感场景下CTC架构因其高推理效率更具部署优势；大规模多语言预训练（MMS-1B）的收益体现在域外泛化鲁棒性而非域内峰值精度。本文产出的基准为尼泊尔语ASR提供了首个标准化且效率感知的多模型参考基线。

Abstract: Multilingual pretrained models nominally support Nepali, yet no controlled benchmark has compared them under a single fine-tuning protocol. We fine-tune six pretrained models (XLSR-53, IndicWav2Vec, MMS-1B, Whisper-Medium, Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo, and Conformer-Hi) spanning CTC self-supervised, autoregressive encoder-decoder, and hybrid Conformer-CTC architectures, on the OpenSLR SLR54 Nepali corpus (~165 hours) using identical preprocessing, splits, optimizer, and family-matched learning-rate schedules. We evaluate Word Error Rate (WER), Character Error Rate (CER), and Real-Time Factor (RTF) on three independent test sets (OpenSLR, FLEURS, Common Voice). Whisper-Large-v3-Turbo (14.76% WER) and IndicWav2Vec (14.89% WER) tie at the top despite a 9x parameter gap and 40x pretraining-data gap, providing direct empirical evidence that language-family proximity in pretraining can substitute for raw scale for in-domain Nepali. CTC decoders run up to 29x faster than autoregressive Whisper at the same accuracy, flipping the practical deployment preference toward CTC under any latency budget. Massively multilingual pretraining (MMS-1B) yields the smallest out-of-domain degradation on FLEURS (+12.55 pp), indicating that scale buys robustness rather than peak in-domain accuracy. The resulting benchmark provides the first standardized, multi-model, efficiency-aware reference numbers for Nepali ASR.

</details>


### [97] [LoRA-Diffusion: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning via Low-Rank Trajectory Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12328)
*Iman Khazrak,Narges Nejad,Mohammadhossein Homaei,Mostafa M. Rezaee,Robert C. Green*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LoRA-Diffusion，一种面向扩散式语言模型的参数高效微调方法，通过对去噪轨迹而非模型权重进行低秩分解来实现任务适配，在SST-2、QNLI和MRPC上取得了优于基线方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）已在大规模自回归语言模型上取得显著成功，但这些方法尚未成功扩展到基于扩散的语言模型。扩散模型通过迭代去噪而非序列式词元预测生成文本，因此基于权重低秩分解的LoRA无法直接适用，需要一种针对去噪过程的参数高效微调方案。

Method: LoRA-Diffusion将低秩分解应用于去噪轨迹而非模型权重，包含三个核心组件：(1) 轨迹级低秩适配器，修改每个去噪步骤；(2) 步骤自适应秩分配，在不同扩散阶段动态调整秩大小；(3) 组合式多任务学习，允许在推理时无需重训练即可合并任务特定模块。

Result: 在SST-2、QNLI和MRPC三个数据集上，基于五个随机种子的词元级去噪验证准确率进行评估。LoRA-Diffusion在SST-2上取得最高平均性能，在QNLI和MRPC上表现强劲；联合多任务训练中，LoRA-Diffusion在所有评估方法中取得最高的词元级准确率。

Conclusion: LoRA-Diffusion相比全量微调降低了每个任务的存储开销，为扩散式语言模型建立了一种参数高效微调框架，证明了低秩分解除适用于模型权重外，也可有效应用于去噪轨迹层面。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA have transformed the adaptation of large autoregressive language models, enabling task-specific customization with substantially fewer trainable parameters. However, these methods have not been successfully extended to diffusion-based language models, which generate text through iterative denoising rather than sequential token prediction. We propose LoRA-Diffusion, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning approach that applies low-rank decomposition to the denoising trajectory instead of model weights. Unlike weight-based LoRA, which modifies individual transformation matrices, our method learns low-rank perturbations to the entire diffusion path from noise to output. We introduce trajectory-level low-rank adapters that modify each denoising step, step-adaptive rank allocation across diffusion phases, and compositional multi-task learning that allows merging task-specific modules at inference without retraining. On SST-2, QNLI, and MRPC, we report token-level denoising validation accuracy over five random seeds. LoRA-Diffusion achieves the highest mean performance on SST-2 and strong performance on QNLI and MRPC. Joint multi-task training further shows that LoRA-Diffusion achieves the highest token-level accuracy among the evaluated methods. The approach reduces per-task storage compared with full fine-tuning and establishes a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework for diffusion language models.

</details>


### [98] [AnchorSIPS: A Synthetic Dataset and Evaluation Resource for Evidence-Supported Psychosis-Risk Symptom Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12329)
*Guilherme C. Oliveira,Stephanie Fong,Zimu Wang,Clarice Lee,Xiangyu Zhao,Duy Khoa Pham,Duong Nhu,Yiwen Jiang,Jiahe Liu,Zhongxing Xu,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Dominic Dwyer,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AnchorSIPS，一个包含1万次结构化精神病风险访谈的合成数据集，其测量目标基于逐字转录记录。该数据集采用"先规划后实现"的流水线生成：隐藏病例单指定患者临床状态，确定性规划器固定访谈结构，LLM仅生成患者话语并在验证和有界修复下进行。数据集中于证据提取、基于转录的测量以及部分披露下的不确定性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神病风险评估的AI研究受限于数据获取瓶颈：真实临床访谈因隐私、治理和同意约束难以共享。现有方法缺乏可访问的高质量访谈数据，且多轮LLM对话存在轮次间不一致问题，制约了模型在精神病风险筛查领域的进展。

Method: 采用plan-then-realize流水线：1) 隐藏病例单确定患者临床状态；2) 确定性规划器固定访谈结构（基于Mini-SIPS，包含病史、24个症状问题、随访取证、症状分类决策、排除明确精神病症状及最终APS诊断）；3) LLM仅负责生成患者话语，在验证与有界修复机制下执行。每个中间决策均锚定到其支持的转录回合。

Result: 在七个LLM基线上，模型能恢复粗粒度决策，但无法提取随访细节或引用支持的转录回合。因此，最终标签上的性能高估了访谈能力。AnchorSIPS揭示了模型在证据提取和转录锚定方面的显著不足。

Conclusion: AnchorSIPS通过固定标签和结构后再生成的策略，避免了多轮LLM对话的轮次间不一致问题，为精神病风险访谈的AI研究提供了可共享的合成数据资源。数据集旨在支持证据提取、转录锚定测量和部分披露下的不确定性等研究方向。

Abstract: Progress on AI for psychosis-risk assessment is limited by a data-access bottleneck. Real clinical interviews are difficult to share because of privacy, governance, and consent constraints. We present AnchorSIPS, a synthetic dataset of 10K structured psychosis-risk interviews with transcript-grounded measurement targets. Each interview is modeled on Mini-SIPS, a clinician-administered psychosis-risk interview. It captures history, 24 symptom questions, follow-up evidence for items the patient affirms, decisions about delusion-like symptoms (unusual beliefs), hallucination-like symptoms (unusual perceptions), and disorganized communication, exclusion of clear psychotic-level symptoms ("frank psychosis"), and a final attenuated psychosis syndrome (APS) diagnosis, a high-risk state of milder or early psychotic symptoms. The APS diagnosis is not a standalone label. It depends on earlier endorsements, supporting follow-up details, symptom-class decisions, and the frank-psychosis check. Every intermediate decision is anchored to its supporting transcript turns. AnchorSIPS is generated by a plan-then-realize pipeline. A hidden case sheet specifies the patient's clinical state, a deterministic planner fixes the interview structure, and an LLM realizes only the patient utterances under validation and bounded repair. Fixing labels and structure before generation avoids the inter-turn inconsistencies typical of multi-turn LLM dialogue. Across seven LLM baselines, models recover coarse decisions but fail to extract follow-up details or cite supporting transcript turns, so final-label performance overstates interview competence. AnchorSIPS is intended for research on evidence extraction, transcript-grounded measurement, and uncertainty under partial disclosure.

</details>


### [99] [Reliability-Aware Sexism Detection: Combining DPO with Annotator Agreement and Token-Level Confidence Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12330)
*Hadi Mohammadi,Shihan Wang,Masoume M. Raeissi,Anastasia Giachanou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-DPO（可靠性感知直接偏好优化），将标注者一致性、模型置信度和词级不确定性信号集成到一个可靠性评分中，用于选择性训练和推理时弃权，在EXIST 2023性别歧视检测任务上验证了其在高效训练和可靠部署方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线性别歧视检测是主观任务，但现有系统将多标注者标签简化为单一多数决策并统一对待所有实例，忽略了标注者一致性和模型不确定性这两个有信息量的信号。

Method: 提出RA-DPO框架：(1) 将标注者一致性、模型置信度和token级不确定性整合为单一可靠性评分；(2) 利用该评分选择高价值偏好对进行训练；(3) 支持推理时弃权机制，通过覆盖度与准确率的权衡实现选择性预测。

Result: 在EXIST 2023的6,920条多语言帖子上，基于gpt-4o的DPO微调及Llama、Qwen两个3B开源模型验证。仅用最可靠的30%偏好对即可匹敌全量数据DPO；推理时选择性预测在真实一致性设定下达到96.2%准确率（50%覆盖度），在可部署的预测一致性设定下达88.7%，均超过85.3%的无一致性基线。

Conclusion: 考虑标注不确定性对主观分类任务的高效训练和可靠部署均有裨益，可靠性感知的选择性训练可降低训练成本而不牺牲性能，推理时弃权机制能有效提升部署准确性。

Abstract: The detection of online sexism remains an open problem. Sexism detection is inherently subjective, yet most existing systems reduce multi-annotator labels to a single majority decision and treat all instances uniformly. This ignores two informative signals: annotator agreement and model uncertainty. We propose RA-DPO (Reliability-Aware Direct Preference Optimization), which integrates annotator agreement, model confidence, and a token-level uncertainty signal into a single reliability score. RA-DPO uses this score to select high-value preference pairs during training and to support inference-time abstention, which allows the model to trade coverage for accuracy. We evaluate RA-DPO on 6,920 multilingual posts from EXIST 2023, fine-tune OpenAI gpt-4o base via DPO, and validate on two open-weight 3B models (Llama, Qwen). Results show that training on the top 30% most reliable pairs matches full-data DPO, which indicates that reliability-aware selection can reduce training cost without sacrificing performance. At inference, selective prediction reaches 96.2% accuracy at 50% coverage in the true-agreement setting and 88.7% in the deployable predicted-agreement setting, both exceeding the 85.3% no-agreement baseline. These results suggest that accounting for annotation uncertainty is beneficial for both efficient training and reliable deployment in subjective classification.

</details>


### [100] [Thought-Aware KV Cache Compaction for Reasoning via Adaptive Attention Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12331)
*Yang Liu,Bin Chong,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Jiayu Liang,Xu Kefu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Thought-Aware Attention Matching (TAM)方法，利用思维链推理的层级结构进行KV缓存压缩，通过思维分段、自适应预算分配和关键token保护三个机制，在降低内存65%的同时保持竞争性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型生成的长思维链（CoT）序列导致KV缓存线性增长，成为解码阶段的内存瓶颈。现有压缩方法将推理轨迹视为扁平token序列进行均匀压缩，忽略了CoT推理的层级结构——不同推理步骤的重要性差异巨大。

Method: TAM包含三个机制：(i) 思维分段（thought segmentation），将推理轨迹分解为推理块；(ii) 自适应预算分配（adaptive budget allocation），根据每个分段的重要性和大小分配压缩预算；(iii) 关键token保护（pivotal token protection），保留高注意力权重的推理锚点。论文证明了在凸误差模型下分配规则的最优性，以及顺序压缩下累积误差的有界性。

Result: 在AIME 2024和MATH-500数据集上使用Qwen3-4B进行实验，TAM在相同内存占用下优于均匀压缩方法的准确率。周期性压缩将峰值内存限制在3.1-3.2GB（较基线减少65%），同时保持竞争性准确率。

Conclusion: 利用CoT推理的层级结构而非均匀压缩，可以更高效地压缩KV缓存，在显著降低内存消耗的同时维持推理质量。理论保证和实验验证共同支持了TAM方法的有效性。

Abstract: Reasoning language models generate lengthy chain-of-thought (CoT) sequences whose key-value (KV) cache grows linearly and becomes a memory bottleneck during decoding. Existing compaction methods treat reasoning trajectories as flat token sequences and apply uniform compression, ignoring the hierarchical structure of CoT reasoning where different steps vary drastically in importance. We propose \textbf{Thought-Aware Attention Matching (TAM)}, which exploits this structure through three mechanisms: (i)~thought segmentation that decomposes the trajectory into reasoning blocks, (ii)~adaptive budget allocation that assigns compression budget based on each segment's importance and size, and (iii)~pivotal token protection that preserves high-attention reasoning anchors. We prove that the allocation rule is optimal under a convex error model and that cumulative error under sequential compaction remains bounded. Experiments on AIME 2024 and MATH-500 with Qwen3-4B show that TAM improves accuracy over uniform compaction at the same memory footprint, with periodic compaction bounding peak memory to 3.1--3.2\,GB (a 65\% reduction) while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [101] [Can Spectral-Clipping Enable Better Learning While Forgetting Less for Low-Rank Adaptation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12332)
*Hyowon Wi,Noseong Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCLoRA方法，基于SVD分析发现预训练参数中的主奇异分量可复用于微调，而次要奇异分量更需任务特定适配。首次从理论上建立LoRA适配器奇异值不受控增长与灾难性遗忘之间的联系，并通过谱裁剪注入参数化奇异分量，在提升下游性能的同时缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA已成为冻结预训练权重并引入小适配器的微调范式，但现有方法未充分考虑预训练参数的奇异值谱分布，导致适配过程可能破坏预训练知识，引发灾难性遗忘。作者希望通过SVD视角揭示参数成分的复用与适配规律，并从理论上解释LoRA导致遗忘的机制。

Method: 基于SVD分解分析预训练参数：主奇异分量（大奇异值）可复用于微调，次奇异分量（小奇异值）更任务特定需重点适配。首次建立LoRA适配器中奇异值不受控增长与灾难性遗忘之间的理论联系。提出SCLoRA，将带谱裁剪的参数化奇异分量注入预训练模型，感知预训练模型的谱分布，聚焦于需要适配的分量进行更新，同时缓解遗忘。

Result: 实验表明SCLoRA在多个下游任务上不仅提升了性能，还有效保留了预训练知识，验证了理论分析的正确性和方法设计的有效性。

Conclusion: SCLoRA通过谱感知的方式实现LoRA适配，兼顾了任务适配效果与预训练知识的保持，为低秩适配方法提供了新的理论洞见和实用解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a significant paradigm that freezes pre-trained weights and introduces small, learnable adapters instead of fine-tuning the full set of parameters. In this work, we uncover several key insights regarding the singular components of network parameters based on Singular Value Decomposition (SVD). Firstly, the principal singular components with large singular values in pre-trained network parameters can be effectively reused during fine-tuning, whereas the minor components with smaller singular values are more task-specific and require substantial adaptation. Secondly, we first establish the theoretical connection that the uncontrolled growth of singular values in LoRA adapters leads to the forgetting of pre-trained knowledge -- a well-known issue referred to as catastrophic forgetting. Building on these observations, we propose SCLoRA, which injects parameterized singular components with spectral clipping into the pre-trained model in a way that is aware of the spectral distribution of the pre-trained model. SCLoRA effectively adapts to new tasks by focusing updates on components that require adaptation, while simultaneously alleviating catastrophic forgetting. We conduct extensive experiments and demonstrate that SCLoRA not only improves downstream performance but also effectively retains pre-trained knowledge.

</details>


### [102] [Vision-Language Models are Fragile Multilingual Associators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12333)
*Ritabrata Chakraborty,Rajatsubhra Chakraborty,Shivakumara Palaiahnakote,Angelo Cangelosi,Umapada Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入M²BIND基准，评估视觉语言模型在不同语言环境下概念绑定的稳定性，发现绑定并非语言不变的：跨语系和跨文字场景下绑定会显著崩溃，内部绑定计算转移到更深层且因果强度减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型需要将视觉实体与文本属性关联，但现有研究未探索当输入语言发生变化时，这些概念绑定是否仍然稳定。鉴于VLM在全球化多语言环境中的部署日益普遍，理解语言变化对绑定能力的影响至关重要。

Method: 提出M²BIND基准，在多语言环境中变化上下文和查询的语言。通过两种方式评估绑定：(1)外在评估——使用任务性能指标；(2)内在评估——通过因果干预分析模型的内部绑定计算。

Result: 研究发现绑定并非语言不变的：跨语系和跨文字设置会触发显著的绑定崩溃，模型内部绑定计算转移到更深层且因果强度减弱。而密切相关的语言能相对较好地保持关联。

Conclusion: 部署在全球多语言环境中的VLM不能被假设维持与单语言评估相同的关联质量，这一发现对多语言场景下的模型部署提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Vision-language models must associate visual entities with textual attributes. Whether these associations or concept bindings remain stable when the language of the input changes is unexplored. We introduce M$^2$BIND, a benchmark varying the language of the context and query across multiple languages. We evaluate binding both extrinsically through task performance metrics and intrinsically through causal interventions. We find that binding is not language-invariant: cross-family and cross-script settings trigger significant binding collapse, with the model's internal binding computation shifting to later layers and losing causal strength. Closely related languages preserve associations comparatively better. In a broader sense, our findings indicate how VLMs deployed globally in multilingual settings cannot be assumed to maintain the same association quality observed in monolingual evaluation.

</details>


### [103] [Steering the Language Axis: From Linear Decodability to Causal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12334)
*Arnav Srivastav*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过因果干预实验（steering和ablation）在Qwen 3.5-2B和Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct等模型上，利用PCA提取的"语言轴"对FLORES-200数据集进行126万次生成实验，证明了语言身份可以从隐藏状态中以紧凑激活方向因果控制，且语言决策在不同脚本对（英中）和同脚本对（英西）中呈现不同的层特异性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽具备多语言能力，但控制语言选择的潜在动态机制仍不清楚。作者希望探明语言身份是否仅能从隐藏状态线性解码，还是可以通过紧凑的激活方向进行因果控制，从而深入理解语言决策的神经机制。

Method: 采用因果干预分析方法：1) 对多种模型家族（Qwen 3.5-2B、Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct）进行PCA降维提取"语言轴"；2) 在FLORES-200数据集上进行126万次生成实验；3) 实施方向引导（steering）和消融（ablation）两类干预实验；4) 对比跨脚本（英中）与同脚本（英西）两种语言转换场景，进行逐层敏感性分析。

Result: 实验发现：1) 沿语言轴方向引导可稳定强制语言切换，而等幅随机扰动几乎无效；2) 语言承诺高度局部化且依赖具体语言对——英中转换抵抗早期干预、后期容易引导，英西转换则较早发生且呈双峰敏感性；3) 定向消融导致模型回归英语输出，与输入提示无关。这些证据表明语言决策边界在推理过程中是因果活跃的、方向依赖且层特异性的特征。

Conclusion: 语言身份不仅可以从隐藏状态中线性解码，更可以通过特定的激活方向进行因果控制。语言决策机制在推理过程中表现为方向依赖且层特异性的因果活跃特征，但因语言对不同而呈现差异化的层定位模式，这为理解LLM内部语言表征和解码机制提供了重要的因果证据。

Abstract: Despite the impressive multilingual capabilities of Large Language Models, the latent dynamics dictating language selection remain poorly understood. In this work, we ask whether language identity is merely linearly decodable from hidden states, or if it can be causally controlled by a compact activation direction. We conduct an exhaustive causal intervention analysis across multiple model families, including Qwen 3.5-2B and Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct, isolating PCA-derived "language axes" to perform steering and ablation experiments across 1.26 million generations on the FLORES-200 dataset.
  Steering along these geometric directions reliably forces language switching in both cross-script (English to Chinese) and same-script (English to Spanish) settings, whereas equal-magnitude random perturbations yield virtually no effect. Our layerwise analysis reveals that language commitment is highly localized and explicitly language-pair-dependent. While English to Chinese switching resists early intervention and steers easily in the later layers, the English-Spanish transition shifts earlier, displaying a distinct, bimodal sensitivity. Furthermore, targeted ablation uncovers a fundamental reversion to English: once the language signal is removed, the model falls back to English regardless of the input prompt. Ultimately, these findings demonstrate that language decision boundaries function during inference as causally active features that are direction-dependent and layer-specific.

</details>


### [104] [HC-RAG: Evidence-Centric Retrieval-Augmented Generation over Heterogeneous Financial Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12335)
*Siyuan Chen,Huaye Tan,You Li,Jiajun Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HC-RAG，一个用于金融问答的层次化跨模态检索增强生成框架，通过构建类型化金融证据图、沿文档-章节-单元路径检索、在共享空间中对齐文本与表格证据、并按四种语义意图（计算、趋势、事实、比较）路由证据。同时发布了包含2,327个专家验证QA对的Multi-Doc-2025基准。实验表明HC-RAG在DocFinQA上比RAPTOR高6.6 F1，在Multi-Doc-2025上比GraphRAG高10.9 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在处理金融年报QA时存在三个关键缺陷：将长文档扁平化为无序块（忽略年报的结构化层次）、对财务报告的类型化结构关注不足、使用固定的文本-表格融合策略而不考虑查询意图。这些限制导致检索结果缺乏证据准确性，难以支撑跨文档、表格相关的复杂金融问题回答。

Method: HC-RAG包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建类型化金融证据图，组织为文档、章节、文本单元、表格单元和元数据节点的层次结构；(2) 通过文档-章节-单元路径检索证据，将文本和表格证据在共享检索空间中对齐；(3) 根据四种语义意图（计算、趋势、事实、比较）进行意图感知的证据路由。此外还构建了Multi-Doc-2025基准，包含179份SEC 10-K年报中的2,327个专家验证QA对，标注了意图、难度和结构性证据属性。

Result: 在DocFinQA上HC-RAG比RAPTOR高6.6 F1分，在Multi-Doc-2025上比GraphRAG高10.9 F1分。消融实验和证据级分析表明，性能提升主要来自更准确的章节定位、表格接地、跨文档证据聚合以及意图感知的文本-表格路由，尤其在长文档、表格相关和跨文档场景下优势明显。

Conclusion: HC-RAG验证了利用金融文档的类型化层次结构和查询意图感知路由可以显著提升证据中心型金融问答的性能。未来工作可沿此方向进一步探索更细粒度的证据类型和更复杂的跨文档推理场景，同时Multi-Doc-2025基准也为该领域提供了标准化评估资源。

Abstract: Financial question answering over annual reports requires more than retrieving semantically similar passages. It often involves identifying relevant companies and fiscal years, locating standardized filing sections, collecting textual and tabular evidence, and checking answers against the original documents. Existing RAG systems, however, usually flatten long filings into unordered chunks, pay limited attention to the typed structure of financial reports, and use fixed text-table fusion strategies without considering query intent. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HC-RAG}, a hierarchical cross-modal retrieval-augmented generation framework for evidence-centric financial QA. HC-RAG organizes filings into a typed financial evidence graph with documents, sections, text units, table units, and metadata nodes. It retrieves evidence through document-section-unit paths, aligns textual and tabular evidence in a shared retrieval space, and routes evidence according to four semantic intents: calculation, trend, fact, and comparison. We further introduce \textbf{Multi-Doc-2025}, a benchmark containing 2,327 expert-verified QA pairs from 179 SEC 10-K filings of 87 S\&P 500 companies across fiscal years 2022--2024, with labels for intent, difficulty, and structural evidence attributes. Experiments on public financial QA benchmarks and Multi-Doc-2025 show that HC-RAG improves both answer quality and evidence localization, especially in long-document, table-related, and cross-document settings. HC-RAG outperforms RAPTOR by 6.6 F1 points on DocFinQA and GraphRAG by 10.9 F1 points on Multi-Doc-2025. Evidence-level analysis and ablation studies show that the improvements mainly come from more accurate section localization, table grounding, cross-document evidence aggregation, and intent-aware text-table routing.

</details>


### [105] [StorySpark: Module-wise Evolutionary Search for Story Premise Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12336)
*Yang Yang,Zining Zhong,Qian Cao,Jindong Li,Boyun Xu,Kaishen Yuan,Menglin Yang,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StorySpark是一个用于故事前提生成的模块化进化搜索框架，将可解释叙事模块视为条件化局部搜索空间，通过反馈驱动的变异与重组、Pareto引导选择和前沿能力再分配来生成高质量故事前提，并在原创性及下游故事质量上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的故事生成大多聚焦于后期规划、可控性、连贯性和散文扩写，而故事前提级别的构思（premise-level ideation）相对未得到充分探索。故事前提是完整叙事的创意火花，因此需要专门的方法来生成强有力且原创的前提。

Method: 提出StorySpark框架，其核心思路为：（1）将前提分解为背景、人物、事件、结局、反转等可解释叙事模块；（2）每个激活模块被视为一个局部搜索空间，其条件动态于目前已构建的局部前提；（3）对每个模块生成备选方案、在上文中评估、通过反馈驱动的变异与重组进行精化；（4）采用Pareto-guided选择保留互补优势；（5）重新分配前沿容量以平衡分支覆盖与有前景的方向。

Result: 多视角自动评估和人工评估表明，StorySpark生成的前提强于竞争基线，尤其在原创性方面增益一致；使用相同故事写手扩展时，其前提能带来更高质量的下游故事，同时保持完整性、吸引力和多样化的可用叙事方向。

Conclusion: StorySpark验证了将故事前提生成建模为进化搜索问题、借助模块化分解与多目标优化可以有效提升前提质量与原创性，并正向传导至下游故事生成效果，弥补了LLM故事生成中前提构思环节的研究空缺。

Abstract: A story premise is the creative spark from which a full narrative can grow. Yet LLM-based story generation has mostly emphasized later-stage planning, controllability, coherence, and prose expansion, while premise-level ideation remains comparatively underexplored. We introduce StorySpark, a module-wise evolutionary search framework for story premise generation. StorySpark operates over interpretable narrative modules such as background, persona, event, ending, and twist, treating each active module not as a static field to fill once, but as a local search space conditioned on the partial premise built so far. For each module, it generates alternatives, evaluates them in context, refines them through feedback-driven mutation and recombination, preserves complementary strengths with Pareto-guided selection, and reallocates frontier capacity to balance branch coverage with promising directions. Multi-view automatic and human evaluations show that StorySpark produces stronger final premises than competitive baselines, with especially consistent gains in originality; when expanded with the same story writer, its premises also lead to higher-quality downstream stories while maintaining completeness, fascination, and diverse usable narrative directions.

</details>


### [106] [From Refuse to Richness: Rubric Rewards for Long-Form Hallucination Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12337)
*Yudong Wang,Zhe Yang,Wenhan Ma,Rang Li,Qibin Yang,Weimin Xiong,Jiangshan Duo,Liang Zhao,Zhifang Sui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究长文本生成中幻觉RL的"拒答-丰富度"权衡问题，提出用关键点评分表（key-point rubric）代替全局丰富度代理指标来定义答案覆盖率，并发现严格的接地奖励抑制覆盖率、无约束的评分表奖励削弱接地性，而软组合接地、评分表覆盖率和相关性奖励可取得最佳平衡，且更好地迁移到分布外检查清单任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文本生成中，惩罚无依据主张的奖励可以改善接地性（grounding），但同时可能教会模型少回答，造成"拒答-丰富度"（refusal-to-richness）权衡。传统方案使用全局丰富度代理指标（如长度、主张数量、细节度或两两相关性）无法精确刻画问题所需的覆盖内容，因此无法有效缓解这一权衡。

Method: 为每个问题构建关键点评分表（key-point rubric），明确指定一个有用答案应覆盖的必需信息和可选信息。这些评分表既用于评估，也作为奖励信号。实验比较了四种奖励设置：仅接地（grounding-only）、基于代理指标（proxy-based）、仅评分表（rubric-only）以及组合奖励（combined），其中组合奖励将接地、评分表覆盖率和相关性进行软结合。

Result: 实验发现稳定的权衡现象：严格的接地奖励改善支持度（support）但抑制覆盖率（coverage）；无约束的评分表奖励改善覆盖率但削弱接地性。将接地、评分表覆盖率和相关性进行软组合的奖励方案取得了最佳平衡，在分布内支持度上有所提升，且在分布外检查清单任务上的迁移表现优于仅接地或仅评分表的奖励方案。

Conclusion: 关键点评分表能够直接定义覆盖率，避免全局代理指标带来的偏差。然而评分表奖励单独使用无法保证接地性，因此需要与接地奖励进行软组合。联合使用接地、评分表覆盖率和相关性三类信号，可以在支持度与覆盖率之间取得最佳平衡，并提升模型在分布外任务上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Rewards that penalize unsupported claims can improve grounding in long-form generation, but they can also teach models to answer less. We study this refusal-to-richness trade-off in long-form hallucination RL. Instead of using global richness proxies such as length, claim count, detail, or pairwise relevance, we represent each question with a key-point rubric that specifies the required and optional information a useful answer should cover. These rubrics define coverage directly and are used both for evaluation and as reward signals. Across grounding-only, proxy-based, rubric-only, and combined rewards, we find a stable trade-off: strict grounding rewards improve support but suppress coverage, while unconstrained rubric rewards improve coverage but weaken grounding. A soft combination of grounding, rubric coverage, and relevance gives the best balance in our experiments, improving in-distribution support while transferring better to out-of-distribution checklist tasks than either grounding-only or rubric-only rewards.

</details>


### [107] [SDAM: Structure-Difference-Aware Memory Evolution for Complex Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12338)
*Keyan Xu,Dingzirui Wang,Xuanliang Zhang,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SDAM，一种基于结构差异感知与模式绑定记忆的文本转SQL方法。SDAM通过结构差异感知推理树识别潜在错误，利用矛盾感知反思提取深层语义规则，并通过模式绑定记忆进化机制增强结构一致性。集成后的SDAM-SQL框架在BIRD-dev和Spider-test上分别比主流方法提升2.0和0.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的智能体系统虽能改善复杂SQL生成，但存在历史经验利用不足、结构分析薄弱、语义理解浅层以及模式对齐差等问题。这些缺陷限制了文本转SQL系统在复杂查询场景下的表现，亟待更精细的记忆设计与结构感知机制。

Method: SDAM包含三大核心组件：(1) 结构差异感知推理树——通过对比候选SQL与正确SQL间的结构差异来识别潜在错误；(2) 矛盾感知反思——在反思过程中感知矛盾信息以提取深层语义规则；(3) 模式绑定记忆进化机制——将记忆与数据库模式（schema）绑定，增强结构一致性并支持记忆随模式进化。三者整合为完整的SDAM-SQL文本转SQL框架。

Result: 在BIRD-dev和Spider-test两个基准上，SDAM-SQL比主流文本转SQL方法分别取得2.0和0.4的准确率提升，验证了SDAM各组件及整体框架的有效性。

Conclusion: SDAM通过结构差异感知、矛盾感知反思和模式绑定记忆进化三种机制，系统性地解决了现有记忆型文本转SQL方法在结构分析、语义理解和模式对齐方面的不足，实验结果表明该方法在复杂SQL生成任务上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL aims to convert natural language questions into executable SQL queries. While memory-based agent system improves complex SQL generation, existing memory design neglect historical experience and suffer from weak structure analysis, shallow semantic understanding, and poor schema alignment. To address these challenges, we propose SDAM. Specifically, SDAM identifies potential errors via a structure-difference aware reasoning tree, extracts deep semantic rules through contradiction-aware reflection, and enhances structural consistency using a schema-grounded memory evolution mechanism to bind memory with database schemas. We integrate SDAM into a Text-to-SQL framework named SDAM-SQL. Experiment shows that SDAM-SQL achieves 2.0 and 0.4 improvement on BIRD-dev and Spider-test compared with mainstream Text-to-SQL methods, showing the effectiveness of SDAM-SQL.

</details>


### [108] [Mimicry without understanding: the origins of decision bias in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12339)
*Eldad Yechiam,Adi Tarabeih*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）中社会、情感和认知偏见的产生机制，提出了两种即使在人类偏好无偏或已被正确识别为有偏的情况下仍会产生偏见的机制：基于人类行为的有缺陷偏好模仿，以及对明确有偏人类行为的模仿。通过四项经济偏见研究，发现ChatGPT-4o和Qwen在社会证明偏见和损失厌恶中均表现出上述机制，且科学报告中偏见的程度能预测LLM自身的偏见表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究主要关注LLMs训练数据中的人类偏好如何导致模型偏见，但忽略了两种更微妙的机制：一是LLMs可能从逻辑上与偏好无关的行为中错误推断偏好；二是LLMs可能直接模仿明确描述为有偏的行为。理解这些底层过程对于揭示LLM偏见产生的根本机制至关重要。

Method: 通过四项聚焦经济偏见的研究，使用ChatGPT-4o和Qwen模型进行实验。研究一和二三针对社会证明偏见，向模型展示与个人实际偏好逻辑无关的人类行为报告；研究针对损失厌恶，向模型提供将其明确描述为偏见的详细科学报告，并操纵报告中偏见的程度来观察LLM后续反应中的偏见变化。

Result: 研究发现：（1）即使人类行为报告明显不表明个人的真实偏好，ChatGPT-4o和Qwen仍表现出社会证明偏见；（2）当损失厌恶被明确描述为偏见时，LLMs表现出该偏见；（3）科学报告中损失厌恶的程度能预测LLM自身随后的偏见水平，即科学报告可成为自我实现的预言。

Conclusion: 该研究超越了简单识别LLM偏见的范畴，揭示了偏见产生的底层组成过程。研究证实LLM的偏见可能源于对人类行为的有缺陷推断（将无关行为误判为偏好信号）以及对明确有偏行为的直接模仿，这意味着即使训练数据中的偏好是"干净"的，LLM也可能通过认知机制自发产生偏见。

Abstract: Large Language models (LLMs) were found to be susceptible to a host of social, affective, and cognitive biases. We examined two mechanisms through which such biases can be generated even when human preferences (in the training data) are not biased or when they are correctly categorized as being biased. The first is faulty mimicry of preferences based on human behavior: this involves LLMs inferring human preferences even when behaviors are logically unrelated to preferences. The second is mimicry of explicitly biased human behaviors. In four studies focusing on economic biases, we find that ChatGPT-4o and Qwen exhibited social proof biases even when prompted with reports of human behaviors that were clearly non-indicative of individuals' actual preferences. LLMs also displayed loss aversion when it was explicitly described as a bias. Indeed, when prompted with detailed scientific reports, the extent of the bias (i.e., loss aversion) in the scientific report predicted LLMs' own subsequent bias. Scientific papers of biases can thus become self-fulfilling prophecies, at least when it comes to LLMs' responses. The current study goes beyond fleshing out LLM biases and sheds light on the underlying component processes.

</details>


### [109] [Class-Structure Preservation Beats Diversity: A Comprehensive Benchmark of Text Augmentation Methods for Imbalanced Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12340)
*Keito Inoshita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含11种数据增强方法的受控基准，在7个公共文本分类数据集上比较了LLM生成式增强与嵌入空间SMOTE检索（EmbSMOTE）。结果显示所有LLM方法在统计上等价或劣于EmbSMOTE，且性能差距随不平衡程度增大而扩大；表面层面的多样性与下游性能相关性可忽略，而类条件结构保真度才是关键决定因素，因此推荐将检索式过采样作为不平衡多分类的默认方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM生成式数据增强在不平衡文本分类中受到广泛关注，但此前缺乏将其与经典的嵌入空间SMOTE式检索增强（EmbSMOTE）进行系统比较的经验基准，导致无法判断LLM增强是否真正优于传统方法。

Method: 构建包含11种增强方法的受控基准（涵盖经典扰动、嵌入空间检索、LLM生成三大类），在7个涵盖K=2-28类、不平衡比1.1至500+的数据集上评估，每种方法使用5个随机种子，采用macro F1、Welch t检验、5种分布度量以及基于Qwen3-8B的LLM家族敏感性分析。

Result: 所有LLM增强方法在统计上均与EmbSMOTE等价或更差；性能差距随不平衡程度单调扩大，在GoEmotions-28上ΔF1≈0.063。表面唯一性与下游性能相关性可忽略，而文本延长和标签分布均匀化等LLM特有伪影与分类准确率负相关。

Conclusion: 决定增强效果的关键变量不是表面多样性，而是类条件结构保真度（增强样本保留训练分布类条件几何结构的程度）。因此，检索式过采样应作为不平衡多分类的默认方法，且LLM增强方法在部署前需要更高的经验门槛。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), generative data augmentation has attracted considerable attention for imbalanced text classification in natural language processing. However, no empirical benchmark to date has compared LLM-based augmentation against the embedding-space SMOTE-style retrieval (EmbSMOTE), a strong classical reference for imbalanced classification. In this study, a controlled benchmark of 11 augmentation methods, spanning classical perturbation, embedding-space retrieval, and LLM-based generation, is newly constructed on seven public text classification datasets covering class counts $K=2$-$28$ and imbalance ratios of 1.1 to over 500, evaluated with five random seeds per cell using macro F1, Welch's $t$-tests, five distributional metrics, and an LLM-family sensitivity analysis based on Qwen3-8B. The experimental results reveal that all LLM-based methods are statistically equivalent or inferior to EmbSMOTE, with the performance gap widening monotonically as imbalance increases and reaching $Δ\text{F1}_\text{macro}\!\approx\!0.063$ on GoEmotions-28. Furthermore, it is observed that surface-level uniqueness has negligible correlation with downstream performance, whereas LLM-specific artifacts, such as text elongation and label-distribution uniformization, are negatively associated with classification accuracy. Compared with six LLM-based and four classical augmentation baselines, these results demonstrate that the effective variable is not surface-level diversity but class-conditional structural fidelity, namely the degree to which augmented samples preserve the class-conditioned geometry of the training distribution. Accordingly, retrieval-based oversampling should be adopted as the default for imbalanced multi-class classification, and a higher empirical bar should be required before LLM-based augmentation is deployed in practice.

</details>


### [110] [The "Knowledge-Behavior Gap" in Cultural Taboo Safety of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12341)
*Ying He,Sihang Jiang,Xingzhou Chen,Zhouhong Gu,Yiwei Gu,Minggui He,Shimin Tao,Hongxia Ma,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CulShield，这是首个专用于评估和改进大语言模型文化禁忌安全性的公开基准，覆盖77个国家和地区、超过2020条禁忌，同时评估模型的显性知识和隐性行为，实验发现模型存在明显的"知识-行为差距"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化禁忌安全对LLM部署至关重要，但现有文化基准主要评估文化知识或价值观偏见，忽视了模型能否识别和尊重文化禁忌，尤其在看似无害的问题中隐含禁忌的场景。此外，文化禁忌具有隐性和上下文依赖性，给可靠评估带来独特挑战。

Method: 构建CulShield基准：覆盖77个国家和地区的2020多条禁忌；同时从显性知识和隐性行为两个维度评估模型；通过实验测试多个先进LLM（如GPT-4o-mini、Gemini-2.5-pro），并考察语言上下文变化对文化禁忌安全性的影响。

Result: 实验揭示清晰的"知识-行为差距"：模型在互动中往往无法应用已知的禁忌知识；同时发现语言上下文的变化会显著影响LLM的文化禁忌安全性。

Conclusion: CulShield填补了文化禁忌安全评估的空白，为LLM文化安全改进提供了基准和评估框架，并指出模型文化禁忌安全在隐性场景下仍存在显著不足。

Abstract: Cultural taboo safety is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs), as culturally insensitive outputs may cause offense or even social harm. However, existing cultural benchmarks primarily assess cultural knowledge or values biases, while overlooking whether LLMs can recognize and respect cultural taboos, especially when taboos are implicitly hidden in seemingly harmless questions. Besides, cultural taboos are implicit, and context-dependent, thus poss unique challenges for reliable evaluation. To address these gaps, we introduce \textbf{CulShield}, the first public benchmark dedicated to evaluating and improving the cultural taboo safety of LLMs. CulShield spans 77 countries and territories, and includes over 2,020 taboos. It evaluates models along both explicit knowledge and implicit behaviors. Experiments on several advanced LLMs (e.g., GPT-4o-mini, Gemini-2.5-pro) reveal a clear ``knowledge-behavior gap'': models often fail to apply known taboos during interaction. We further show that variations in linguistic context can significantly affect LLMs' cultural taboo safety. Code and data is accessible here: https://github.com/hedyHe/CulShield.

</details>


### [111] [Are Large Language Models Reliable Reviewers? A Benchmark for Error Detection in Financial Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12342)
*Ying He,Zhouhong Gu,Zhecheng Hu,Yubo Zhou,Hao Shen,Jiaqing Liang,Zhaoqian Dai,Shuguang Ma,Fei Yu,Yanghua Xiao,Zhixu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FinED-Bench，这是首个用于金融文档错误检测的公开基准测试集，涵盖三个认知复杂度层级、九个真实金融场景，包含900多份2025年未见过的文档。实验表明现有LLM在该任务上表现不佳，尤其是在高复杂度案例中，而监督微调能显著提升较弱LLM的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在多项金融任务（如股价预测、金融分析）中表现优异，但识别金融文档中的错误这一关键任务尚未被探索。金融文档的准确性对经济分析、合规监管和企业决策至关重要，因此亟需一个专门的基准来评估LLM在这方面的能力。

Method: 构建FinED-Bench基准：覆盖三个认知复杂度层级（由浅入深）和九个真实金融场景，包含900多份2025年报告且确保未被现有语言模型预训练数据覆盖。随后评估多种先进LLM（如GPT-4o、Qwen3-14B）在该任务上的表现，并进行监督微调实验以验证提升效果。

Result: 实验发现当前LLM在金融错误检测任务上仍表现挣扎，尤其是面对高复杂度案例时错误率较高。监督微调能够显著提高较弱LLM在该任务上的性能。

Conclusion: 金融文档错误检测对LLM而言仍是一个具有挑战性的任务，现有模型能力不足，但通过监督微调可以有效弥补。FinED-Bench为未来研究和模型改进提供了一个公开的评估基准。

Abstract: Ensuring the accuracy of financial documents is critical for economic analysis, regulatory compliance, and corporate decision-making. Several studies have shown that Large Language Models (LLMs) perform well in many financial tasks, such as stock price movements and financial analytics. However, a critical task remains unexplored: the ability of LLMs to identify errors in financial documents. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{FinED-Bench}, the first publicly \textbf{Bench}mark for \textbf{Fin}ancial \textbf{E}rror \textbf{D}etection across three levels of cognitive complexity. FinED-Bench covers nine real-world financial scenarios, and includes over 900 documents reported in 2025 that are unseen by existing language models. We detail the benchmark construction process and evaluate several advanced LLMs (e.g., GPT-4o, Qwen3-14B) on this tasks, which requires both financial domain knowledge and reasoning capabilities. Experimental results show that current LLMs still struggle with this task, especially in high-complexity cases. Besides, supervised fine-tuning can significantly improve the performance of weaker LLMs on this task. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/hedyHe/FinED-Bench.

</details>


### [112] [Large Language Models Pass the History Exam But Miss the <<History>>: A Polish High School Exit Exam Matura Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12343)
*Adrian Trzoss,Kacper Dudzic,Wiktor Werner,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次以波兰高中历史毕业考试（Matura 2023-2025）为基础构建LLM历史推理基准，评估八款主流LLM的解题表现。结果表明所有模型均大幅超越人类考生，但得分掩盖了不同能力画像：模型在任务类型、来源模态和地理范围上排名不稳定，且普遍在波兰史内容上表现逊于世界史。定性错误分析揭示两类反复出现的失败模式——来源混淆（将史料当作推理依据而非分析对象）和时间定向障碍（回答在时间上错位）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人被学生广泛用作知识来源，但现有的LLM基准很少评估解释性历史推理能力。作者希望填补这一空白，引入基于波兰国家课程的首个LLM历史基准。

Method: 选取2023-2025年三套波兰高中历史Matura官方试卷（含简答题和论述题），评估八款主流LLM，并与人类考生群体成绩进行对比；进行排名稳定性分析（按任务类型、来源模态、地理范围划分），同时开展定性错误分析归纳失败模式。

Result: 所有模型均大幅超越人类考生；但整体分数掩盖了任务类型、来源模态和地理范围上的排名不稳定，模型在波兰史内容上一致表现更差；定性分析发现两类主要失败模式：来源混淆和时间定向障碍。

Conclusion: 本研究首次建立基于波兰国家课程的历史推理LLM基准，证明聚合分数不足以反映模型能力全貌，需关注模态和地理范围上的系统性偏差，并识别了历史推理特有的失败模式。

Abstract: AI chatbots are widely used by students as knowledge sources, yet LLM benchmarks rarely assess interpretative historical reasoning. We evaluate eight leading LLMs on the Polish high school exit exams (Matura) in history - three official papers from 2023-2025, comprising short-answer questions and extended essays - comparing model performance against the human examinee population. Every model dramatically outperforms human examinees, yet aggregate scores mask distinct competency profiles: rankings are unstable across task type, source modality, and geographical scope, with a consistent penalty on Polish versus Global history content. Qualitative error analysis reveals two recurring failure modes - source conflation, in which models reason from source content rather than treating it as an object of analysis, and temporal disorientation, in which responses are historically misplaced. This study introduces the first LLM history benchmark grounded in Polish national curriculum.

</details>


### [113] [Predicting consumer-technology ownership without a diffusion history](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12344)
*Irina Vartanova,Niels Selling,Jennifer Viberg Johansson,Pontus Strimling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文测试消费者技术感知属性是否能预测其普及率。基于2022年美国成人调查（n=678）对65项技术的六维属性评分，以及两个前沿大语言模型（Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5.5）的相同评分，使用带符号约束的惩罚回归进行预测。结果显示属性模型相比"上市年数"基线在平均绝对误差上降低17%（人类评分），大模型表现更优（Opus 4.7最佳）。在2022-2025短期窗口内属性不优于无变化基线。论文附带2025-2026年推出产品的2027年普及率预测示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术采用研究（如UTAUT2模型）多关注个体层面的采纳意愿，但缺乏对宏观技术普及率的量化预测验证。本文旨在检验感知属性是否以及能在多大程度上预测技术的实际拥有率，并探索大语言模型是否可以作为人类评分的替代来源。

Method: 采用2022年Prolific平台对美国成年人的调查（n=678），让受访者对65项消费技术在六个属性维度上评分。同时向两个大语言模型（Anthropic Claude Opus 4.7和OpenAI GPT-5.5）elicitation相同评分。以拥有率为因变量，对UTAUT2中的四个接受度属性加上年龄对数协变量进行带符号约束的惩罚回归，采用留一技术法交叉验证评估。对比的基线模型包括"上市年数"和无变化基线。

Result: 在留一技术法评估下，属性模型相比"上市年数"基线将平均绝对误差降低17%（人类评分），大语言模型评分的表现更好，其中Claude Opus 4.7效果最佳。但在2022至2025的短期窗口内（拥有率变化甚微），属性模型未能优于无变化基线，说明短期预测能力有限。

Conclusion: 感知属性对于预测消费技术的中长期普及率有一定价值，且大语言模型可作为人类评分的有效替代。但方法存在局限：模型评分可能反映的是对技术的已有知识而非独立属性推理；短期预测效果不佳。尽管如此，该方法具备部署价值，论文给出了2025-2026年推出产品在2027年普及率的预测示例。

Abstract: We test whether the perceived attributes of a consumer technology predict how widely it is owned. In a 2022 Prolific survey of US adults (n = 678), respondents rated 65 consumer technologies on six attributes. We then elicited the same ratings from two frontier language models, Anthropic Claude Opus 4.7 and OpenAI GPT-5.5. We regress ownership prevalence on four UTAUT2 acceptance attributes plus a log-age covariate with a sign-constrained penalized regression and evaluate it by holding out one technology at a time. The attribute model improves on a baseline of years-since-launch: mean absolute error falls by 17% with the human ratings, and by more with either model, most with Opus 4.7. Over the short 2022-to-2025 window, where ownership moved little, the same attributes do not improve on a no-change baseline. We set out the limitations of the approach, including the possibility that language-model ratings reflect prior knowledge of these technologies rather than independent attribute reasoning. We include a deployment illustration: 2027 ownership predictions for eleven products launched in 2025 and 2026.

</details>


### [114] [New Terms, New Toxicity: Consensus-based Chinese Neologism Toxicity Detection via Search-Augmented LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12361)
*Shiyao Cui,QingLin Zhang,Di Wang,Yida Lu,Zhexin Zhang,Jinhua Gao,Jinglin Yang,Min He,Han Qiu,Minlie Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究通过新词表达的隐式毒性检测问题，提出毒性新词分类体系并构建跨风险类别词典，同时引入SeTox搜索增强框架，使静态大语言模型能够利用实时网络上下文来判断新词的毒性，实验表明3B规模模型即能超越更大规模的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新词（新造词）在形式或意义上不断涌现，可成为毒性表达的新载体（如"country girl"被用作诋毁女权主义的污名化标签）。这类毒性新词表面看似良性，但在公众共识中已演变为有毒用法，对现有内容审核系统构成挑战，且这一问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 本文方法包括三个部分：（1）提出一个捕捉毒性新词起源和共识验证标准的分类体系（taxonomy）；（2）构建覆盖多种广泛关注风险类别的毒性新词词典；（3）提出SeTox框架，一种搜索增强方法，使静态大语言模型能够融入实时网络上下文来检测新词毒性。

Result: 实验表明，SeTox即使使用3B参数规模的小型模型，其性能也超过了近期大规模模型的表现，证明了该框架在融入真实世界知识用于毒性新词检测方面具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: SeTox框架验证了搜索增强策略的有效性：通过引入实时网络上下文，可以将公众共识对毒性判断的语义注入静态语言模型，从而在不依赖更大模型参数量的情况下实现更准确的毒性新词检测。论文同时声明包含可能令部分读者不适的冒犯性内容。

Abstract: Neologisms, emerging terms in meaning or form, can serve as new vehicles for toxic expression, like "country girl" as a stigmatizing label targeting feminism. Such toxic neologisms appear benign but have evolved into toxic usage in public consensus, posing challenges to moderation systems and remaining underexplored. In this paper, we investigate how to detect implicit toxicity expressed via neologisms. We first propose a taxonomy that captures the origins and consensus-verification criteria of toxic neologisms, followed by the construction of a lexicon spanning widely observed risk categories. To capture toxicity grounded in public consensus, we introduce SeTox, a search-augmented framework that enables static large language models (LLMs) to incorporate real-time web context for neologism toxicity detection. Experiments show that SeTox, even with 3B-scale models, outperforms recent large-scale models, demonstrating its scalability to incorporate real-world knowledge for toxic neologism detection. Disclaimer: this paper has offensive contents that may be disturbing to some readers.

</details>


### [115] [Are you Talking Logic to Me? Assessing Language Models Syllogistic Reasoning Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12374)
*Hanna Abi Akl,Fabien Gandon,Catherine Faron,Pierre Monnin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了不同知识表示(KR)形式化符号对三段论推理任务的影响，通过扩展FOLIO和P-FOLIO数据集，在小型语言模型上进行有监督微调和零样本实验，发现选择合适的输入符号能在与自然语言性能相当的同时实现更快的推理速度。此外提出了SEF三段论分类方法用于丰富零样本提示，并开源了首个自动生成KR符号三段论及其SEF类别的Python库CLGC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在逻辑任务（如三段论推理）上表现不佳，而知识表示(KR)形式对模型理解和解决问题有重要影响。作者希望系统研究不同形式化KR符号如何影响小型语言模型的三段论推理能力，以寻找能兼顾性能与效率的输入表示方式。

Method: 1) 扩展FOLIO和P-FOLIO数据集，引入多种KR形式化符号表示三段论；2) 在小型语言模型上分别进行SFT和Zero-Shot实验，对比KR符号与自然语言的推理性能及推理速度；3) 提出SEF（Syllogistic Elicitation Framework）三段论分类方法，用于在Zero-Shot提示中注入逻辑定义；4) 开发并开源CLGC（Common Logic Grammar Construction）Python库，实现KR符号三段论的自动生成与SEF类别定义。

Result: 实验表明，输入符号的选择可使小型语言模型在推理性能上与使用自然语言时相当，同时推理速度更快；使用SEF分类增强的Zero-Shot提示能够进一步提升小型模型的推理能力。CLGC框架作为首个支持自动生成KR符号三段论并定义SEF类别的Python库被开源。

Conclusion: 知识表示符号的选择对语言模型的三段论推理具有显著影响，KR符号能够在保持推理性能的同时加速推理过程，SEF分类方法可有效增强小模型的逻辑推理；该工作为逻辑推理领域的输入表示优化提供了新的方向和开源工具支持。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) struggle with logical tasks like reasoning on syllogisms. It has been shown that Knowledge Representation (KR) plays a crucial role in expressing input information to help models solve tasks. This observation motivates our study of the impact of different formal KR notations on syllogistic reasoning by extending the FOLIO and P-FOLIO datasets. Our experiments on Small Language Models (SLMs) in Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Zero-Shot (ZS) settings show that the choice of input notation can yield performances competitive with natural language while enabling faster inference.
