Experiments on 16 real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an average F1 score of 85.78 and a mean AUC of 93.30, outperforming eight DST-based baselines and three gradient boosting methods. Ablation analysis confirms the contribution of each component we proposed. The framework offers an effective approach for adaptive evidence fusion in multi-source decision making.

</details>


### [314] [SkillEvo: Self-Renewing Evolution Gradients from Multi-Turn Interaction Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13120)
*Qianxi Yan,Chunrong Chen,Jiuzhou Zhao,Min Zhang,Yongzhou Xu,Xiaochuan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillEvo框架，解决Agent技能演化停滞的问题。核心创新在于将多轮用户模拟从评估端点转变为反馈生成器，并用独立治理层替代标量门控以主动修复退化，使技能在每次修订中持续产生可信反馈梯度。在6类云服务、9个生产技能和98个参考文件上，SkillEvo比自反思演化高23.0分、比单轮QA驱动演化高15.4分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent技能要么手工编写要么单次LLM生成，缺乏从交互失败中学习的闭环。虽然近期工作尝试闭环，但仅从单轮问答评估提取反馈，导致第一轮修补后演化梯度迅速衰减、多轮才暴露的缺陷无法被发现、演化陷入停滞。同时现有治理依赖端到端标量评分门控，只能拒绝退化候选而无法定位和修复结构性问题。作者认为限制持续演化的关键不是编辑能力或迭代次数，而是评估反馈能否持续提供可信的演化梯度。

Method: SkillEvo包含两个核心组件：1) 可信反馈生成器——将多轮用户模拟从评估端点转变为反馈生成器，通过追问逐层暴露缺陷，使每轮修订既消费反馈又产生新反馈，形成持续的演化梯度；2) 可控治理层——用独立治理层替代被动标量门控，主动修复事实性退化和结构性膨胀，防止梯度随退化累积而漂移，约束演化方向。

Result: 在6类云服务、9个生产技能和98个技能参考文件上，SkillEvo比基于自反射的演化方法高23.0分，比基于单轮QA驱动的演化方法高15.4分。

Conclusion: SkillEvo验证了持续技能演化的关键不在编辑能力或迭代数量，而在于评估反馈能否持续提供可信的演化梯度，以及治理机制能否主动约束演化方向而非被动拒绝。多轮用户模拟作为反馈生成器与独立治理层的结合，有效突破了单轮QA驱动的演化瓶颈。

Abstract: Agent Skills are today either hand-authored or produced in a single LLM generation pass, and consequently possess no closed loop through which they might improve from the interaction failures they actually cause. Recent work does close this loop, but derives its feedback from single-turn question-answering evaluation. The consequence is a sharp asymmetry: once the first round has patched the gaps that a single exchange can reveal, the evolution gradient decays, the defects that surface only across multiple turns remain invisible, and evolution stalls. Governance in these systems is likewise driven by an end-to-end verification score, a scalar gate that can reject a degraded candidate but can neither localize nor repair its structural cause. We argue that the binding constraint on sustained skill evolution is neither editing capability nor the number of iterations, but whether the evaluation feedback keeps supplying trustworthy evolution gradients. We introduce SkillEvo, in which trustworthy feedback generates the gradient and controllable governance constrains its direction. The first component recasts multi-turn user simulation from an evaluation endpoint into a feedback generator: follow-up questions expose defects layer by layer, so that every round of revision both consumes feedback and produces new feedback. The second replaces the passive rejection of a scalar gate with an independent governance layer that actively repairs factual degradation and structural bloat, preventing the gradient from drifting as degradation accumulates. Across six categories of cloud services, 9 production Skills, and 98 skill-reference files, SkillEvo surpasses self-reflection-based evolution by 23.0 points and single- turn-QA-driven evolution by 15.4 points.

</details>


### [315] [Numeracy in Large Language Models: Fundamental Limitations and Paths to Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13129)
*Aoxin Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统调研了大型语言模型在基础数值理解上的能力缺陷，提出数值锚定框架（NGF），将数感分解为表征锚定（RG）和程序锚定（PG），并据此组织梳理相关诊断基准、失败模式、结构解释与缓解策略，最后对多个前沿模型家族进行协调评估并提出部署建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在数学推理基准上表现优异，但在幅度比较、大整数运算、分数和科学计数法等基础数值任务上仍不可靠。基础数值理解应被视为独立于高级数学推理的能力，现有研究缺乏统一的组织框架来理解这一问题的成因与解决方案。

Method: 提出NGF框架将数值能力分解为表征锚定（RG：数值形式到值、幅度和等价表示的映射）与程序锚定（PG：按数学定义执行算术运算）。基于该框架组织近期诊断基准与失败模式，分析tokenization、位置编码、嵌入几何和预训练数据分布等结构性因素。随后对三个前沿模型家族在Number Cookbook、NumericBench和GSM-Symbolic上进行协调评估，比较原子、上下文和推理辅助三种数值能力层次。

Result: 从零训练的架构干预（如数字感知tokenization和Abacus Embeddings）可提升数值能力，但对预训练模型用户不可用；监督微调、推理脚手架和外部工具是更实际的缓解手段。协调评估揭示了不同模型家族在原子、上下文和推理辅助数值任务上的差异化表现。

Conclusion: 基础数值理解是LLM中独立于高级推理的关键能力维度；NGF为诊断与改进提供了统一框架；面向实际部署，建议结合微调、推理策略和外部工具来增强基础模型的数值可靠性，并指出值得继续研究的方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong results on mathematical reasoning benchmarks yet remain unreliable on elementary numerical tasks, including magnitude comparison, large-integer arithmetic, fractions, and scientific notation. This survey examines basic numerical understanding as a capability distinct from high-level mathematical reasoning. We propose the Numerical Grounding Framework (NGF), which decomposes numeracy into Representational Grounding (RG), mapping numeral forms to value, magnitude, and equivalent representations, and Procedural Grounding (PG), executing arithmetic operations in accordance with their mathematical definitions. Using NGF, we organize recent diagnostic benchmarks, failure modes, structural explanations, and mitigation strategies. We review evidence concerning tokenization, positional encoding, embedding geometry, and pretraining-data distribution. We also apply NGF in a coordinated evaluation of three frontier model families across Number Cookbook, NumericBench, and GSM-Symbolic, comparing atomic, contextual, and reasoning-assisted numeracy. Architectural interventions such as digit-aware tokenization and Abacus Embeddings can improve models trained from scratch but are generally unavailable to users of pretrained systems, for whom supervised fine-tuning, reasoning scaffolds, and external tools are more practical. We conclude with deployment recommendations and research directions for more reliable numerical behavior in foundation models.

</details>


### [316] [Rethinking Normalization Placement for LLMs: Post-Norm under Curriculum Depth Growing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13156)
*Sheng Ren,Yadong Wang,Naiqiang Tan,Jiangang Kong,Jun Fang,Rui Liu,Jun Wang,Kai Chen,Lipeng Liang,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer中归一化位置（pre-norm vs post-norm）与课程式深度增长（curriculum depth growth）之间的交互。在Qwen3-8B教师蒸馏九层学生的受控实验中，联合训练下二者几乎无差异（验证CE差0.0004），而在课程增长下post-norm比pre-norm优越0.0328（大一个数量级）。对照实验排除了计算量作为唯一解释，边界诊断表明post-norm保持了稳定的残差尺度，而pre-norm出现结构token尺度漂移。作者建议将归一化位置与训练课程视为耦合的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Transformer默认采用pre-norm（前置归一化）以利于全深度模型的联合优化，但作者质疑：当深度通过课程式方式引入（逐块追加）时，该偏好是否仍然成立。由于课程增长中每个新追加的块接收已训练前缀产生的边界表示，归一化位置对"前向条件化"（forward conditioning）至关重要，因此值得考察归一化位置与训练课程是否存在交互作用。

Method: 在受控蒸馏框架下（Qwen3-8B教师、九层学生），系统对比pre-norm与post-norm在两种训练范式下的表现：(1) 联合训练（joint training）；(2) 课程式深度增长（curriculum growth）。通过post-joint对照控制学生活跃层token的计算量，排除计算量差异；通过单块与冻结对照（single-block和freeze controls）将排名变化定位到块追加环节；并通过边界诊断（residual scale、结构token尺度漂移、identity-mapping检测）分析归一化位置对边界表示的影响。

Result: 联合训练下pre-norm与post-norm不可区分（验证CE仅差0.0004），但课程增长下post-norm领先0.0328，差距大一个数量级。post-joint对照（匹配学生活跃层token）仍差于post-grow，排除计算量因素。排名在课程过程中发生交叉：post-norm在块追加后占据优势。单块与冻结对照表明排名变化源于块追加行为本身而非浅层块质量或重训练效应。边界诊断显示post-norm保持稳定残差尺度，pre-norm则出现结构token尺度漂移，且pre-grow最终块近似为恒等映射。

Conclusion: 归一化位置与训练课程在深度蒸馏设置中是耦合的设计选择：尽管pre-norm在联合训练中是标准偏好，但在课程式深度增长下post-norm显著更优。这一现象与块追加后的边界尺度条件化（boundary-scale conditioning）一致。作者据此建议将归一化位置与训练课程作为联合设计维度来考虑。

Abstract: Pre-norm is the standard normalization placement in modern Transformers because it facilitates joint optimization of full-depth models. We ask whether this preference persists when depth is introduced through a curriculum. In curriculum depth growth, each appended block receives the boundary representation produced by a trained prefix, making normalization placement relevant to forward conditioning. We therefore test whether placement and training curriculum interact. In a controlled distillation study with a Qwen3-8B teacher and a nine-layer student, pre-norm and post-norm are indistinguishable under joint training, differing by $0.0004$ validation CE, while post-norm improves over pre-norm by $0.0328$ under curriculum growth, an order of magnitude larger. A post-joint control matched by student active-layer tokens remains worse than post-grow, which rules out compute as the sole explanation. The ranking crosses over during the curriculum: post-norm takes the lead once blocks are appended. Single-block and freeze controls localize the ranking change to block appending rather than shallow-block quality or retraining. Boundary diagnostics associate post-norm with stable residual scales and pre-norm with structural-token scale drift; on a fixed batch, the final pre-grow block is also nearly identity-mapped. Together with the phase-wise crossover, these observations are consistent with boundary-scale conditioning after new blocks are appended. The results motivate treating normalization placement and training curriculum as coupled design choices in this distillation setting.

</details>


### [317] [SkillShapley: Boundary-Adaptive Shapley Valuation for Skill Step Attribution in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13173)
*Chang Liu,Yuqi Zhang,Yiman Zhong,Boyi Liu,Hengjun Wang,Shuyue Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillShapley，一个基于Shapley值的智能体技能步骤级归因框架，用于量化智能体技能中每个步骤对整体任务表现的贡献。该框架利用离散化基准奖励和步骤间加性交互两个经验观察，分两阶段识别信息丰富的联合区域并自适应采样新联合以获取可复用的边缘证据，实验证明其能高效识别高/低价值步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体技能主要通过人工编写或执行轨迹创建，缺乏对技能中每个步骤在特定任务上贡献度的量化理解，即如何衡量技能内单步贡献这一问题尚未解决。

Method: SkillShapley将技能步骤归因建模为基于Shapley值的贡献估计问题，基于两个关键经验洞察（离散化基准奖励导致的性能陡峭下降、步骤间主要为加性而非协同交互），分两阶段实施：首先识别信息丰富的联合区域，然后自适应采样能产生可复用边缘证据的新联合。

Result: 在SkillsBench基准上的实验表明，SkillShapley能够有效且高效地识别高价值或低价值的技能步骤，并为智能体技能创建提供多项关键启示。

Conclusion: SkillShapley为智能体技能中步骤级贡献量化提供了可行且高效的解决方案，其归因结果可指导智能体技能的创建与优化，填补了技能步骤归因领域的空白。

Abstract: Agent skills are crucial external instructions that enable language agents to execute long procedural tasks such as coding or document processing. Existing agent skills are primarily created through human manual crafting or agent execution traces, with limited understanding of how each step contributes to overall skill performance on specific tasks; i.e., there remains an open problem in quantifying the contribution of individual steps within an agent skill. To address this issue, we first model skill-step attribution as a Shapley value-based contribution estimation problem, and then propose SkillShapley, a step-level attribution framework for agent skills. Notably, SkillShapley operates in two phases, motivated by key empirical insights, i.e., discretized benchmark rewards that create sharp performance cliffs, and step interactions that are largely additive rather than synergistic. Specifically, it first identifies informative coalitional regions, and then adaptively samples new coalitions that can yield reusable marginal evidence. Experiments on skills from the widely adopted SkillsBench demonstrate that our SkillShapley can effectively and efficiently identify high- or low-value skill steps, providing several key takeaways for agent skill creation.

</details>


### [318] [Teach the Magnitude, Not the Direction: Verifier-Bounded Credit Assignment for Multi-Turn Multi-step LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13179)
*Zechuan Wang,Siyuan Lu,Hongxuan Zhang,Linjian Mo,Chenyi Zhuang,Leilei Gan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CrEST是一个层次化信用分配框架，通过回合级分段验证优势解决回合间信号稀释，并结合熵门控自教师调制细化回合内token贡献，在保留验证器有界性能上界的同时引入密集token级监督，在BFCL V3和WildToolBench上超越RL和蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮工具使用代理的强化学习（RLVR）受限于验证器提供的稀疏轨迹级奖励，无法区分不同回合的异质结果；而在线蒸馏虽提供密集token级监督但受限于教师性能或出现梯度集中坍塌。现有方法无法同时兼顾验证器有界上界与密集信用分配。

Method: CrEST采用两层信用解析：(1) 回合级分段验证优势（turn-segmented verified advantages）缓解回合间信号稀释；(2) 熵门控自教师调制（entropy-gated self-teacher modulation）细化回合内token贡献。教师从决定更新方向降级为调节更新幅度，从而解锁密集信用分配而不牺牲验证器有界上界。

Result: 在BFCL V3和WildToolBench两个基准上，跨两个模型规模，CrEST一致优于RL和蒸馏基线；在长轨迹和严格会话级指标上收益最大。

Conclusion: 论文展示了教师在策略优化中的角色可以从决定更新方向简化为调节更新幅度，在保留验证器有界性能上界的前提下实现密集信用分配，为多轮工具使用代理的强化学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) offers a verifier-bounded performance ceiling for training multi-turn tool-use agents, yet its trajectory-level credit assignment conflates heterogeneous per-turn outcomes into a single reward signal. On-policy distillation provides dense per-token supervision but is either teacher-bounded or prone to gradient concentration collapse. We introduce $\textbf{CrEST}$, a hierarchical credit assignment framework that retains RL's verifier-bounded ceiling while incorporating dense token-level signals from a privileged self-teacher. $\textbf{CrEST}$ resolves credit at two levels: turn-segmented verified advantages address inter-turn dilution, while entropy-gated self-teacher modulation refines intra-turn token contributions. Experiments on BFCL V3 and WildToolBench show that $\textbf{CrEST}$ consistently outperforms both RL and distillation baselines across two model scales, with the largest gains on long-trajectory and strict session-level metrics. Our work demonstrates that the teacher's role in policy optimization can be reduced from determining update directions to modulating update magnitudes, unlocking dense credit assignment without sacrificing the verifier-bounded ceiling.

</details>


### [319] [TsuGO: Probing Search Efficiency in LLM Reasoning via Go Life-and-Death Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13221)
*Shunwen Bai,Ziping Ma,Chaoyang Zhang,Yarong Wang,Jiale Liu,Zhen Qin,Qingpei Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TsuGO，一个基于围棋死活题的过程级推理基准，用于评估LLM推理中的搜索效率（候选生成、分支比较、回溯等搜索组织能力），通过约束解空间将领域知识与搜索组织解耦，实验表明当前LLM在搜索组织上仍远未达到稳定水平，长CoT或高Token效率并不等于更好的搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理评估方法主要关注最终答案准确率或链式思维（CoT）的一致性与冗余性，未能捕捉模型如何规划推理路径、分配推理资源（即搜索组织）。且大多数基准任务的目标单一、可通过推导和工具使用等静态能力求解，导致搜索组织这一维度未被测量。

Method: TsuGO利用围棋死活题构建过程级推理基准：围棋死活题具有封闭可验证的解空间和内在对抗性结构，迫使候选生成、响应检验、分支比较和回溯成为推理的必要组成部分。通过约束解空间将领域知识与搜索组织解耦，将CoT解析为结构化搜索树，并报告Search Efficiency、Token Efficiency及其他诊断指标与可视化。

Result: 实验发现：当前LLM在死活题求解上远不稳定；较强的模型通过更早找到正确候选并在有效分支上持续投入而成功，但大多数模型的行为更接近无引导的搜索算法，而非神经引导的KataGo。更长的CoT或更高的Token效率并不必然意味着更好的搜索表现。

Conclusion: 搜索组织和推理资源分配是当前LLM推理评估中缺失的关键维度，TsuGO可作为衡量该维度的过程级基准工具，推动LLM推理评估从结果导向走向过程导向。

Abstract: The evaluation of LLM reasoning is moving from final-answer accuracy to process-level assessment, yet existing methods still fail to capture how models plan reasoning paths and allocate reasoning resources--that is, how they organize search. Prior process-level methods focus on the coherence and redundancy of chain-of-thought (CoT), and most benchmark tasks have a single objective solvable by static capabilities such as derivation and tool use, leaving search organization unmeasured. We introduce TsuGO, a process-level reasoning benchmark for evaluating Search Efficiency in LLM reasoning through Go life-and-death problems. These problems provide closed and verifiable solution spaces with an inherent adversarial structure, making candidate generation, response checking, branch comparison, and backtracking necessary parts of reasoning rather than incidental trace patterns. By constraining the solution space, TsuGO disentangles domain knowledge from search organization, parses CoT into a structured search tree, and reports Search Efficiency together with Token Efficiency and other diagnostic metrics and visualizations. Experiments show that current LLMs remain far from stable tsumego solving: stronger models succeed by finding the correct candidate earlier and sustaining effort on productive branches, but most models still behave much closer to unguided search algorithms than to neural-guided KataGo. Longer CoT or higher Token Efficiency does not necessarily imply better search. Our results identify search organization and reasoning-resource allocation as missing dimensions in LLM reasoning evaluation.

</details>


### [320] [Capability Sheaves for Compositional Agent-Harness Repair: Controlled Quotients and a Real-Repository Stress Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13228)
*Saveliy Batruin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用有限"能力层"（capability sheaf）建模多智能体系统在共享状态上的不一致失败，将接受判定转化为精确CSP问题，并用线性化相对上同调类作为诊断特征。受控实验验证了商化隐变量消除无关状态的效果，但真实SWE-bench Multilingual基准测试显示，上同调方法在补丁选择上没有显著优于非上同调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体harness（检索、路由、状态、溯源、验证等组件组合）中，各局部组件在各自任务上表现良好，但可能对共享状态产生分歧，导致系统级失败。作者希望用数学结构（层与上同调）系统化刻画这种失败模式，并提供可计算的诊断与搜索工具。

Method: 1) 将多智能体不一致性建模为有限"能力层"：stalk编码类型化的行为签名，限制映射保留共享字段，可接受的运行即有用的整体截面。2) 用精确有限CSP定义接受性，同时用线性化的相对上同调类作为诊断和搜索特征。3) 受控实验引入隐藏内部中介变量（nuisance），通过商掉其coboundary减少候选预算。4) 在SWE-bench Multilingual的PatchFuseBench上测试：先构造pool-level层（因coker D中[b-Dx]=[b]退化为常值），再构造candidate-indexed修复版本。

Result: 受控实验：商化coboundary将候选预算从2000降至1000，对齐隐状态后差距消失，精确CSP与商化结果一致，证明方法是"对过时代表元不变性"而非优于精确推理。真实测试：candidate-indexed版本在848/875候选上非平凡，120/160问题上可变；解决118个issue vs 116个非上同调基线（p=0.75不显著）；弃权门达127/160，与强锚点持平，仅超匹配基线1个issue（p=1.0）。发现门未通过，确证性split保持密封。

Conclusion: 研究支持受控实验中的不变性机制和可辨识性修正的有效性，但未能在真实世界数据集上证明上同调方法的实际优势。作者诚实报告了负面结果：发现门失败，真实场景中的上同调收益不具统计显著性。

Abstract: Agent harnesses combine retrieval, routing, state, provenance, and verification, but locally successful components may disagree on shared state. We model this failure with a finite \emph{capability sheaf}: stalks encode typed behavior signatures, restriction maps retain shared fields, and accepted runs are useful global sections. An exact finite constraint-satisfaction problem (CSP) defines acceptance, while a linearized relative cohomology class provides a diagnostic and search feature.
  A controlled experiment over 20 task clusters introduces hidden interior mediators whose raw states are nuisance variables. Quotienting their coboundaries reduces the candidate budget from 2,000 to 1,000 per cluster; aligning the hidden state removes the gap. Exact CSP matches the quotient, so the result demonstrates invariance to stale representatives, not superiority over exact reasoning.
  We then test the method on a discovery split from the SWE-bench Multilingual pool of PatchFuseBench: 160 issues from 20 repositories, 875 real candidate patches, 2,579 source-aware edit atoms, and 153 newly executed patches. A first pool-level construction is constant because $[b-Dx]=[b]$ in $\operatorname{coker}D$ and therefore cannot rank configurations. A candidate-indexed repair is nontrivial on 848/875 candidates and varies within 120/160 issues. It resolves 118 issues versus 116 for a matched noncohomological selector, but the difference is not supported across repositories (exact sign-flip $p=0.75$). A leave-one-repository-out abstention gate reaches 127/160, tying the strong anchor and exceeding its matched gate by one issue ($p=1.0$). The discovery gate therefore fails and the confirmatory split remains sealed. The study supports the controlled invariance mechanism and an identifiability correction, but not a real-world cohomological advantage.

</details>


### [321] [vToken: Token-Level Virtualization for Reclaimable KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13263)
*Yuanhang Gao,Xiangrui Yang,Yuanfeng Chen,Hongjia Chen,Qianru Lv,Wenfei Wu,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: vToken提出了一种轻量级的token级虚拟化层，通过token表间接寻址将逻辑token存活性与物理block布局解耦，在保留PagedAttention内核和CUDA Graph兼容性的前提下，解决了KV驱逐算法按token粒度操作与block级内存管理之间的不匹配问题，从而消除intra-block碎片并显著提升内存利用率。

本内容已通过合规性审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理服务面临严重的KV cache内存瓶颈。现有PagedAttention使用固定大小内存块来减少分配器级碎片，但KV驱逐算法以token粒度工作，与block级管理粒度不匹配，导致intra-block碎片，大量已分配的KV内存无法回收，浪费严重。

Method: vToken引入一个轻量级token级虚拟化层：通过token-table间接寻址维护稳定的逻辑token视图，将逻辑token存活性与物理block放置解耦；通过异步repacking将活跃token重新打包以实现物理空间的回收。设计保持PagedAttention内核和CUDA Graph兼容，并在vLLM中实现。

Result: 在H2O、Random和Scissorhands策略及多种模型上的评估显示：与Naive-Evict基线相比，vToken将每请求保留的KV block数量减少27.2%-72.3%，SLA约束下吞吐量最高提升1.37倍；在约束active-KV预算下，最大可行并发度扩展至2倍，且策略集成代码量从500+行降至50行以下。

Conclusion: vToken通过Token级虚拟化有效解决了KV驱逐粒度与block管理粒度的失配问题，显著降低内存碎片、提升吞吐和并发能力，同时以极小的集成成本保持对现有PagedAttention基础设施的兼容性。

Abstract: Large language model serving faces a critical memory bottleneck: the KV cache grows with sequence length and batch size. PagedAttention uses fixed-size memory blocks to reduce allocator-level fragmentation, but recent KV eviction algorithms operate at a token granularity finer than block-level management. This mismatch causes intra-block fragmentation, leaving a large fraction of allocated KV memory unreclaimable. We present vToken, a lightweight token-level virtualization layer that decouples logical token liveness from physical block placement. vToken maintains a stable logical token view through token-table indirection and realizes physical reclamation by repacking live tokens asynchronously. The design preserves PagedAttention kernels and CUDA Graph compatibility. We implement vToken in vLLM and evaluate it with H2O, Random, and Scissorhands across models. Compared with a paired Naive-Evict baseline, vToken reduces retained KV blocks per request by 27.2\%--72.3\% and improves SLA-constrained throughput by up to 1.37$\times$. Under a constrained active-KV budget, it extends the maximum feasible concurrency by up to 2$\times$, while reducing the per-policy integration footprint from 500+ lines to under 50.

</details>


### [322] [Sovereign by necessity? Frontier AI export controls, cyber security, and the limits of national AI capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13272)
*Alan Woodward,Andrew Rogoyski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨前沿人工智能模型的地缘政治控制与网络防御之间的关系。少数公司垄断最先进AI模型，其所在国政府可决定其他国家能否使用这些系统。以2026年6月美国要求领先开发者对发布先进模型实行许可制度为例，说明AI访问权已成为可被随时撤销的国家网络安全资产。文章分析前沿模型如何改变网络攻防经济学，并提出分层战略：协商访问保障、推理层主权、开放权重模型对冲、区域能力整合、人才培育和基础网络韧性投资。指出开放权重对冲比普遍认知更强大但也更暴露于政治风险，近期风险更多在于模型的部署与遏制而非表面性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI模型高度集中于少数国家和公司，其使用权正被用作地缘政治工具。鉴于前沿模型显著改变网络攻防双方的成本结构和能力对比，作者认为需要探讨各国（尤其是中小国家）在AI技术日益集中的背景下，如何在网络防御领域维护真正意义上的主权。

Method: 基于案例分析（美国2026年的许可证要求及模型全球撤回事件、AI自主网络间谍活动首次记录案例）和实证证据（训练成本数据、算力集中度、国家AI扶持计划），结合地缘政治与国际关系理论，对前沿AI的访问控制与国家网络防御主权进行多层面分析，并提出分层战略框架。

Result: 文章论证了访问前沿AI正成为国家网络防御的组成部分，且该访问权具有可撤销性；对中小国家而言，建立完全的主权能力仅部分可行；提出包含协商访问保障、推理层主权、开放权重对冲、区域能力共享、人才培育和基础网络韧性投资在内的分层应对策略。同时揭示开放权重模型作为对冲手段既比预期更有能力，也更易受政治干预。

Conclusion: 结论认为，短期内网络安全的决定性风险不在于模型的表面性能，而在于强大模型如何被部署和遏制。各国需要务实地在追求技术主权与接受受控访问之间寻找平衡，并优先强化部署治理和基础网络防御韧性。

Abstract: A small number of firms based in two states produce the most capable frontier AI models. The governments of those states have shown both the legal power and the political will to decide which other countries may use these systems. In June 2026 the United States required a leading developer to obtain licences before releasing its most advanced models to any foreign person, including foreign nationals resident in the United States. The affected models were withdrawn worldwide at short notice, partly because the restriction proved impractical to administer. This followed within months of the first documented case of a largely autonomous, AI-run cyber espionage campaign, and coincided with mounting evidence that frontier models alter the economics of both cyber attack and cyber defence. This article examines how these two developments interact, and situates them within the unusual market dynamics now driving large-scale AI development. It argues that access to frontier AI is becoming part of national cyber defence, that such access can be revoked, and that the obvious remedy of sovereign capability remains only partly feasible for all but a handful of states. Drawing on evidence about training costs, the concentration of computing power and the support offered by national AI programmes, it asks what sovereignty can realistically mean for small and middle powers, and for large powers as well. The article proposes a layered strategy: negotiated access guarantees, sovereignty at the level of inference, hedging with open-weight models, pooled regional capability, sustained talent development and continued investment in basic cyber resilience. The open-weight hedge proves at once more capable and more politically exposed than is commonly assumed. Much of the near-term risk lies in how capable models are deployed and contained rather than in their apparent performance.

</details>


### [323] [Towards Context-Aware Clinical Motion Understanding in Daily Living at Home: Freezing of Gait Detection with Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13283)
*Vayalet Stefanova,Diwas Lamsal,Margot Genbrugge,Maxim Yudayev,Christian Schlenstedt,Moran Gilat,Bart Vanrumste,Benjamin Filtjens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探索利用自发视视频作为穿戴传感器（IMU）的补充上下文信息，用于帕金森病患者的冻结步态（FOG）检测。结果显示IMU-based TCN在事件检测上优于预训练的自我中心视频特征，但自我中心视觉仍提供了独立于IMU的FOG相关信息，可增强日常生活中的临床运动理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常活动中的运动理解需要超越运动学的上下文信息，因为相似的惯性模式可能反映不同意图或病理状态。FOG检测受日常情境因素强烈影响，而自发视视频提供的任务相关上下文有望帮助区分这些情况，弥补纯IMU感知的不足。

Method: 采集13名帕金森病参与者在家中的同步自发视视频、可穿戴IMU数据和专家标注的FOG标签。评估预训练的自发视视频基础模型（如V-JEPA2）与时间序列基础模型的冻结特征，同时训练一个从头开始的基于IMU的TCN模型，使用留一受试者（leave-one-subject-out）验证策略进行对比。

Result: 基于IMU的TCN达到最佳事件检测性能（F1=42.3，AUROC=83.0），优于V-JEPA2自发视视频特征（F1=32.6，AUROC=77.2）。自发视视频虽未超过IMU感知性能，但表现出高于随机水平的判别能力，且定性分析表明其可能捕获与IMU独立的FOG相关信息。

Conclusion: 结果表明预训练的自发视视频表示可以用于为基于穿戴传感器的临床运动理解补充上下文信息，尽管单独使用时性能不及IMU，但其独立且互补的信息价值支持多模态融合的未来方向。

Abstract: Understanding motion in daily living requires context beyond kinematics, because similar inertial patterns during activities of daily living (ADLs) can reflect intentional stopping, object interaction, or pathological movement impairment. Egocentric vision provides task-related context that may help disambiguate these cases. We investigate this challenge through freezing of gait (FOG) detection in Parkinson's disease (PD), a symptom strongly influenced by contextual factors during ADLs. Using synchronized egocentric video, wearable IMUs, and expert-annotated FOG labels collected from 13 PD participants in their homes, we evaluate frozen representations from pretrained ego-video and time-series foundation models, alongside an IMU-based TCN trained from scratch, under leave-one-subject-out evaluation. The IMU-based TCN achieved the strongest event-detection performance, reaching 42.3 F1 and 83.0 AUROC, compared with 32.6 F1 and 77.2 AUROC for V-JEPA2 ego-video features. Although ego-video alone did not outperform IMU-based sensing, it showed above-chance discrimination, and qualitative analyses suggest that egocentric vision may capture FOG-relevant information independent of IMUs. Together, these results support the use of pretrained ego-video representations to add contextual information to wearable-sensor-based clinical motion understanding in daily living.

</details>


### [324] [NAS-Driven Hardware Accelerator Exploration for Edge AI and Quantization Effects on the Pareto Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13293)
*Eleftherios Mylonas,Angelos Kouprizas,Michael Birbas,Alexios Birbas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段流水线，将硬件无关的NAS与后训练量化（PTQ）及可重构加速器上的进化式领域空间探索（DSE）相结合，解决边缘AI部署中架构精度、效率与硬件适配的联合优化问题。同时通过实证研究量化了INT4 PTQ对NAS-Bench-201帕累托空间的扰动，并发现FP32零样本替代模型在帕累托覆盖上优于专门训练的INT4替代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI部署需要同时满足精度、计算效率和硬件可部署性的神经架构，硬件感知NAS是解决途径。但现有将量化直接纳入NAS循环的方法增加了搜索复杂度并紧密耦合了架构与量化设计；而更简单的搜索后量化（PTQ）策略缺乏分析性关注——PTQ对NAS发现的帕累托结构的影响未被刻画，也没有将量化架构映射到可重构加速器并自动化硬件探索的框架。本文旨在解决这两个空白。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 前端：基于NAS-Bench-201的硬件无关帕累托排位替代模型（surrogate）；(2) 量化桥梁：带帕累托感知过滤和反馈控制的量化桥接模块；(3) 后端：在CGRA4ML上使用进化式领域空间探索（DSE）进行最优硬件映射。此外，对全部15,625个架构的地面真值数据使用形式化稳定性指标进行实证研究，刻画INT4 PTQ如何扰动NAS-Bench-201帕累托空间。

Result: 实证研究表明INT4 PTQ会扰动NAS-Bench-201的帕累托空间结构；关键发现是FP32零样本替代模型在帕累托空间覆盖上优于专门训练的INT4替代模型（在两种标准搜索策略下均成立）。

Conclusion: PTQ策略在硬件感知NAS中具有重要价值，其扰动效应可被有效刻画，且无需为量化专门训练替代模型——FP32零样本替代模型在帕累托覆盖上表现更优。三阶段流水线将架构搜索、量化映射与硬件探索解耦，降低了搜索复杂度。

Abstract: Edge AI deployment demands neural architectures that are simultaneously accurate, computationally efficient, and hardware-deployable - a challenge addressed by hardware-aware Neural Architecture Search (NAS). While recent works incorporate quantization directly into the NAS loop, these approaches expand search complexity and tightly couple architecture and quantization design. The simpler post-search quantization strategy has received little analytical attention: the effects of Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) on the NAS-discovered Pareto structure remain uncharacterised, and no framework combines quantized architecture mapping onto reconfigurable accelerators with automated hardware exploration. This paper addresses both gaps. First, a three-stage pipeline is proposed: a hardware-agnostic Pareto rank surrogate frontend on NAS-Bench-201, a quantization bridge with Pareto-aware filtering and feedback control, and an evolutionary Domain Space Exploration (DSE) backend on CGRA4ML for optimal hardware mapping. Second, an empirical study characterises how INT4 PTQ perturbs the NAS-Bench-201 Pareto space through formal stability metrics on ground-truth data for all 15,625 architectures, and demonstrates that an FP32 zero-shot surrogate outperforms a dedicated INT4-trained surrogate in Pareto space coverage across two standard search strategies.

</details>


### [325] [StateBridge: Training-free Hidden-state Alignment for Latent Communication in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13317)
*Yanwen Peng,Delvin Ce Zhang,Xi Wang,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StateBridge，一种无需训练的潜在通信方法，通过闭式正交变换将发送方最终层隐藏状态对齐到接收方输入空间，作为连续前缀前置到接收方输入中，在数学推理、代码生成和问答任务上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统通常通过文本（离散token）通信，但文本引入了离散瓶颈，将发送方连续隐藏状态转换为离散token会丢失信息。现有潜在通信方法要么逐层注入工作记忆（侵入性强），要么需要训练投影器（限制可移植性），因此需要一种无需训练且可移植的潜在通信方案。

Method: StateBridge通过闭式正交变换将发送方最终层隐藏状态对齐到接收方的输入空间。具体包括：(1) 闭式正交变换实现空间对齐；(2) 轻量级范数校准确保与预训练输入分布兼容；(3) 词汇锚定进一步保证语义一致性。对齐后的状态作为连续前缀前置到接收方智能体的输入中，整个过程无需训练。

Result: 在两个模型家族的四个模型上评估了数学推理、代码生成和问答任务，StateBridge在26个模型-任务组合中的22个上取得最佳或并列最佳成绩，始终优于最强基线。

Conclusion: StateBridge证明了无需训练的闭式正交变换可以有效实现跨智能体潜在状态对齐，避免文本离散瓶颈的同时保持模型可移植性，为多智能体系统的潜在通信提供了轻量级且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model based multi-agent systems usually communicate in text, i.e., using discrete tokens. However, text introduces a discrete bottleneck. Converting the sender's continuous hidden states into discrete tokens discards information that token identities alone cannot capture. Recent work proposes latent communication as an alternative, where agents transmit hidden representations directly without converting them to text. However, existing latent methods either inject working memory layer by layer across the transformers, or require trained projectors that limit portability. We propose StateBridge, a training-free latent communication approach that aligns the sender's final-layer hidden states to the receiver's input space via a closed-form orthogonal transformation. Lightweight norm calibration and vocabulary anchoring ensure compatibility with the pretrained input distribution. The aligned states are prepended to the input of the receiver agent as a continuous prefix. We evaluate StateBridge on math reasoning, code generation, and question answering with four models from two families. StateBridge achieves the best or tied-best score on 22 out of 26 model-task pairs, consistently outperforming the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [326] [LLM-Guided Graph Generation for Structure-Based Local Improvement Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13333)
*Hai Xia,Vaidyanathan Peruvemba Ramaswamy,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个与问题无关的自动流水线，利用LLM的语义指导生成图生成器，将任意MiniZinc格式的约束优化问题实例映射为统一的加权图（节点表示决策变量、边表示约束关系），并基于该图构建结构感知的局部改进框架SLIM用于变量选择，同时提取统一的图特征进行配置选择。在20个MiniZinc竞赛问题上，算法选择平均问题加权胜率39.5%，远超最佳单一配置的19.3%，加入配置和特征消融后进一步提升至44.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型邻域搜索通常随机选择决策变量子集进行迭代优化，但针对不同问题需要人工设计基于领域结构特征的变量选择策略。作者希望构建一个无需领域知识、对所有MiniZinc格式问题通用的自动化流水线，以自动进行结构提取和特征提取，避免人工设计。

Method: 通过语义指南提示LLM生成一个图生成器，将任意问题实例映射为统一加权图（节点=决策变量，边=约束关系）。基于该问题无关的加权图构建SLIM（structure-based local improvement framework）进行变量选择，同时利用统一的图特征进行配置选择（算法选择）。

Result: 在20个MiniZinc竞赛问题上，算法选择达到39.5%的平均问题加权胜率（对比one-shot Gurobi基线），是最佳单一配置（19.3%）的两倍多。加入配置和特征消融后性能进一步提升至44.0%。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的语义生成能够实现自动化的结构提取和特征提取，有效支持约束优化中问题无关的变量选择与算法配置选择。

Abstract: Large neighborhood search normally selects a random subset of decision variables for iterative optimization. For efficiently solving different problems, researchers tend to design variable selection strategies by taking into account structural features from different domains. In this paper, we build an automatic pipeline that is problem-agnostic to all problems in the MiniZinc format. By prompting an LLM with our semantic guidelines, we guide the LLM to produce a graph generator that maps any instance of a problem type to a uniform weighted graph, where nodes represent decision variables and edges represent constraint relationships. These problem-agnostic graphs guide our structure-based local improvement framework (SLIM) in variable selection. Meanwhile, the weighted graph enables all problem instances to share the same generic graph representation, from which the same graph features can be extracted and used for configuration selection. We evaluated our pipeline on instances across 20 MiniZinc competition problems, finding that algorithm selection achieves a 39.5% average problem-weighted win rate against a one-shot Gurobi baseline, more than doubling the best single configuration (19.3%). Configuration and feature ablation boost the performance further to 44.0%, demonstrating that LLM-based semantic generation enables effective automated structure extraction and feature extraction for constraint optimization.

</details>


### [327] [LongEarth-R1: Benchmarking and Aligning Vision-Language Models for Long-Horizon Earth Observation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13344)
*Yupan Ding,Jing Xiao,Zhenyuan Zhang,Chaofeng Chen,Liang Liao,Gui-Song Xia,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LongEarth-Bench基准，包含约12万条问答样本（来自11.7万张唯一图像），序列平均15.14帧、最长30帧，覆盖12个长序列遥感推理任务；并在此基础上构建LongEarth模型（监督微调+结构化思维链）与LongEarth-R1（引入群体相对策略优化及格式/时间/空间奖励），后者在全部12个长序列任务上取得最佳结果，同时在标准遥感基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感视觉语言模型主要聚焦于单张图像、图像对或短序列，难以可靠地在相关帧和区域上进行时空接地（grounding），无法支撑长时序地球观测推理所需的多阶段地理演变组织、空间变化定位、时间异常检测及未来推断等能力。

Method: 1) 构建LongEarth-Bench基准：约120k问答样本，11.7万张唯一图像，平均15.14帧可扩展至30帧，涵盖12个子任务（演变摘要、空间推理、异常识别、逻辑预测），其中30k子集提供结构化推理轨迹以关联关键帧、变化区域与最终答案。2) 开发LongEarth模型：通过监督微调，引入显式序列标识符和结构化思维链监督。3) 进一步提出LongEarth-R1：采用群体相对策略优化（GRPO），结合格式奖励、时间奖励和空间奖励进行强化学习。

Result: LongEarth-R1在全部12个长序列任务上取得最优表现，同时在标准遥感基准上仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过大规模长序列基准与"监督微调+结构化思维链+强化学习奖励机制"的组合，能够有效增强遥感模型的长时间跨域推理与时空接地能力，且不牺牲在标准遥感任务上的性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon Earth observation reasoning requires models to organize multi-stage geographic evolution, localize spatial changes, detect temporal anomalies, and infer future from extended image sequences. However, existing remote sensing vision-language models mainly focus on isolated images, image pairs, or short sequences, limiting reliable grounding in the relevant frames and regions. We introduce LongEarth-Bench, a benchmark containing approximately 120k question-answering samples derived from 117k unique images. Its sequences average 15.14 frames and extend to 30 frames, covering 12 tasks across evolution summarization, spatial reasoning, anomaly identification, and logical prediction. A 30k-sample subset further provides structured reasoning traces linking key frames and changed regions to final answers. We develop LongEarth through supervised fine-tuning with explicit sequence identifiers and structured chain-of-thought supervision. Building on LongEarth, LongEarth-R1 applies group relative policy optimization with format, temporal, and spatial rewards. LongEarth-R1 achieves the best results on all 12 long-sequence tasks while remaining competitive on standard remote sensing benchmarks.

</details>


### [328] [Rules or Character? Scaling Laws for AI Safety Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13345)
*Satoshi Takahashi,Nobuji Kouno,Masaaki Komatsu,Ryuji Hamamoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个风格化的比较静态模型，研究AI安全系统中性格塑造与规则执行两种方法的最优资源分配比例alpha*如何随部署规模T变化。通过闭合解和蒙特卡洛模拟（含CVaR尾部风险分析），发现在三种情景下最优alpha*随T增大而向性格塑造方向偏移，但决定性参数是基础性格脆弱率p^0_frag，其对alpha*的影响（0.50）远超其他因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI安全系统同时使用训练时的性格塑造（如RLHF、Constitutional AI）和推理时的规则强制（如输出过滤器、安全分类器），但缺乏对两者最优平衡如何随部署规模扩大而变化的正式分析。现有工作均为直觉性或经验性，缺少理论框架来指导安全架构的资源分配决策。

Method: 构建风格化的比较静态模型，将安全设计参数化为两种方法间的资源分配系数alpha∈[0,1]，纳入规模依赖的过滤器退化、共因失效和性格脆弱性。在乘性Pareto损害模型下推导期望损害的闭合解，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟补充CVaR尾部风险分析，比较三种情景（乐观、中度、悲观）下的最优alpha*。

Result: 三种情景下的最优alpha*均为内点解或处于纯规则边界，且随部署规模T增大呈弱向性格塑造方向偏移（+0.01到+0.21不等）。主导参数是基础性格脆弱率p^0_frag，其在整个范围内可将alpha*移动0.50，远超尾部严重度、过滤器质量和共因失效概率的影响。在大T时，CVaR与期望损害的最优点趋于收敛。

Conclusion: 安全架构决策对部署规模本身的依赖程度不如对性格塑造在分布偏移下可靠性的依赖程度高。这提示实际AI安全设计应优先关注shaped行为的稳健性，而非仅根据部署规模调整规则强制与性格塑造的分配比例。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) safety systems combine character shaping (e.g., Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback [RLHF], Constitutional AI), which modifies behavioral distributions at training time, with rule enforcement (e.g., output filters, safety classifiers), which blocks harmful outputs at inference time, yet little formal analysis exists on how their optimal balance should change as deployment scales increase. We introduce a stylized comparative-statics model that parameterizes safety design as a resource allocation alpha in [0,1] between these two approaches, incorporating scale-dependent filter degradation, common-mode failures, and character fragility -- the risk that shaped behavior degrades or collapses under novel conditions. Under a multiplicative Pareto damage model, we derive closed-form expected harm and supplement it with tail-risk (CVaR) analysis via Monte Carlo simulation. Across three scenarios (optimistic, moderate, pessimistic), the optimal alpha* is interior or at the rules-only boundary and shifts weakly toward character shaping as deployment scale T grows, from negligible (Delta alpha* = +0.01) to pronounced (Delta alpha* = +0.21) depending on scenario. The dominant parameter is the baseline character fragility rate p^(0)_frag, which shifts alpha* by 0.50 across its range -- far exceeding the effect of tail severity, filter quality, or common-mode failure probability. CVaR and expected-harm optima converge at large T. These results suggest that safety architecture decisions depend less on deployment scale per se than on the reliability of character shaping under distributional shift.

</details>


### [329] [TopoIntent: Compiling Security Intent into Executable, Compliance-Checked Network Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13389)
*Xiaokang Qu,Jianliang Ma,Zao Fan,Tianshu Chu,Tianlong Fan,Linyuan Lü*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoIntent是一个从自然语言安全意图自动编译为可执行、合规检查的网络拓扑的系统。它通过模式契约约束生成、基于密集向量检索参考架构模板、采用分阶段融合实现意图-模板对齐和安全补全，并对生成结果进行CIS Controls v8.1.2合规检查和结构缺陷的模态保持修复。系统最终导出为带内核级iptables ACL的Mininet脚本以支持可执行可达性和allow/deny测试。在自建的评估集上，补丁式修复将CIS满足率从0.78提升至1.00，单轮反馈将ACL策略通过率从0.78提升至0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业安全拓扑设计需要将业务意图、法规要求和风险假设转化为区域、边界设备、区域间路径和访问控制策略。现有的NetOps自动化工具主要在拓扑设计确定之后才介入，缺乏从非结构化自然语言需求生成结构化安全拓扑的能力。该任务缺乏公开基准，且从欠约束的语言需求到可执行拓扑之间存在显著鸿沟。

Method: TopoIntent采用四阶段流程：(1) 使用模式契约（schema contract）约束生成过程；(2) 通过密集向量搜索从精选模板库中检索参考架构；(3) 应用分阶段融合（staged fusion）进行意图-模板对齐和安全补全；(4) 对生成的拓扑执行CIS Controls v8.1.2安全合规检查，未解决案例标记为人工审查。结构缺口通过增量式、模式保持的编辑进行修复，最终导出为Mininet脚本并配置内核级iptables ACL，支持可执行的可达性和allow/deny测试。

Result: 由于该需求-拓扑任务没有公开基准，研究者从参考安全架构图中构建了评估集：检索集包含22个模板和44个合成意图（覆盖5个场景），留出集包含7个模板和14个意图（来自金融和政府场景，不在检索集中）。在留出集上，增量式修复使拓扑层可见的CIS满足率从0.78提升到1.00，平均无需超过1.5轮；一次反馈轮次可将ACL策略后通过率从0.78提升到0.88。

Conclusion: TopoIntent证明了从自然语言安全意图自动编译为合规且可执行网络拓扑的可行性。该系统通过模式契约、模板检索、分阶段融合和增量修复的组合，实现了高水平的合规满足率和策略通过率，填补了从需求到拓扑的自动化设计空白。由于缺乏现有基准，该方法提供了一个新的评估框架供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Enterprise security topology design requires translating business intent, regulatory requirements, and risk assumptions into zones, boundary devices, inter-zone paths, and access-control policies. Existing NetOps automation tools mainly operate after this design is fixed, providing limited support for generating structured security topologies from underspecified natural-language requirements. We present TopoIntent, a system that compiles security intent into executable, compliance-checked network topologies. It uses a schema contract to constrain generation, retrieves reference architectures from a curated template library via dense-vector search, and applies staged fusion for intent-template alignment and security completion. The generated topology is checked against CIS Controls v8.1.2 safeguards visible at the topology layer, while unresolved cases are marked for manual review. Structural gaps are repaired through additive schema-preserving edits. The final topology is exported to Mininet scripts with kernel-level iptables ACLs, enabling executable reachability and allow/deny tests. Because no public benchmark exists for this requirement-to-topology task, we construct an evaluation set from reference security architecture diagrams. The retrieval set contains 22 templates and 44 synthetic intents across five scenarios, while the held-out set contains 7 templates and 14 intents from finance and government scenarios excluded from retrieval. On the held-out set, additive repair improves topology-visible CIS satisfaction from 0.78 to 1.00 in fewer than 1.5 rounds on average, and one feedback round raises the post-ACL policy pass rate from 0.78 to 0.88.

</details>


### [330] [Jointly Predicting Courses and Grades Using a Transformer-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13409)
*Paul Savala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE模型，一种基于Transformer的学术课程成绩预测框架。该模型摒弃了将学生学业历史视为简单序列的传统做法，而是按学期对课程进行编码以捕捉课程并修（concurrency）效应，并通过联合预测学生下学期的课程集合及其对应成绩来提升预测精度。在十年机构数据上的实验表明，该联合预测方法相比仅预测成绩的相同架构，平均绝对误差降低近50%，并优于LSTM序列模型和GNN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习分析中的预测模型将学生的学业历史视为简单序列，忽略了同一学期内课程并修的本质特征。这种简化尤其对课业负担重或选课困难的学生导致预测不准确。作者旨在通过显式建模课程并修效应来提升成绩预测的准确性。

Method: TRACE采用Transformer架构，以学期为单位对课程进行编码，从而捕捉课程并修效应。模型联合预测两个目标：学生下学期将选修的课程集合，以及这些课程的对应成绩。训练采用一种结合课程集合预测与成绩预测的新型损失函数。模型还支持自然地融入学生属性数据，可通过重训练和重新校准适配新机构。

Result: 在十年机构数据的训练中，联合预测模型相比仅预测成绩的相同架构将平均绝对误差降低近50%；同时优于传统LSTM序列模型和基于图神经网络的方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了现代神经架构在创建可解释的学习分析模型方面的价值，强调了训练期间预测所修课程这一关键技术的重要性，并探讨了将模型整合到高等教育机构早期预警系统中的可行性。

Abstract: Existing predictive models in learning analytics often treat student academic history as a simple sequence, overlooking the concurrent nature of courses taken within a semester. This simplification can lead to inaccurate performance predictions, particularly for students with heavy or challenging course loads. This paper introduces a TRansformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation (TRACE) that addresses this limitation by jointly predicting both the set of courses a student will take and their corresponding grades for an upcoming semester. Our approach encodes courses on a per-semester basis to capture the effects of course concurrency and utilizes a novel loss function combining course-set prediction with grade prediction. We demonstrate that predicting courses taken in addition to the grades in those courses leads to significant improvements in prediction quality. Trained on ten years of institutional data, our joint prediction model reduces mean absolute error by nearly 50% compared to an identical architecture that predicts grades alone. The model also outperforms traditional LSTM-based sequential models, as well as graph neural network-based approaches, and offers natural ways to incorporate student attribute data. This work demonstrates the utility of modern neural architectures for creating interpretable models that can be adapted to new institutions via retraining and recalibration, as well as the importance of key techniques, such as predicting courses taken during training. We discuss how this model could be incorporated into early detection systems at institutions of higher education.

</details>


### [331] [Who Speaks Matters: Authority-Aware Multi-View RAG over Italian Parliamentary Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13410)
*Mirko Tritella,Riccardo Pozzi,Matteo Palmonari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ParliamentRAG，一个针对意大利众议院议会记录的检索增强生成系统，通过主题依赖的权威模型解决RAG在议会场景中的三大风险：高频发言者主导、无法按主题专长加权、政治敏感文本中的引文误归属问题。实验表明其在政治群体覆盖率和引文 faithfulness 上优于 Google NotebookLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 议会记录是民主审议的主要载体，但其体量大且碎片化，公民、记者和研究者难以进行多视角访问。将RAG应用于议会转录文本会引入三个特定风险：（1）高频发言者主导检索结果；（2）无法根据主题专长对发言人加权；（3）政治敏感文本中的引文归属错误。

Method: 核心贡献是主题依赖的权威模型（topic-dependent authority model），该模型根据当前查询估计每位发言人的权威性，结合可解释组件如职业、教育背景和过往发言记录。系统流程为：给定用户查询→检索相关发言片段→跨议会党派识别主题相关专家→生成综合各方视角的摘要并附带支持性引文。评估采用双层协议，结合自动化指标和六位领域专家的盲法A/B人类评估，对照Google NotebookLM，覆盖15个政策主题。

Result: ParliamentRAG在政治群体覆盖率上更高（0.97 vs 0.95），引文 faithfulness 达到完美（1.00 vs 0.95），且在来源相关维度上获得更强的专家偏好；而NotebookLM在散文风格相关维度上表现更强。

Conclusion: ParliamentRAG通过主题依赖的权威建模联合解决了议会场景下RAG的三个核心风险，在来源准确性和党派覆盖方面优于现有通用RAG工具，展示了将可解释的领域知识与RAG结合以增强民主透明度的潜力。

Abstract: Parliamentary proceedings are a primary record of democratic deliberation, yet their volume and fragmentation make multi-perspective access difficult for citizens, journalists, and researchers. Applying Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to parliamentary transcripts introduces three specific risks: dominance of the most frequent speakers, inability to weight speakers according to topical expertise, and citation misattribution in politically sensitive text. We present ParliamentRAG, a RAG system for the Italian Chamber of Deputies that addresses these risks jointly. Its core contribution is a topic-dependent authority model that estimates each speaker's authority as a function of the current query, combining interpretable components such as profession, education, and previous interventions. Given a user query, the system retrieves relevant speech chunks, identifies topic-relevant experts across parliamentary groups, and generates a summary synthesizing their perspectives, accompanied by supporting quotations. ParliamentRAG is evaluated against Google NotebookLM on 15 policy topics via a two-level protocol combining automated metrics and blind A/B human evaluation by six domain experts. The system achieves higher coverage across political groups (0.97 vs. 0.95), perfect quotation faithfulness (1.00 vs. 0.95), and stronger expert preferences on source-related dimensions, while NotebookLM remains stronger on prose-oriented dimensions.

</details>


### [332] [Beyond Final Scores: A Systematic Evaluation of Agents for Long-Horizon AI Research and Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13417)
*Yiwei Li,Wanli Yang,Hexiang Tan,Xiangzhou Huang,Zhengyu Chen,Ziran Li,Borun Chen,Shanglin Lei,Huaisheng Zhu,Hao Tian,Fei Sun,Xunliang Cai,Jingang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统化评估框架，对7个前沿模型在36个长时程任务上的行为进行细粒度分析，通过基于规则的过程指标（解决方案框架、执行、反馈控制）和受控对照实验评估经验复用，揭示当前自主智能体更像是"工程优化器"而非"完全自主的研究者"：能制定和实施实用方案，但跨运行表现波动大、真正的方法论创新罕见，且经验复用既可能帮助也可能误导后续决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统只考察最终分数的评估无法揭示能力提升或损失发生在何处，也无法判断累积的实践经验是否真正改善了后续决策，因此需要超越最终成绩的、能刻画过程中行为的评估体系。

Method: 提出一个新评估框架，包含：(1) 使用基于规则（rule-based）的指标从Solution Framing、Execution、Feedback Control三个维度刻画运行内行为；(2) 通过受控对照比较来评估经验在任务内部和跨任务间的复用效果；共覆盖7个前沿模型、36个长时程任务。

Result: 结果显示：当前智能体的行为更像工程优化器而非自主研究者——能够提出并实施实用方案，但跨运行性能差异显著；最强方案主要依赖对已有技术的适配或组合；真正的方法论创新罕见。深入分析发现：相似最终结果背后存在不同的过程瓶颈；经验复用既可能帮助也可能误导后续决策；harness设计会影响性能稳定性。

Conclusion: 这些发现为改进模型训练、推理时策略、经验管理以及harness设计提供了具体方向。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly capable of improving models, systems, and other technical artifacts through long-horizon experimentation. To understand the current state of this capability, however, evaluation must go beyond final scores, which neither reveal where progress is gained or lost nor indicate whether accumulated experience improves later decisions. We therefore present a systematic evaluation of seven frontier models on 36 long-horizon tasks based on a new framework that uses rule-based metrics to characterize within-run behavior through Solution Framing, Execution, and Feedback Control and controlled comparisons to assess experience reuse within and across tasks. The results show that current agents operate more like engineering optimizers than fully autonomous researchers: they can formulate and implement practical solutions, but their performance varies substantially across runs, their strongest solutions mainly adapt or combine established techniques, and genuine methodological novelty remains rare. Detailed analysis reveals that observed performance is shaped by multiple factors, including distinct process bottlenecks behind similar final outcomes, experience reuse that can help or mislead subsequent decisions, and harness designs that affect performance stability. These findings suggest concrete directions for improving model training, inference-time strategies, experience management, and harness design.

</details>


### [333] [Enhancing Virtual Agents through SLMs and Edge-Computing: An Exploratory Evaluation of Think and Memory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13420)
*Aimilios Hadjiliasi,Louis Nisiotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在边缘计算环境中使用小型语言模型（SLMs）支持认知具身智能体架构（CEAA）中的"思考"和"记忆"组件的可行性。作者在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上开发了一个边缘虚拟智能体网关系统，使用不同规模的Qwen2.5模型进行实验，通过模拟实验评估了路由准确率、记忆读取性能和延迟，验证了SLM驱动的原型系统能够部分实现CEAA核心流程，为沉浸式虚拟世界中的具身智能体提供了高效、上下文感知的认知"大脑"支持方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能虚拟代理需要在复杂的虚拟世界和元宇宙中作为持久、自适应、上下文感知的实体运行，但实现具备认知能力的智能体在概念和技术上都具有挑战性。现有CEAA框架虽然为组件设计提供了蓝图，但其"思考"和"记忆"等认知核心流程的实际部署仍面临挑战，尤其是需要兼顾边缘计算的实时性与生成式AI的智能水平。

Method: 本文采用实现导向的方法，基于CEAA框架构建了边缘侧虚拟代理网关系统。具体包括：1) 在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX边缘设备上部署不同规模的Qwen2.5 SLMs；2) 将CEAA中认知编排和持久化核心的"Think"与"Memory"过程映射到SLM驱动流程；3) 通过一系列模拟实验对系统进行路由准确性、记忆读取性能和延迟等维度评估。

Result: 模拟实验结果表明，所开发的SLM驱动原型智能体系统能够部分实现CEAA选定的认知过程，验证了在边缘设备上使用小型语言模型支持具身智能体认知操作的技术可行性，同时在不同Qwen2.5模型规模下均展现出可接受的路由准确率和延迟表现。

Conclusion: 研究证明边缘SLM驱动的认知架构能够为具身智能体提供高效且上下文感知的认知支持，为在沉浸式虚拟世界中开发持久、自适应的智能体提供了可行路径。该工作弥合了CEAA理论框架与实际部署之间的差距，展示了将生成式AI模型与边缘计算相结合来支持虚拟世界认知智能体的未来方向。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent virtual agents are expected to operate as persistent, adaptive, and context-aware entities within complex virtual and Metaverse worlds. However, implementing cognitively capable agents in such environments is conceptually and technologically challenging. Among a range of blueprints and development approaches, the Cognitive Embodied Agent Architecture (CEAA) has been developed as an implementation-oriented framework for architecting components of perception, memory, reasoning, planning, and embodied action. Considering the recent advances in edge computing and generative AI language models, this paper explores the use of Small Language Models (SLMs) to support edge-based operation of selected CEAA components, focusing on "Think" and "Memory" as processes central to cognitive orchestration and persistence of virtual agents in interactive virtual worlds. An edge-based virtual agent gateway system was developed and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using Qwen2.5 models of different sizes, exploring the system's capability to process service requests and handle memory-driven conversations. A series of simulation experiments evaluated routing accuracy, memory-read performance, and latency, demonstrating an SLM-driven prototype agent system that partially implements selected CEAA processes to support the development of embodied agents whose cognitive "brain" can operate efficiently and contextually for interactive experiences in immersive virtual worlds.

</details>


### [334] [RAIL: An Automatic Classifier of the Artificial Intelligence Readiness Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13428)
*Juan Irving Vasquez,Juan Terven,Laura-Ivoone Garay-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一AI就绪度等级（AIRL），一种九级序数量表，基于环境证据阶梯并辅以维度上限、通用性锚定规则和明确的分配纪律，使就绪度可仅凭自然语言描述即可判定。同时提出RAIL，一个专家小组分类器，由证据代理和六个独立维度代理组成，每个代理都是窄范围授权的大语言模型，通过确定性最小规则聚合、首席专家以非对称权威复核（只能确认或降低、不可提升）来判定等级，测试显示一致性优于单体LLM分类器且避免高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能技术成熟度的评估对投资决策、项目管理和政策监测至关重要，但现有就绪度框架异质性高且难以自动应用：TRL对AI缺乏特定门控标准、MLTRL预设需访问内部过程工件、AI/数据就绪度维度模型采用难以直接比较的量表。

Method: 首先统一三种现有框架为AIRL九级量表（基于环境证据阶梯，配合六个维度上限：规格、数据存在性、数据质量、数据合法性、专家知识、算法成熟度，以及通用性锚定规则和显式分配纪律）。其次提出RAIL分类器：一个证据代理+六个独立维度代理（均为窄范围授权的大语言模型），通过确定性最小规则聚合裁决，首席专家以非对称权威复核——只能确认或降低小组建议，不可越过上限提升。

Result: 该方法在多个研究工作的分析测试中表现出一致性，且避免了单体LLM分类器的过度估计问题。

Conclusion: AIRL与RAIL为AI技术成熟度自动评估提供了统一、可判定且可靠的方案，通过窄范围多代理架构有效抑制了单体大语言模型的高估倾向，适用于投资、项目管理和政策监测等场景。

Abstract: Assessing the maturity of artificial intelligence technologies is essential for investment decisions, project management, and policy monitoring, yet the available readiness frameworks are heterogeneous and difficult to apply automatically: the adaptation of Technology Readiness Levels to AI lacks AI-specific gating criteria, the Machine Learning Technology Readiness Levels presuppose access to internal process artifacts, and AI/data readiness dimension models employ scales that resist direct comparison. This paper makes two contributions. First, we unify these three frameworks into the Unified AI Readiness Level (AIRL), a nine-level ordinal scale built on an environmental evidence ladder and complemented by dimensional caps (covering specification, data existence, data quality, data legality, expert knowledge, and algorithmic maturity) together with a generality-anchoring rule and explicit assignment disciplines, so that a readiness level becomes decidable from a natural-language description of the work alone. Second, we propose RAIL (Readiness Assessment via Independent LLM-experts), a panel-of-experts classifier that operationalizes the scale: one evidence agent and six independent dimension agents, each a large language model with a narrowly scoped mandate, deliver verdicts that a deterministic minimum rule aggregates and a chief expert reviews under asymmetric authority, confirming or lowering the panel's recommendation but never raising it above the caps. The method was tested in the analysis of several research works showing consistency and avoiding overestimation from monolithic LLM classifiers.

</details>


### [335] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学（UFSC）人工智能学术联盟（LIA）的组织框架，该框架通过学生中心、项目驱动的方式整合教学、科研与大学扩展三大支柱，采用民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织来培养技术与横向能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术联盟已成为促进课外教育和加强大学与社会融合的重要机制，但缺乏可复制的组织框架来系统性地整合教学、科研与扩展活动。

Method: 提出一个结合民主治理、协作学习与动态项目组织的组织框架，并通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库及有社会影响的人工智能应用等代表性项目进行说明。

Result: 该框架成功在统一组织结构下开发了多样化教育、科研与扩展活动，促进了领导力、科学生产、社区参与和知识保存。

Conclusion: 该框架是灵活且可复制的模型，可为学术联盟及类似学生组织整合大学三大支柱提供实践指导，尤其在工程和计算教育领域。

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [336] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角系统研究世界模型（WM），提出因果世界模型（CWM）的形式化定义，强调WM不仅需要生成能力，还需捕捉实体属性、实体间及实体与环境间的因果交互，并将世界模型与因果表示学习、目标中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策制定等现有工作联系起来，同时探讨了可识别性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被视为智能体在分布外进行预测、规划和行动的基础，但现有世界模型主要关注生成能力，缺乏对环境中因果结构的显式建模，使得模型难以真正支持因果推理和稳健的决策制定。作者希望从因果视角重新审视世界模型，为其提供更坚实的理论基础。

Method: 作者在多个抽象层面研究世界模型，从感知观测到概念表征。首先论证有用的世界模型必须超越纯生成能力，需捕捉实体属性、实体间交互及实体与环境交互；其次基于其所支持的任务给出因果世界模型（CWM）的形式化定义；然后将其与因果表示学习、目标中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策制定等文献建立联系；最后从可识别性角度分析世界模型各组件何时可从数据中恢复及其等价性。

Result: 作者给出了因果世界模型的形式化定义，建立了世界模型与多个相关研究领域的理论联系，并阐明了世界模型各组件的可识别性条件及其等价性。

Conclusion: 通过将世界模型扎根于支持因果推理和知情决策的表征与结构中，本文为世界模型提供了更坚实的因果理论基础，指明了未来构建真正能理解环境因果结构的世界模型的方向。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [337] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARC（Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination），一个开源的多智能体临床推理框架，用确定性的多智能体编排替代传统的单体LLM提示方法。MARC协调提取、推理、答案生成和评估等角色专业化智能体，支持显式上下文传递和可追踪的中间输出，并引入Decomposer模块自动生成任务特定的智能体提示，无需人工提示工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单体LLM提示方法在临床推理任务中存在可解释性差、失败难以归因、需要大量人工提示工程等问题，缺乏面向临床领域专家的可访问性和可调试性。

Method: MARC采用确定性的多智能体编排架构，包含提取、推理、答案生成和评估四个角色专业化智能体，支持显式上下文传递和阶段级失败归因；引入Decomposer模块从自然语言描述自动生成任务特定提示；框架支持API和本地CPU部署，通过YAML完全配置，模型无关且无需代码修改。

Result: MARC提供了完全开源的实现（GitHub仓库），支持多种部署方式，通过阶段级失败归因实现了可解释性，并通过自动提示生成消除了人工提示工程的需求。

Conclusion: MARC为临床推理提供了一种可解释、可访问、模型无关的多智能体框架，使临床领域专家无需编程专业知识即可使用和调试，代表了临床LLM应用从单体提示向可编排多智能体系统转变的方向。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [338] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlayaWorld的改进版本，在保持骨干架构、分块自回归生成方案和训练数据不变的前提下，围绕"条件信号应在潜在表示和时间结构上尽可能匹配生成内容"这一原则，对条件信号的表示和集成方式进行了系统性重构。核心改动包括：以流式3D点缓存渲染器替代深度扭曲空间记忆，并将视觉条件统一编码到与生成视频一致的因果VAE潜空间。具体包含六项修改：运动感知潜在条件、空间记忆的因果序列编码、时间记忆窗口对齐、硬记忆丢弃、VAE编解码协议统一以及移除相机AdaLN分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前版本的AlayaWorld中，条件信号与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上存在不一致，导致条件信息的利用效率有限。作者提出核心原则：条件信号应在潜在表征和时间结构上尽可能贴近生成内容，从而提升条件生成的质量与可控性。

Method: 在保持骨干架构、分块自回归生成方案与训练数据不变的基础上，提出两项主要改动：（1）以流式3D点缓存渲染器取代基于深度扭曲的空间记忆，实现空间条件的高效重渲染；（2）重构条件管道，使视觉条件编码进与生成视频一致的因果VAE潜空间。具体引入六项修改：运动感知潜在条件替代静态帧图像条件、对重渲染的空间记忆进行因果序列编码、在像素空间中对齐时间记忆窗口、采用硬记忆丢弃（删除记忆token而非置零）、统一训练与推理阶段的VAE编解码协议、移除相机AdaLN分支使视角控制完全由重渲染空间条件提供。

Result: 论文着重描述了改进版AlayaWorld的系统设计，未在摘要中给出具体的量化实验指标，但通过六项系统性改动实现了条件信号与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上的高度对齐，预期提升了条件信号利用效率和生成质量。

Conclusion: 改进版AlayaWorld通过统一条件信号的潜在表示空间与时间结构，强化了生成内容与条件信息的一致性。这一设计原则及其六项具体实现，为视频条件生成模型提供了一种更简洁、更一致的条件整合范式。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [339] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuoteBench基准，通过56个一次性任务和14个源自事故的家族，在生成合同与执行传输之间设置一个故意未转义的解析器，来测量LLM编码代理在命令生成后的边界适应能力。结果表明，边界适应是区分模型的关键因素，而匹配执行分数会掩盖真实的生成缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理通过接口发出Bash命令时，输出会被序列化、包装和重新解析。仅依靠匹配执行分数无法区分命令生成阶段的错误与生成后引入的失败，需要一种方法来精确测量这一边界。

Method: 设计QuoteBench基准，包含56个从14个事故衍生家族中获取的一次性任务。方法上通过构造一个故意未转义的解析器，跨越生成合同与执行传输的边界，并在插值点进行转义以复现原始回复的路径结果，从而确保任何在公开边界下的恢复只能来自模型改变其生成内容。

Result: 在八个相同窗口配置中，通过额外解析器重放相同回复使成功率下降55.4至73.2个百分点；其中六个配置通过披露边界恢复了30.4至60.7个百分点，另外两个配置恢复为零或轻微负值。GPT-5.6-sol的匹配差距仅-3.6点，但实际包含-64.3点损坏和+60.7点补偿。部署配置会重新排序模型的排名。

Conclusion: 对命令下发代理的评估应当报告模型配置、生成合同、执行路径、操作点和最终状态验证器，而不应将匹配分数视为模型的内在属性。边界适应能力是当前区分前沿模型的关键维度。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


### [340] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniScientist，一个端到端的多模态 AI 科学家系统，能够直接从异构原始证据（图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式、图等）进行跨学科科学研究。系统由感知层和3个自主智能体（构思、实验、写作）组成确定性流水线，并通过代码级检查强制执行新颖性筛选、统计有效性和数值可追溯性。在36个真实数据案例中全部完成从原始数据到编译手稿的完整流程，平均论文得分6.3，且在感知对比实验中全面优于仅依赖预计算标量特征的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统虽能覆盖研究流程，但仅能处理文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要，无法访问对科学发现至关重要的空间、时间、跨通道和程序性关系，限制了证据驱动研究的深度和可靠性。

Method: 构建端到端多模态AI科学家系统：1）感知层处理各种原始证据模态；2）三个自主智能体分别负责构思、实验和写作；3）确定性流水线使观察结果贯穿研究全生命周期；4）在代码中执行新颖性筛查、统计有效性、执行来源和数值可追踪性检查。

Result: 在涵盖5个学科家族、4类科学证据类型、9种模态的36个真实数据案例中，系统全部完成从原始数据到编译手稿的完整流程，平均论文得分6.3；在配对对比中，直接感知比仅使用预计算标量特征的变体在所有7个评估维度上更优，赢下85%的正面比较。

Conclusion: 全生命周期感知对于证据驱动的科学发现至关重要，OmniScientist为构建广泛能力的AI科学家提供了实用路径，表明感知层在AI科学研究系统中不可或缺。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [341] [RoboSynChallenge: Mastering Real-World Dexterity via Generalizing Synthesized Manipulation Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12416)
*Runyi Zhao,Ruixin Wu,Chengkun Li,Hongrui Zhang,Ang Li,Ruixing Jin,Yueci Deng,Yingying Guo,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Tianfan Xue,Yanjun Gao,Yudong Luo,Pascal Poupart,Simo Wu,Kui Jia,Wei-shi Zheng,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSynChallenge是一个统一基准平台，旨在通过将大规模合成数据生成与标准化真实世界机器人评估相结合，推动机器人操作策略的泛化能力研究。该挑战赛鼓励参赛者利用合成状态-动作轨迹进行策略训练，最终在未见过的真实操作环境中进行测试，并提供Transformer、扩散模型、VLA和世界动作模型等基线实现以保证可比性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界机器人数据的稀缺性和多样性不足严重制约了操作策略的泛化能力，尽管模型架构和学习算法在快速发展，但数据瓶颈仍未得到有效解决。需要一个统一的基准来系统性地评估和推动操作策略的泛化性能。

Method: 该挑战赛构建了一个跨多个任务、环境和难度级别的统一基准，整合大规模合成数据生成管线与标准化真实世界机器人评估协议。提供多种基线实现（基于Transformer、扩散模型、视觉-语言-动作模型和世界动作模型）以确保可复现性和公平比较。

Result: 通过将可扩展的仿真训练与严格的真实世界验证相结合，该挑战赛旨在培育具有广泛能力、数据高效且适应性强的操作系统，推动机器人操作泛化研究的进展。

Conclusion: RoboSynChallenge通过合成数据训练与真实世界评估的范式，为机器人操作策略的研究提供了一个可复现、可比较的标准化平台，有望推动迈向通用机器人智能的进程。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable robotic manipulation remains a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Despite rapid advances in model architectures and learning algorithms, progress is often limited by the scarcity and narrow diversity of real-world data. The RoboSynChallenge competition introduces a unified benchmark to evaluate and advance the generalizability of manipulation policies across a spectrum of tasks, environments, and difficulty levels. To alleviate the shortage of realistic data, the challenge integrates large-scale synthetic data generation with standardized real-world robotic evaluation. Participants are encouraged to leverage synthesized state-action trials to improve general-purpose policy learning, while final assessments are conducted exclusively on unseen real-world manipulation environments. Baseline implementations, including Transformer-, Diffusion-, Vision-Language-Action, and World-Action-Model-based policies, are provided to ensure reproducibility and comparability. By coupling scalable simulation-based training with rigorous real-world validation, RoboSynChallenge aims to foster the development of broadly capable, data-efficient, and adaptable manipulation systems, thereby paving the way toward truly general robotic intelligence.

</details>


### [342] [Attune: A Self-Annotation Tool for Understanding Robot Operator Attention Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12650)
*Puqi Zhou,Sungsoo Ray Hong,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Attune，一种部署前的交互设计工具，通过捕捉和分析操作员的眼动注视模式，帮助多机器人监督界面设计者理解如何通过机器人行为来吸引、维持或释放操作员的注意力，从而为机器人行为设计提供实证指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂真实环境中部署机器人车队需要人类操作员同时监督多个机器人，而管理操作员注意力是多机器人监督界面设计中的基本挑战。目前设计者缺乏关于如何通过改变机器人行为来捕获、维持或放弃操作员注意力的实证指导，这阻碍了界面的优化设计。

Method: 作者将操作员的眼动注视视为机器人行为设计的线索，开发了名为Attune的预部署elicitation工具。Attune能够自动识别有意义的注视移转，提供AI辅助来注释移转原因，并输出操作员注视模式摘要供操作员审查。作者通过用户研究评估了Attune，让参与者标注吸引其注意力的视觉触发因素。

Result: 研究发现观察到的注视模式存在差异，并揭示了Attune如何帮助表征操作员注意力。这些发现表明了该方法在刻画操作员注意力特征方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为多机器人监督界面中机器人行为设计提供了基于眼动的实证工具和方法，未来设计者可以利用此类指导将机器人行为校准到不同操作员的注意力特征上，从而实现更有效的多机器人监督。

Abstract: Deploying robot fleets in complex, real-world environments requires human operators to supervise multiple robots simultaneously. Managing operator attention is a fundamental challenge of designing multi-robot supervision interfaces, encompassing both feed layout and feed content (i.e., robot behavior design). Thus far, designers lack empirical guidance on the latter-how to change a robot's behavior to capture, sustain, or relinquish operator attention during multi-robot supervision. In our vision of the future, designers should be able to use this guidance to calibrate robot behavior to different operator attention profiles. Treating operator eye gaze as a robot behavior design clue, we created a pre-deployment elicitation tool called Attune. Attune automatically identifies when meaningful gaze shifts occur, provides AI assistance for annotating why shifts occurred, and outputs a summary of operator gaze patterns for operator review. We evaluated Attune through a user study in which participants annotated the visual triggers that drew their attention. Our findings unveil variation in observed gaze patterns and reveal how Attune helps characterize operator attention.

</details>


### [343] [FUSE: Active Functional Affordance Grounding through Adaptive Semantic-Geometric Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12683)
*Zhou Chen,Sathyanarayanan N. Aakur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出主动功能可供性接地（Active Functional Affordance Grounding）这一新任务，并构建了FUSE框架——一种自适应语义-几何证据采集方法，通过显式不确定性驱动的探索与学习型摊销规划器相结合，使智能体在场景中主动探索以识别并空间定位满足功能查询的对象。基于Habitat的基准测试表明，FUSE在非神谕接地性能上取得最高水平，计算量较完全显式探索降低1.33倍，且在多种可供性知识源下均保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体常需基于对象功能而非身份进行识别和交互，但现有可供性接地方法局限于固定视角，缺乏在功能线索被遮挡或不完整时主动决定探索位置的机制，因此需要引入主动探索与信息采集能力。

Method: 提出FUSE（自适应语义-几何证据采集）框架，将显式不确定性驱动的探索与学习型摊销规划器相结合，高效选择信息量最大的视角；同时引入基于Habitat的基准来评估主动功能接地性能。

Result: FUSE在非神谕接地性能上达到最高水平，相比完全显式探索方法计算量减少1.33倍，并在多种可供性知识源下均保持有效性。

Conclusion: 显式不确定性探索与学习型摊销规划的融合能有效平衡性能与计算效率，为功能驱动的主动具身感知提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Embodied agents must often identify and interact with objects based on their function rather than their identity, requiring them to actively acquire observations that reveal discriminative functional evidence. Existing affordance grounding methods operate from fixed viewpoints and lack mechanisms for deciding where to look when functional cues are occluded or incomplete. We introduce Active Functional Affordance Grounding, a new task in which an agent sequentially explores a scene to identify and spatially ground an object satisfying a functional query. To address this problem, we propose FUSE, an adaptive semantic-geometric evidence acquisition framework that combines explicit uncertainty-driven exploration with a learned amortized planner to efficiently select informative viewpoints. We further introduce a Habitat-based benchmark for evaluating active functional grounding. Experiments show that FUSE achieves the highest observed non-oracle grounding performance while reducing computation by 1.33x relative to fully explicit exploration, and remains effective across multiple affordance knowledge sources.

</details>


### [344] [SAP-Nav: Spatial Semantic Representation Meets Active Perception for Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12707)
*Xuetong Pei,Jian Liu,Vidura Munasinghe,Bo Miao,U-Xuan Tan,Wenrui Ding,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAP-Nav是一个完全在线、零样本的分层开放词汇目标导航框架，通过主动感知增量构建可查询的空间语义表示（QSSR），并采用主动视点验证（AVV）来评估当前观测是否足够，必要时重新定位到信息更丰富的视点以验证候选目标。在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上取得最优性能，区域级导航SR较训练方法提升12.2%，并经过真实机器人实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层开放词汇目标导航（OVON）要求代理在未知环境中通过场景、房间、区域和实例级线索进行导航定位，但部分观测条件下存在根本矛盾：空间grounding需要持续的环境级证据，而目标验证需要清晰且具有区分度的候选视图。现有方法难以在在线、零样本设置下同时满足这两项要求。

Method: SAP-Nav由两个核心组件构成：(1) 查询式空间语义表示（Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation, QSSR），从主动采集的房间视图中增量构建，支持从任何已探索位置进行空间语义查询；(2) 主动视点验证（Active Viewpoint Verification, AVV），评估当前观测是否提供足够的验证证据，必要时将代理重定位到信息更充分的视点，再按类别和属性约束验证候选目标。该框架无需任务特定训练或预计算场景地图，同时支持分层和标准类别级OVON。

Result: 在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上，SAP-Nav取得整体最优性能，区域级导航成功率（SR）较基于训练的方法提升12.2%。真实机器人实验进一步验证了方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: SAP-Nav通过主动感知策略统一解决空间grounding和视点验证的矛盾需求，证明了完全在线、零样本框架在分层OVON中的优越性和实际部署可行性，为该方向提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical open-vocabulary object navigation (OVON) requires agents to follow free-form instructions that may specify targets through scene-, room-, region-, and instance-level cues in unseen environments. Although recent work LangMap has formalized this setting, reliably solving it under partial observations remains challenging: spatial grounding requires persistent environment-level evidence, whereas target verification requires clear and discriminative candidate views. We present SAP-Nav, a fully online, zero-shot framework that addresses both requirements through active perception. SAP-Nav incrementally constructs a Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation from actively acquired room views, enabling spatial semantic queries from any explored location. It further employs Active Viewpoint Verification to assess whether the current observation provides sufficient evidence and, when necessary, reposition the agent to a more informative viewpoint before verifying candidates against category and attribute constraints. Although designed for hierarchical OVON, SAP-Nav supports both hierarchical and standard category-level OVON without task-specific training or precomputed scene maps. Experiments on LangMap and HM3D-OVON show that SAP-Nav achieves the overall best performance, including a 12.2% improvement in SR over training-based methods on region-level navigation. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate its practical feasibility. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [345] [Genetic Fuzzy System-Based Multi-Robot Coordination for Planetary Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12755)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于遗传模糊系统的分散式多机器人协同搬运方法，在非结构化环境中通过地形坡度可穿越性分析将高程图转换为二维可穿越性地图，利用遗传算法优化模糊推理系统生成机器人速度，以最小化总路径长度并避开障碍物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人在非结构化环境中协同搬运物体时，需要同时满足路径最短和避障需求，而传统集中式方法在复杂环境中计算量大、鲁棒性差，因此需要一种分散式且自适应能力强的解决方案。

Method: 首先对高程图进行基于坡度的地形可穿越性分析，将不可穿越区域视为障碍物并降维转化为二维可穿越性地图；然后设计模糊推理系统（FISs）来生成多机器人系统将物体搬运至目标位置所需的速度；最后利用遗传算法在局部极小值、目标靠近障碍物、杂乱环境等多种场景下训练优化FIS参数。

Result: 训练好的FIS模型被应用于测试环境（即转换后的可穿越性地图），并通过多种场景进行了验证。

Conclusion: 该分散式遗传模糊系统能够有效地通过离线训练获得FIS模型并应用于新环境，实现多机器人协同搬运中的路径最短化和避障目标。

Abstract: This paper proposes a decentralized approach for a multi-robot system (MRS) using a genetic fuzzy system to perform a collaborative object transportation task that minimizes the total path length of the MRS in unstructured environment while avoiding obstacles. For an environment given by an elevation map, terrain traversability analysis with respect to the slope is performed to reduce the dimension and identify non-traversable areas that can be considered as obstacles, and the given map is converted into a traversability map in two dimensional space. In the training process, proposed fuzzy inference systems (FISs) to generate the MRS's velocity for transporting an object to a target position are optimized by a genetic algorithm with several scenarios, such as a local minima, a target that is close to an obstacle, and a cluttered environment. The trained FIS models are applied to the testing environment, which is the converted traversability map, and validated using multiple scenarios.

</details>


### [346] [AirForesight: Current-to-Future Spatial Map Imagination with Cross-Space Planning Consistency for UAV-VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12835)
*Yutong Liu,Xiaojie Li,Mingzhu Xu,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AirForesight，一种"当前到未来"的空间地图想象框架，用于无人机视觉-语言导航（UAV-VLN）。该框架从多视角观测学习结构化当前地图表示，并通过联合当前地图重建与未来轨迹预测进行监督，利用结构化因果注意力将当前空间知识传播到未来地图推理，最终聚合当前与未来表示以预测下一个3D航点。在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN方法大多将视觉-语言输入直接映射到动作，缺乏明确的场景地面（scene grounding）和面向未来的空间推理能力，难以在复杂户外环境中做出可行的3D运动决策。

Method: AirForesight框架包含四个核心组件：（1）从多视角观测学习结构化当前地图表示（current-map representation）；（2）联合监督当前地图重建与未来轨迹预测，使表示同时编码当前场景结构和未来运动意图；（3）通过结构化因果注意力将当前空间知识传播到未来地图推理；（4）聚合当前与未来表示预测下一个3D航点。此外引入跨空间规划一致性损失（cross-space planning consistency loss），促使预测的地图空间轨迹与由真实航点位移导出的专家动作方向保持一致。

Result: 在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S两个基准数据集上取得了强性能，广泛的消融实验验证了框架各模块的有效性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过显式构建当前到未来的空间地图想象机制，AirForesight有效提升了UAV-VLN中的场景理解与空间推理能力，验证了"当前-未来"联合空间推理范式在复杂3D导航任务中的可行性。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to follow language instructions, infer spatial structure from sparse multi-view observations, and execute feasible 3D motion in complex outdoor environments. Despite recent progress with large language models, most existing methods still map vision-language inputs directly to actions, providing limited explicit scene grounding and future-aware spatial reasoning.
